## 1330760
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おー、さすがは伝説の魔王だね。<line-break>風格があるよ。頼もしいーっ！<line-break>　<line-break>闇の王女・ティアナ、<line-break>魔王バロルに仕える神聖なる闇の巫女よ<line-break>彼ならきっと君の願いを救えるよ。<line-break>さあ、そのかわいい唇で告げて？<line-break>君は、何を、望むの？<end>"
EN: "Oh, just what you'd expect from a legendary Demon King. So imposing! So reassuring! Tiana, Princess of Darkness, sacred priestess of darkness who serves Balor, Demon King―surely he can grant your wish. Now, speak it with those pretty lips of yours. What... do you... desire?<end>"
KO: "오오, 역시 전설의 마왕이네. 위엄이 있어. 든든해~! 어둠의 왕녀 티아나, 마왕 발로르를 섬기는 신성한 어둠의 무녀여. 그라면 분명 네 소원을 구해 줄 거야. 자, 그 귀여운 입술로 말해 봐. 너는 무엇을 바라는 거야?<end>"

## 1340006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>君。<line-break>君のすばらしい活躍ぶりは<line-break>アキュリュースの者から聞いているよ。<line-break>そこで相談なのだが、<line-break>今度は私の下で、その力を<line-break>発揮してはくれないかね？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I've heard from the people of Aqyurius about your remarkable exploits. So I have a proposition for you: would you consider putting your talents to use under my command?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 군. 자네의 훌륭한 활약상은 아큐류스 사람들에게 들었네. 그래서 상의할 게 있는데, 이번에는 내 밑에서 그 힘을 발휘해 주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1340007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々とアキュリュースは、<line-break>もはや敵対関係ではない。<line-break>悪い話ではないと思うのだがね。<end>"
EN: "Aqyurius is no longer our enemy. I don't think it's a bad offer.<end>"
KO: "우리와 아큐류스는 이제 적대 관계가 아니다. 나쁜 이야기는 아니라고 생각하는데.<end>"

## 1340008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>君、<line-break>君は実に有能な戦士だ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>一度刃を交えたからこそ、<line-break>その実力をよく理解しているつもりだ。<line-break>今後は私の下で働いてくれないかね？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you are an exceptionally capable warrior. Having crossed blades with you myself, I believe I know your skill well. Would you consider serving under my command from now on?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 군, 자네는 정말 유능한 전사야. 한 번 칼을 맞댄 적이 있으니 그 실력을 잘 안다고 생각하네. 앞으로는 내 밑에서 일해 주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1340009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様の崇高な思想を<line-break>大陸すべてに広めるためには<line-break>君のような勇者が必要なのだよ。<end>"
EN: "If we are to spread Lord Nemea's noble ideals across the entire continent, we need a hero like you.<end>"
KO: "네메아 님의 숭고한 사상을 대륙 전체에 퍼뜨리려면 자네 같은 용사가 필요하네.<end>"

## 1340010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君が<value:$28>君か。<line-break>噂は聞いているよ。<line-break>かなり腕のたつ冒険者だそうだね。<line-break>突然このような形で招いた非礼は<line-break>許してくれたまえ。<line-break>あまり時間がないものでね。<end>"
EN: "So you are <value:$28>. I've heard about you. By all accounts, you're a remarkably skilled adventurer. Forgive me for summoning you so abruptly, but time is short.<end>"
KO: "자네가 <value:$28> 군인가. 소문은 들었네. 상당히 솜씨 좋은 모험자라지. 갑자기 이런 식으로 부른 무례는 용서해 주게. 시간이 별로 없어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1340011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "単刀直入に言おう。<line-break>我々と共に戦ってはくれないかね？<line-break>君のような勇者が必要なのだよ。<end>"
EN: "I will come straight to the point. Will you fight alongside us? We need a hero like you.<end>"
KO: "단도직입적으로 말하지. 우리와 함께 싸워 주지 않겠나? 자네 같은 용사가 필요하네.<end>"

## 1340015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その答えを期待していたよ。<line-break>では、今後君と君の仲間には<line-break>私の直属兵として働いてもらおう。　<line-break>任務は特殊なものを与えるつもりだ。<line-break>そのかわり、普段は冒険者として、<line-break>今まで通りにしてくれてかまわんよ。<end>"
EN: "That is the answer I was hoping for. From now on, you and your companions will serve under my direct command. I intend to entrust you with special assignments. In return, you may carry on as adventurers as you always have when not on duty.<end>"
KO: "그 대답을 기대하고 있었다. 앞으로 너와 동료들은 내 직속병으로 일해 줘야겠다. 임무는 특수한 것을 맡길 생각이다. 대신 평소에는 지금까지처럼 모험자로 지내도 상관없다.<end>"

## 1340016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "이 진영에 오고 싶을 때는 아큐류스 선착장에 있는 우리 군 기사에게 말을 걸게.<end>"

## 1340017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "That is regrettable. Forgive me for summoning you so abruptly. You may go.<end>"
KO: "그건 유감이군. 갑자기 미안했다. 물러가 주게.<end>"

## 1340018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml

KO: "그럼 잘 부탁하네, <value:$28> 군.<end>"

## 1340019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、帰り道かね？<line-break>心配無用だ。我が軍の者に<line-break>アキュリュースまで送らせよう。<line-break>外にいる騎士に声をかけたまえ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, wondering how you will get back? No need to worry. I shall have one of my soldiers escort you to Aqyurius. Speak to the knight outside.<end>"
KO: "아, 돌아가는 길 말인가? 걱정 마라. 우리 군이 아큐류스까지 데려다주지. 밖에 있는 기사에게 말하게.<end>"

## 1340020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、ちょっと待ちたまえ。　<line-break>ひとつ言い忘れていた。<line-break>　<line-break>単独行動をしている間、<line-break>アルノートゥンに対する<line-break>一切の破壊、略奪行動は禁止する。<end>"
EN: "Ah, wait a moment. There was one thing I neglected to mention. While you are acting independently, all acts of destruction or pillage against Alnortun are forbidden.<end>"
KO: "아, 잠깐. 하나 말하는 걸 잊었군. 단독 행동 중 알노툰에 대한 모든 파괴와 약탈 행위는 금지한다.<end>"

## 1340021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は特例的存在なのでね。<line-break>他の者たちの不満を買うわけには<line-break>いかないのだ。よろしく頼んだよ。<end>"
EN: "You are an exceptional case, you see. I cannot afford to provoke resentment among the others. I trust you will comply.<end>"
KO: "자네는 특례적인 존재라서 말이지. 다른 사람들의 불만을 살 수는 없어. 잘 부탁하네.<end>"

## 1340023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml

## 1340025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　西方攻略をその任とし、<line-break>近々アルノートゥン侵攻を計画していた<line-break>　ディンガル軍の白虎将軍ジラークが<line-break>　　　　突然、その姿を消した。<line-break>　　アルノートゥン攻略の目的の<line-break>　ひとつである、聖光石の鉱山へと<line-break>　　　　視察に向かったきり、<line-break>　　　連絡が途絶えたのである。<end>"
EN: "White Tiger General Zsilak of the Dyneskal Army, tasked with conquering the west and planning an imminent invasion of Alnortun, suddenly vanished. He had gone to inspect the Holy Light Stone mine, one of the campaign's objectives, and all contact with him was lost.<end>"
KO: "서방 공략을 맡아 곧 알노툰 침공을 계획하고 있던 딘갈군의 백호장군 지라크가 갑자기 모습을 감췄다. 알노툰 공략 목적 중 하나인 성광석 광산으로 시찰을 나간 뒤 연락이 끊긴 것이다.<end>"

## 1340026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　幾度となく派遣された捜索隊も帰ってこず、<line-break>　　白虎軍の士気は下がる一方であった。<line-break>　　そしてついに、ディンガル本国より<line-break>　　全軍の本国への一時撤退が通達された。<end>"
EN: "The search parties sent out time and again also failed to return, and the White Tiger Army's morale continued to decline. At last, orders came from Dyneskal for the entire army to withdraw home temporarily.<end>"
KO: "몇 번이나 파견된 수색대도 돌아오지 않았고 백호군의 사기는 계속 떨어졌다. 그리고 마침내 딩갈 본국에서 전군의 일시 철수 명령이 내려졌다.<end>"

## 1340027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このジラークの失踪は、大いなる闇の力の復活に<line-break>　深く関与していたのだが、それを知るものは<line-break>　　　当事者以外にはほとんどいなかった。<line-break>　　ただひとり、猫屋敷の賢者を除いては…。　<line-break>　　時代は、人々の望む望まざるに関わらず<line-break>　　　着実に、混沌へと向かっていた…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Zsilak's disappearance was deeply connected to the revival of a great dark power, though almost no one beyond those directly involved knew the truth―save the sage of Cat Manor. Whether people wished it or not, the age was steadily moving toward chaos...<end>"
KO: "이 지라크의 실종은 거대한 어둠의 힘이 부활하는 데 깊이 관련되어 있었지만, 이를 아는 자는 당사자 외에는 거의 없었다. 단 한 사람, 고양이 저택의 현자를 제외하고는…. 시대는 사람들의 바람과는 상관없이 착실히 혼돈을 향해 나아가고 있었다….<end>"

## 1340028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは…<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者ですね…。<line-break>　<line-break>お願いです。<line-break>どうぞお助けください。<line-break>あなたの力が必要なのです。<end>"
EN: "You are... the Bearer of the Infinite Soul, aren't you...? Please, help me. I need your power.<end>"
KO: "당신은… 무한의 소울을 지닌 분이군요…. 부탁드립니다. 제발 도와주세요. 당신의 힘이 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1340029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は…、この場所に<line-break>捕らわれているのです。　　　<line-break>もうずっと長い間…。<line-break>どうぞ、力をお貸しください。<line-break>無限のソウルの力が<line-break>必要なのです。<end>"
EN: "I am... trapped in this place. It has been so very long... Please, lend me your strength. I need the power of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "저는… 이곳에 붙잡혀 있습니다. 아주 오랫동안…. 부디 힘을 빌려 주세요. 무한의 소울의 힘이 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1340030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力を貸す<end>"
EN: "Lend her your strength<end>"
KO: "힘을 빌려준다<end>"

## 1340031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I cannot<end>"
KO: "그건 못 해<end>"

## 1340032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you...<end>"
KO: "고마워….<end>"

## 1340033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、私に手を…。<end>"
EN: "Now, give me your hand...<end>"
KO: "자, 내게 손을….<end>"

## 1340034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かな…。<line-break>うぬに力与えしは、<line-break>何者と思うておる？<end>"
EN: "Fool... Who do you think granted you that power?<end>"
KO: "어리석구나…. 네게 힘을 준 자가 누구라고 생각하느냐?<end>"

## 1340035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　我は不死にして力あるもの…。　<line-break>　　　　我こそは邪竜イシュバアル…。<line-break>　　　　　　破壊神により創られ<line-break>　　　この世に破壊をもたらす者なり…。<end>"
EN: "I am immortal and mighty... I am Ishbaal, the Evil Dragon... Created by the God of Destruction, I bring destruction to this world...<end>"
KO: "나는 불사이며 힘 있는 존재…. 나는 사룡 이슈바알…. 파괴신에게 창조되어 이 세상에 파괴를 가져오는 자….<end>"

## 1340036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　 無限のソウルを持つ者よ…　　<line-break>　　　　　 よくぞ我を復活させた…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　 その礼ぞ…<line-break>　　　　うぬにも破滅をくれてやろう…！<end>"
EN: "Bearer of the Infinite Soul... You have done well to revive me. In thanks... I shall bring ruin upon you as well...!<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 지닌 자여… 잘도 나를 부활시켰구나… 그 답례로… 네놈에게도 파멸을 내려주마…!<end>"

## 1340037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　 バカな…！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　竜である我が<line-break>　　　　人の子ごときに敗れようとは…。<end>"
EN: "Impossible... That I, a dragon, should be defeated by a mere human child...<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…! 용인 내가 인간 따위에게 패하다니….<end>"

## 1340038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　…終わらぬぞ…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　これしきでは…終わらぬぞオオオオ！<line-break>　　　　ゴオオオオオオオオオオオ！！！<end>"
EN: "It is not over... This will not end me... It is not over yet! GRAAAAAAAAAAH!!!<end>"
KO: "…끝나지 않는다…. 이 정도로는… 끝나지 않는다아아아! 고오오오오오오오오오!!!<end>"

## 1340040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そんな！　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…いえ、<line-break>あなたが再び訪れる日を<line-break>待つことにします。<end>"
EN: "...No! ...No. I shall wait for the day you return.<end>"
KO: "…그럴 수가! …아니요, 당신이 다시 찾아오는 날을 기다리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたしか、<line-break>私を救える人はいないのです。<line-break>どうか、あわれんでください。<end>"
EN: "You are the only one who can save me. Please, take pity on me.<end>"
KO: "당신밖에, 저를 구할 수 있는 사람은 없습니다. 부디 자비를 베풀어 주세요.<end>"

## 1340042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お疲れさまです、　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>様！<line-break>　<line-break>ジラーク様の陣中へまいりますか？<end>"
EN: "Good to see you, <value:$28>! Would you like me to take you to Lord Zsilak's camp?<end>"
KO: "수고 많으십니다, <value:$28>님! 지라크 님의 진영으로 가시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1340043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、よろしく頼むよ<end>"
EN: "Yeah, please.<end>"
KO: "응, 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 1340044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん、今日はいいや<end>"
EN: "No, not today.<end>"
KO: "아니, 오늘은 됐어.<end>"

## 1340045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ、では、お送りいたします。<line-break>さあ、こちらへ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Allow me to escort you. This way, please.<end>"
KO: "네, 그럼 모시겠습니다. 자, 이쪽으로.<end>"

## 1340046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break>それでは。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 그럼 이만.<end>"

## 1340047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか、<value:$28>君。<line-break>さっそく仕事だ。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>聖光石の鉱山の視察だ。<line-break>今回は私も同行させてもらうよ。<line-break>是非、自分の目で見ておきたいのでね。<end>"
EN: "There you are, <value:$28>. Let's get straight to work. We're going to inspect the Holy Light Stone mine. I'll be coming along this time. I want to see it for myself.<end>"
KO: "왔나, <value:$28> 군. 바로 일이다. 성광석 광산 시찰이야. 이번에는 나도 동행하지. 꼭 내 눈으로 확인하고 싶어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1340048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、出発だ。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let's be off.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 출발이다.<end>"

## 1340049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の視察の目的は<line-break>鉱山内の地形の把握と<line-break>聖光石の質の確認だ。<line-break>では、行こう。<end>"
EN: "The purpose of this inspection is to familiarize ourselves with the layout of the mine and assess the quality of its Holy Light Stone. Let us proceed.<end>"
KO: "이번 시찰 목적은 광산 내부 지형 파악과 성광석의 품질 확인이다. 그럼 가자.<end>"

## 1340050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたのだね？<line-break>まだ何も見てはいないのだよ？<end>"
EN: "What's the matter? We haven't seen anything yet.<end>"
KO: "왜 그러지? 아직 아무것도 보지 않았는데?<end>"

## 1340051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し気分が悪いので…<end>"
EN: "I'm feeling a little unwell...<end>"
KO: "조금 몸이 안 좋아서….<end>"

## 1340052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、なんでもないです<end>"
EN: "No, it's nothing.<end>"
KO: "아뇨, 아무것도 아닙니다.<end>"

## 1340053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意外とだらしがないのだね。<line-break>仕方ない、日をあらためよう。　　<line-break>　<line-break>君の方の準備が整ったら<line-break>また私の陣まで来てくれたまえ。<line-break>では、さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "You're surprisingly feeble. Very well, we'll try again another day. Once you're ready, come back to my camp. Until then, farewell.<end>"
KO: "의외로 칠칠치 못하군. 어쩔 수 없지, 다음 기회로 하자. 준비가 되면 다시 내 진지로 와라. 그럼 잘 가게.<end>"

## 1340054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、ならば行こう。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then let's go.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 그럼 가자.<end>"

## 1340055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これがすべて聖光石なのか…？<line-break>しかも、とても質が高い…。<line-break>　<line-break>これだけの量があれば<line-break>古代魔道国家並みの魔道軍隊創設も<line-break>夢ではないな…。<end>"
EN: "Is all of this Holy Light Stone...? And such high-quality stone at that. With this much, raising a magical army to rival those of the ancient magical nations would be no mere dream.<end>"
KO: "이게 전부 성광석인가…? 게다가 질도 아주 높아… 이 정도 양이면 고대 마도국가 수준의 마도군 창설도 꿈은 아니겠군…<end>"

## 1340056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この空洞に星のようにまたたく光、<line-break>これがすべて、聖光石なのか…？<line-break>　<line-break>すばらしい。<line-break>なんとすばらしいのだ…。<end>"
EN: "That starlike light shimmering throughout this cavern... Is all of it Holy Light Stone? Magnificent. Truly magnificent...<end>"
KO: "이 공동에 별처럼 반짝이는 빛, 이게 전부 성광석인가…? 훌륭하다. 정말 훌륭하군….<end>"

## 1340057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>君、君には<line-break>このすばらしさがわからないのかね？<line-break>　<line-break>これだけの聖光石があると、<line-break>例えば私がこのように<line-break>魔力を解放するとだね…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, can't you see how extraordinary this is? With this much Holy Light Stone, if I were to release my magic like so...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 군, 자네는 이 훌륭함을 모르겠나? 이만큼 성광석이 있으면 예를 들어 내가 이렇게 마력을 해방하면 말이지….<end>"

## 1340058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このように大きな共振を<line-break>生み出すことができるわけだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>なるほど、古代神聖王国が<line-break>アルノートゥンを重要視したのも<line-break>これでうなずけるな。<end>"
EN: "It produces a resonance of this magnitude. Now I understand why the Ancient Holy Kingdom placed such importance on Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 큰 공진을 만들어 낼 수 있는 거다. 과연 고대 신성왕국이 알노툰을 중요하게 여긴 것도 이해가 가는군.<end>"

## 1340059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギー、<line-break>オレノネムリヲサマタゲタノハ<line-break>オマエタチカ？<end>"
EN: "GEE... WAS IT YOU WHO DISTURBED MY SLUMBER?<end>"
KO: "기이, 내 잠을 방해한 건 너희냐?<end>"

## 1340060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは驚いた。<line-break>言葉を話す怪物がいるとは。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>以前帝都でしゃべるゴブリンなどという<line-break>くだらない噂話がはやったようだが、<line-break>なるほど、確かに奇妙なものだな。<end>"
EN: "Well, this is a surprise. A monster that can speak. There was some absurd rumor about a talking Goblin making the rounds in the imperial capital, as I recall. It certainly is a curious thing.<end>"
KO: "이건 놀랍군. 말을 하는 괴물이 있다니. 예전에 제도에서 말하는 고블린이 있다는 시답잖은 소문이 유행했다지만, 과연 확실히 기묘하군.<end>"

## 1340061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オレヲバカニシタノカ？<line-break>ギー、オマエラコロシテヤル！！<end>"
EN: "ARE YOU MOCKING ME? GEE! I'LL KILL YOU ALL!!<end>"
KO: "날 바보 취급한 거냐? 기이, 너희들 죽여 버리겠다!!<end>"

## 1340062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギ、ギ、ギ…<line-break>アリガトウヨ…<line-break>コレデオレハカイホウサレル…。　<line-break>ハルカムカシ<line-break>いおんずニフウジラレタ<line-break>コノ、イマワシキカラダカラナ…！<end>"
EN: "GEE, GEE, GEE... THANK YOU... NOW I CAN BE FREE... FREE OF THIS ACCURSED BODY THAT IONS SEALED ME IN LONG, LONG AGO...!<end>"
KO: "기, 기, 기… 고맙다… 이제 나는 해방된다… 머나먼 옛날 이온즈에게 봉인된 이 저주받은 몸에서…!<end>"

## 1340063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　ブフゥ～。<line-break>　　礼を言うぞ。小さく弱き虫ケラどもよ。　　<line-break>　　　　余は破壊神により生み出されし<line-break>　　　　　　邪竜イシュバアルなり。<line-break>　　　　封印などという辱めに耐え忍び<line-break>　　　歴史の影のそのまた闇に潜み続けた<line-break>　　この恨みと怒り、今こそ晴らしてやるぞ。<line-break>　　　　　　さあ、まずはうぬらだ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... You have my thanks, you tiny, feeble insects. I am Evil Dragon Ishbaal, created by the God of Destruction. I have endured the humiliation of being sealed away, lurking in darkness beyond even the shadows of history. Now, at last, I shall have my revenge for all these years of hatred and rage. And you shall be the first.<end>"
KO: "부후우~. 감사하마, 작고 나약한 벌레들아. 나는 파괴신이 만들어 낸 사룡 이슈바알이다. 봉인이라는 치욕을 견디며 역사의 그림자보다 더 깊은 어둠 속에 숨어 지낸 원한과 분노를 이제야 풀어 보겠노라. 자, 먼저 너희부터다.<end>"

## 1340064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　グアア……<line-break>　　　まさか、余が力を退けるとは……。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　しかし、このままでは朽ちぬ。<line-break>　　そこの老いぼれ、うぬの体をいただくぞ。　<line-break>　　　　うぬと、その角が持つ魔の力、<line-break>　　　　　余の糧とさせてもらう！<end>"
EN: "Graah... To think my power could be repelled... But I shall not perish like this. You there, old fool―I shall take your body. You and the dark power possessed by that horn shall become my sustenance!<end>"
KO: "구아아…… 설마, 짐의 힘을 물리칠 줄이야……. 하지만 이대로 썩어 없어지진 않는다. 거기 늙은이, 네 몸을 받겠다. 너와 그 뿔이 가진 마의 힘을 짐의 양식으로 삼아 주마!<end>"

## 1340065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウグ…アァアア！！<line-break>これは…邪竜の精神か？<line-break>私の体の…自由がきかぬ。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Aaaagh!! Is this... the Evil Dragon's mind? My body... won't obey me.<end>"
KO: "우그… 아아아!! 이건… 사룡의 정신인가? 내 몸이… 말을 듣지 않아.<end>"

## 1340066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…ハハハ。<line-break>余はうぬの野望を依り代としている。<line-break>余を退けることは無理だ。<line-break>うぬが内に秘めた種族再興の野望。<line-break>人間族を退け、コーンス族の世界を<line-break>作りたいという秘めた願い…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Ha ha ha. Your ambition is my vessel. You cannot cast me out. That ambition you harbor to restore your race―to drive out the human race and build a world for the Konsu people...<end>"
KO: "후후후… 하하하. 짐은 네 야망을 그릇으로 삼고 있다. 짐을 물리치는 건 불가능하다. 네 안에 감춘 종족 재흥의 야망, 인간족을 밀어내고 콘스족의 세계를 만들고 싶다는 숨은 소원….<end>"

## 1340067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余が、半分だけかなえてやろう。<line-break>人間族は根絶やしにしてくれる。<line-break>　<line-break>ただし、<line-break>コーンス族も同じように<line-break>根絶やしだがな。フハハハハ！！<end>"
EN: "I shall grant half your wish. I will exterminate humanity. Of course, the Konsu people will be exterminated along with them. Hah hah hah hah!!<end>"
KO: "짐이 절반만 이루어주마. 인간족은 뿌리째 없애주지. 다만 콘스족도 똑같이 멸망시키겠지만 말이다. 후하하하하!!<end>"

## 1340068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>君…。<line-break>私を…殺してくれたまえ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Please... kill me.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>군…. 나를… 죽여 주게.<end>"

## 1340069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このままでは、伝説にある<line-break>邪竜と同化したドワーフと<line-break>同じ道をたどってしまう。<line-break>頼む、私のため、<line-break>そして、世界のため<line-break>さあ、やってくれ！！<end>"
EN: "If this continues, I will share the fate of the Dwarf in the legend who became one with an Evil Dragon. Please―for my sake, and for the sake of the world―do it now!!<end>"
KO: "이대로라면 전설 속 사룡과 동화한 드워프와 같은 길을 걷게 된다. 부탁한다, 나를 위해, 그리고 세계를 위해. 자, 해 줘!!<end>"

## 1340070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウグアアアアア…！<end>"
EN: "Ughaaaaagh...!<end>"
KO: "우그아아아아…!<end>"

## 1340071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>君…。<line-break>私は、自分の中に秘めていた　　　<line-break>野望に負けたのだな…。　<line-break>はじめはネメア様のお力で、せめて<line-break>コーンス族のこの苦しい現状が<line-break>変わることを願っていたのだが…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>... I was defeated by the ambition hidden within me. At first, I only hoped that Lord Nemea's power might ease the suffering of the Konsu people...<end>"
KO: "고맙네, <value:$28> 군…. 나는 내 안에 숨겨 둔 야망에 져 버린 모양이군…. 처음엔 네메아 님의 힘으로 적어도 콘스족의 이 괴로운 현실이 바뀌길 바랐는데….<end>"

## 1340072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつからか、私の中の欲望は　　　<line-break>度を越した醜いものに<line-break>なっていたようだ…。<line-break><value:$28>君、君がもし<line-break>世界の未来を変える力を持つなら、<line-break>どうか、すべての種族が…。<end>"
EN: "But somewhere along the way, my desire seems to have grown monstrous beyond all measure... <value:$28>, if you have the power to change the future of this world, then please... let all its peoples...<end>"
KO: "언제부터인가 내 안의 욕망은 도를 넘은 추악한 것이 되어 있었던 모양이군…. <value:$28> 군, 자네가 만약 세계의 미래를 바꿀 힘을 가졌다면 부디 모든 종족이….<end>"

## 1340073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　最後に、もう一度礼を言ってやろう。　　<line-break>　　　　よくぞ余の封印を解いてくれた。<line-break>　　　　　　　では、さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "One last time, I shall offer you my thanks. You have done well to break my seal. And now, farewell.<end>"
KO: "마지막으로 다시 한번 감사하지. 잘도 나의 봉인을 풀어 주었다. 그럼, 잘 있어라.<end>"

## 1340074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、まだだよ。貴様を<line-break>世に放つわけにはいかんのでね。<line-break>　<line-break>この命を燃やし尽くしてでも<line-break>私が貴様を封じてみせる。<end>"
EN: "No, not yet. I cannot allow you to go free in this world. Even if it consumes the last of my life, I will seal you away.<end>"
KO: "아니, 아직이다. 네놈을 세상에 풀어놓을 순 없어서 말이지. 이 목숨을 다 태워서라도 내가 네놈을 봉인하겠다.<end>"

## 1340075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　ク………ッ。<line-break>　　　　　　　　こしゃくな。<line-break>　　　　　余の動きを封じるつもりか。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　しかし、いつまで続く？<line-break>　　その術でうぬの命が削られていくのが　　<line-break>　　　余には手に取るようにわかるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Ghh... Insolent wretch. You mean to immobilize me? But how long can you keep this up? I can see as plainly as if it were before me that the spell is draining your life away.<end>"
KO: "큭…… 건방진. 짐의 움직임을 봉하려는 것이냐. 하지만 얼마나 버틸 수 있겠느냐? 그 술법이 네 목숨을 깎아먹고 있다는 게 짐에게는 손에 잡힐 듯 보인다.<end>"

## 1340076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>君、<line-break>逃げたまえ。<line-break>今の我々では勝ち目はない。　<line-break>そして、ネメア様に出会えたら<line-break>このことを告げるのだ。<line-break>ネメア様なら、きっと…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, run. As we are now, we cannot defeat it. If you meet Lord Nemea, tell him what happened here. He will surely...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 군, 도망가게. 지금 우리로선 승산이 없다. 그리고 네메아 님을 만나면 이 사실을 전해라. 네메아 님이라면 분명….<end>"

## 1340077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、行くんだ。<line-break>もう言葉を紡ぐのもつらいのでね…。<end>"
EN: "Go. It is already becoming difficult to speak...<end>"
KO: "자, 가라. 이제 말을 잇는 것조차 힘들어서 말이야….<end>"

## 1340078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を…して…いるんだ…ね…。<line-break>私にも余裕はないのだよ。<line-break>急いで去りたまえ。<end>"
EN: "What... are you... doing...? I'm hard-pressed myself. Leave at once.<end>"
KO: "무엇을… 하고… 있는 건가…. 나도 여유가 없네. 어서 떠나게.<end>"

## 1340079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたのだね？<line-break>まだ調べるべきことは<line-break>残っているはずだが？<end>"
EN: "What's the matter? Surely there are still things we ought to investigate.<end>"
KO: "왜 그러지? 아직 조사할 것이 남아 있을 텐데?<end>"

## 1340080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日はこのへんで…<end>"
EN: "Let's call it a day...<end>"
KO: "오늘은 이쯤에서….<end>"

## 1340081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、そうですね<end>"
EN: "Oh, right.<end>"
KO: "아, 그렇네요<end>"

## 1340082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意外とだらしがないのだね。　　<line-break>仕方ない、日をあらためよう。<line-break>　<line-break>君の方の準備が整ったら<line-break>また私の陣まで来てくれたまえ。<line-break>では、さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "You're surprisingly feeble. Very well, we'll try again another day. Once you're ready, come back to my camp. Until then, farewell.<end>"
KO: "의외로 칠칠치 못하군. 어쩔 수 없지, 다음 기회로 하자. 준비가 되면 다시 내 진지로 와라. 그럼 잘 가게.<end>"

## 1340083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、行こう。<end>"
EN: "Good. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 가자.<end>"

## 1340084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか、<value:$28>君。<line-break>準備はいいのだね？<end>"
EN: "There you are, <value:$28>. You're ready, I take it?<end>"
KO: "왔군, <value:$28>. 준비는 됐겠지?<end>"

## 1340086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　うぬは…あの時の虫ケラか。　　　　<line-break>　　　　うぬとともにいた老いぼれは<line-break>　　　　　　ついさっき力尽きた。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　せっかくなので食ろうてやったわ。　　　<line-break>　　　　老いぼれとはいえ、あの角は<line-break>　　　なかなかに食いごたえがあったな。<line-break>　　　　　さあ、次はうぬの番だ！<end>"
EN: "You... You're that worm from before, aren't you? The old fool who was with you succumbed just moments ago. So I took the opportunity to feast. Old though that fool was, that horn made for quite a satisfying meal. Now, it is your turn!<end>"
KO: "네놈은… 그때 그 벌레로군. 네놈과 함께 있던 늙은이는 방금 힘이 다했다. 모처럼이니 내가 먹어 치워 줬지. 늙은이라 해도 그 뿔은 제법 씹는 맛이 있더군. 자, 다음은 네 차례다!<end>"

## 1340087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　余は破壊神により生み出されし者　　　<line-break>　　　　　　邪竜イシュバアルなり。<line-break>　　　　　降り積もる歳月を耐え忍び<line-break>　　　今ここに再び翼を広げる時を迎えた。<line-break>　　　　　封印などという辱めに耐え<line-break>　　　歴史の影のそのまた闇に潜み続けた<line-break>　　この恨みと怒り、今こそ晴らしてやるぞ。<line-break>　　　　　　さあ、まずはうぬだ。<end>"
EN: "I was created by the God of Destruction. I am Evil Dragon Ishbaal. I have endured the long passage of years, and now the time has come for me to spread my wings once more. I bore the humiliation of being sealed away and lurked in darkness beyond even the shadows of history. Now I shall unleash all my hatred and fury. And you shall be the first.<end>"
KO: "나는 파괴신이 만들어 낸 자, 사룡 이슈바알이다. 쌓이고 쌓인 세월을 견디고 이제 다시 날개를 펼칠 때가 왔다. 봉인이라는 치욕을 견디며 역사의 그림자보다 더 깊은 어둠에 숨어 지낸 원한과 분노를 이제 풀어 주마. 자, 먼저 네놈부터다.<end>"

## 1340088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　この余の力を…退けるか…。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　いまわしきは人の子よ…。<line-break>封印の果てに命まで奪われることになるとは…。<line-break>　　　　　ウグァアアアアアア！！<end>"
EN: "You would... repel even my power...? Accursed child of man... To think my long imprisonment would end with my very life being taken... GRAAAAAAAAAAH!!<end>"
KO: "이 나의 힘을… 물리치는가…. 가증스러운 인간의 자식이여…. 봉인의 끝에서 목숨마저 빼앗기게 될 줄이야…. 우그아아아아아!!<end>"

## 1340089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、帰り道かね？<line-break>心配無用だ。我が軍の者に<line-break>アキュリュースまで送らせよう。<line-break>外にいる騎士に声をかけたまえ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, wondering how you will get back? No need to worry. I shall have one of my soldiers escort you to Aqyurius. Speak to the knight outside.<end>"
KO: "아, 돌아가는 길 말인가? 걱정 마라. 우리 군이 아큐류스까지 데려다주지. 밖에 있는 기사에게 말하게.<end>"

## 1340090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キュイ～！<end>"
EN: "Kweee!<end>"
KO: "뀨이~!<end>"

## 1340091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ピュイ～！<end>"
EN: "Pweee!<end>"
KO: "퓨이~!<end>"

## 1340093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、そちらの方は………？<line-break>失礼しました！<line-break>お仕事の最中であられましたか。　　　<line-break>我々としましても、そちらの方を陣中へ<line-break>お連れするわけにはまいりません。<line-break>今日は、これで失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Oh? And this is...? Forgive me! I didn't realize you were occupied. I'm afraid we cannot escort anyone else into the camp with you. I'll take my leave for today.<end>"
KO: "어라, 그쪽 분은………? 실례했습니다! 업무 중이셨군요. 저희로서도 그분을 진중으로 모셔갈 수는 없습니다. 오늘은 이만 물러가겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340094

KO: "그래, 지라크 예비 메시지다. V1~8, 그 6이야.<end>"

## 1340099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やぁ。<value:$28>…<line-break>言いにくいんだけど…<line-break>僕、パーティーを離れるよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>... This is hard to say, but I'm leaving the party.<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>…. 말하기 좀 그렇지만… 나, 파티에서 빠질게.<end>"

## 1340100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待てよ！　おい。<line-break>どういうことだよ、それ！<end>"
EN: "Wait! Hey, what the hell is this about?<end>"
KO: "잠깐! 야. 그게 무슨 뜻이야!<end>"

## 1340101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめん。でも、これが<line-break>最良の方法だって思ったんだ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. But I thought this was the best way.<end>"
KO: "…미안. 하지만 이게 최선의 방법이라고 생각했어.<end>"

## 1340102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジ…<end>"
EN: "Nadge...<end>"
KO: "나지…<end>"

## 1340103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークが、アルノートゥンに<line-break>コーンスを集めてるってな。<end>"
EN: "Word is, Zsilak's gathering the Konsu in Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "지라크가 알노툰에 콘스를 모으고 있다더군.<end>"

## 1340104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは歴史から抹消されてることなんだが<line-break>昔、あそこじゃ<line-break>聖光石の巨大な結晶が産出されたそうだ<end>"
EN: "This has been erased from history, but apparently that place once yielded an enormous crystal of Holy Light Stone.<end>"
KO: "역사에서 지워진 일이지만 옛날 그곳에서는 거대한 성광석 결정이 산출됐다고 한다.<end>"

## 1340105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんでも、聖光石は<line-break>持ち主の魔力を高めるらしい。<line-break>コーンスの魔力は俺たちより上だ。<line-break>聖光石さえあれば、<line-break>頭数の少ないコーンスにでも<line-break>天下が取れるっていう寸法だろうな<end>"
EN: "Apparently, Holy Light Stone heightens its owner's magical power. The magic of the Konsu is already stronger than ours. With Holy Light Stone, even the Konsu, few as they are, could rule the world. That's the idea, I suppose.<end>"
KO: "듣자 하니 성광석은 주인의 마력을 높여 준다는군. 콘스의 마력은 우리보다 뛰어나. 성광석만 있으면 수가 적은 콘스라도 천하를 차지할 수 있다는 계산이겠지.<end>"

## 1340106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテスさん。これが<line-break>最良の方法だって、僕、思うんです。<line-break>…すいません。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... I really do think this is the best way. I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…제네테스 씨. 이게 최선의 방법이라고 전 생각해요. …죄송합니다.<end>"

## 1340107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やさしい奴だな、ナッジは。<end>"
EN: "You've got a kind heart, Nadge.<end>"
KO: "착한 녀석이군, 나지는.<end>"

## 1340108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークのばくちが当たったら、<line-break>コーンスの世界が来る。<line-break>そのとき、お前らを助けられる人間が<line-break>ジラークの隣にいなくちゃいけないって<line-break>思ったんだろう。<end>"
EN: "If Zsilak's gamble pays off, the world will belong to the Konsu. You figured that when it did, someone who could help all of you would need to be at Zsilak's side.<end>"
KO: "지라크의 도박이 맞아떨어지면 콘스의 세상이 온다. 그때 너희를 도울 수 있는 인간이 지라크 곁에 있어야 한다고 생각한 거겠지.<end>"

## 1340109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、ヴァンのダジャレにつっこめる<line-break>貴重な人材を手放すのには反対だな。<line-break>誰かに支配されるのも気に入らない。<line-break>ヴァンちゃんを連れて、<line-break>ジラークの所になぐり込みかけた方が<line-break>いいんじゃないか？<end>"
EN: "Still, I object to losing the one man around here who can call Van on his puns. And I don't much like the idea of anyone ruling over us. Why don't we take Van along and storm Zsilak's place instead?<end>"
KO: "하지만 반의 말장난에 받아칠 귀중한 인재를 놓치는 건 반대야. 누군가에게 지배당하는 것도 마음에 안 들고. 반을 데리고 지라크한테 쳐들어가는 게 낫지 않아?<end>"

## 1340110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうする？<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "What will you do, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 할래? <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1340111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕も悩んだ結果、<line-break>これが最良だって決めたんだ。<line-break>ごめん。…じゃ。<end>"
EN: "I've wrestled with it too, and decided this is for the best. I'm sorry. Well... goodbye.<end>"
KO: "나도 고민한 끝에 이게 최선이라고 결정했어. 미안해. …그럼.<end>"

## 1340113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなところで君に会うなんて…。<end>"
EN: "I never thought I'd run into you here...<end>"
KO: "이런 곳에서 널 만나다니….<end>"

## 1340115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうしてなんだ、ナッジ！<line-break>俺たちは友達だろ！<end>"
EN: "W-why, Nadge? We're friends, aren't we?<end>"
KO: "왜, 왜 그러는 거야 나지! 우린 친구잖아!<end>"

## 1340116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういう問題じゃないんだよ。<line-break>ヴァン…。わかんないかな…。<end>"
EN: "That's not what this is about, Van. Can't you understand?<end>"
KO: "그런 문제가 아니야, 반…. 모르겠어…?<end>"

## 1340117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういう問題じゃないだと！<line-break>どういうことなんだよ！<line-break><line-break>俺たちがお前をコーンスだからって<line-break>差別したか？　俺たちがお前を<line-break>人間と違う扱いをしたか？<end>"
EN: "Not what this is about? Then what is it about? Did we ever discriminate against you for being a Konsu? Did we ever treat you differently from any human?<end>"
KO: "그런 문제가 아니라니! 무슨 뜻이야! 우리가 네가 콘스라고 차별했어? 우리가 너를 인간과 다르게 대했어?<end>"

## 1340118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ううん…。<end>"
EN: "Heh. No...<end>"
KO: "후후, 아니….<end>"

## 1340119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、何で！<end>"
EN: "Then why?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 왜!<end>"

## 1340120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、君たちがここにいる理由と<line-break>同じだよ。<line-break><line-break>ねぇ、ヴァン、僕が君に、<line-break>友達だから特別に<line-break>コーンスと同じ扱いをしてあげる。<end>"
EN: "For the same reason you're here. Van, suppose I said that, because we're friends, I'd make a special exception and treat you like one of the Konsu.<end>"
KO: "그건 너희가 여기 있는 이유와 같아. 있지, 반. 친구니까 특별히 널 콘스와 똑같이 대해 줄게.<end>"

## 1340121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、おとなしく、ジラーク将軍が<line-break>コーンスの世界を作るのを見てて…<line-break>と言ったら、素直に手を引いてくれる？<end>"
EN: "Suppose I told you to stand quietly by and watch General Zsilak create a world for the Konsu. Would you simply back down?<end>"

## 1340122

## 1340123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってないのはお前の方だろ？<line-break>ナッジ！<line-break><line-break>ナッジ！　おい、答えろよ！<line-break>お前は孤児でコーンスで、<line-break>俺は宿屋の跡取りで人間なのかよ？<end>"
EN: "You're the one who doesn't understand, Nadge! Nadge! Hey, answer me! Are you an orphan and a Konsu while I'm a human and heir to an inn? Is that all we are?<end>"
KO: "모르는 건 네 쪽이잖아? 나지! 나지! 야, 대답해! 넌 고아인 콘스고, 나는 여관 후계자인 인간이라는 거냐?<end>"

## 1340130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちがうだろ？<line-break>俺たちゃ、んなもんじゃない。<line-break>ダチだろ？<end>"
EN: "No, we're not! That's not who we are. We're pals, aren't we?<end>"
KO: "아니잖아? 우린 그런 사이가 아니야. 친구잖아?<end>"

## 1340131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン…。<end>"

KO: "반….<end>"

## 1340132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うまく言えねえよ！　ナッジ！！<line-break>どうして俺と同じこと言わねえんだよ！<line-break>俺の言うことに逆らうんだよ！<line-break>なあ、答えろよ！<line-break>俺とお前が友達だってだけじゃ<line-break>ダメなのかよ！<end>"
EN: "I don't know how to say it, Nadge! Why can't you say the same thing I am? Why do you have to go against me? Come on, answer me! Isn't it enough that you and I are friends?<end>"
KO: "말로 잘 표현 못 하겠다고, 나지!! 왜 나랑 같은 말을 안 하는 거야! 왜 내 말에 거스르는 거야! 대답해! 나랑 네가 친구라는 것만으로는 안 되는 거야!<end>"

## 1340133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그래도 넌 이 세상에 있을 곳이 없다고 말하는 거냐!<end>"

## 1340134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジ、答えろよ！<line-break>カベ作んなよ！　なあ！<line-break>向こう行かないでくれよ！<line-break>なあ、俺の常人には理解不可能な<line-break>高等なジョークにつっこんでくれよ。<line-break>なあ！　なあ！<end>"
EN: "Nadge, answer me! Don't shut me out! Come on! Don't go away! Come on, give my sophisticated jokes―incomprehensible to ordinary minds―the comebacks they deserve! Come on! Come on!<end>"
KO: "나지, 대답해! 벽 만들지 마! 야! 저쪽으로 가지 마! 내 평범한 사람은 이해 못 하는 고급 농담에 태클 걸어 줘! 야! 야!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340147

## 1340148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…。なぜだ…。<line-break>どうしてなんだよ…。　　　　<line-break>悪いのは…俺たちなのか…？<line-break>う、薄汚れてんのは、<line-break>お前たちのほうじゃねえのか？<end>"

KO: "으으… 왜지… 어째서야… 나쁜 건… 우리인 건가…? 더, 더러운 건 너희 쪽 아니냐고?<end>"

## 1340156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺やナッジみたいなのが…<line-break>笑いながら暮らせない世界なんて…<line-break>こっちから、出てってやるぜ…。<line-break>ナッジ…、俺たちゃ、<line-break>ダチ…だかんな…。<line-break>いつでも一緒だ…ぜ…。<end>"
EN: "If guys like Nadge and me can't live and laugh in this world... then we'll leave it of our own accord... Nadge... we're pals... you hear? We'll always be together...<end>"
KO: "나나 나지 같은 녀석들이… 웃으며 살 수 없는 세상이라면… 이쪽에서 떠나 주겠어…. 나지… 우리는 친구…니까…. 언제나 함께다….<end>"

## 1340157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラが攻めてきたって<line-break>聞いていたのに。<line-break>…カルラの手下か？<end>"
EN: "I heard Karula was attacking. Are you one of her lackeys?<end>"
KO: "칼라가 공격해 왔다고 들었는데… 칼라의 부하인가?<end>"

## 1340158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界は人間のものじゃなかった。<line-break>僕らにもこの世界を相続する<line-break>権利があった。<line-break>それが人間に奪われてしまった。<line-break>僕らコーンスは<line-break>それを奪い返すだけだよ。<end>"
EN: "This world never belonged to humanity. We had the right to inherit it too, but the humans stole that right from us. We Konsu are merely taking it back.<end>"
KO: "이 세계는 인간의 것이 아니었어. 우리에게도 이 세계를 물려받을 권리가 있었지. 그런데 인간에게 빼앗겼어. 우리 콘스는 그걸 되찾을 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1340159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪魔するものは排除する。<line-break>それが君たち人間のやり方だよね？<line-break>僕もそれに従わせてもらうよ。<end>"
EN: "You eliminate anyone who stands in your way. That's how you humans do things, isn't it? I'll follow your example.<end>"
KO: "방해되는 건 제거한다. 그게 너희 인간의 방식이지? 나도 그대로 따르도록 할게.<end>"

## 1340160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク将軍…！<line-break>同胞の世界を…必ず…！<end>"
EN: "General Zsilak... A world for our people... without fail...!<end>"
KO: "지라크 장군…! 동포들의 세계를… 반드시…!<end>"

## 1340163

## 1340164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見るがいい、この豊かな鉱脈を。<line-break>これだけの聖光石の魔道力があれば…<line-break> <line-break>我ら数で劣るコーンス族も、<line-break>世界の覇権を手にすることができよう。<end>"
EN: "Behold this rich vein. With the magical power of so much Holy Light Stone at our command, even we Konsu, few though we are, could claim dominion over the world.<end>"
KO: "보아라, 이 풍부한 광맥을. 이만큼의 성광석 마도력이 있다면… 수가 적은 우리 콘스족도 세계의 패권을 손에 넣을 수 있다.<end>"

## 1340166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスが種族の誇りを持って<line-break>生き残る道、ネメア様亡き今、<line-break>他に無し！<end>"
EN: "With Lord Nemea gone, this is the only way for the Konsu to survive with our pride as a people intact!<end>"
KO: "콘스가 종족의 긍지를 지키며살아남을 길은, 네메아 님이 돌아가신 지금, 이것밖에 없다!<end>"

## 1340167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを邪魔するというのならば、<line-break>ただ排除するだけである！<end>"
EN: "If you mean to stand in our way, then you will simply be eliminated!<end>"
KO: "그것을 방해하겠다면 그저 제거할 뿐이다!<end>"

## 1340168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐ…。<line-break>ここで、コーンス族の将来は<line-break>ついえるのか…。<end>"
EN: "Guh... Is this where the future of the Konsu comes to an end...?<end>"
KO: "윽…. 여기서 콘스족의 미래가 끊기는 건가….<end>"

## 1340169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よい。<line-break>もはやよいのだ、ナッジ君。<line-break> <line-break>私が間違っていた。<line-break>種族平等は力で達成できるものでは<line-break>なかったのだ。<end>"
EN: "Enough. That's enough now, Nadge. I was wrong. Equality among the races could not be achieved by force.<end>"
KO: "…됐다. 이제 됐다, 나지 군. 내가 틀렸어. 종족 평등은 힘으로 이룰 수 있는 게 아니었던 거야.<end>"

## 1340172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを、焦って…。<line-break>愚かなことだ…。<line-break>…ゴホッ、…ゴホッ！<end>"
EN: "I was too impatient... What a fool I was... Cough... cough!<end>"
KO: "그걸 조급해해서…. 어리석은 짓이다…. …콜록, …콜록!<end>"

## 1340173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク将軍！<end>"
EN: "General Zsilak!<end>"
KO: "지라크 장군!<end>"

## 1340174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これから、コーンスには<line-break>さらに厳しい運命が<line-break>待ち受けているだろう。<line-break>ナッジ君、私は君に託そう。<line-break>コーンスの未来を…。<end>"
EN: "The Konsu will face an even harsher fate from this day forward. Nadge, I entrust it to you... the future of our people.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 콘스에는 더욱 가혹한 운명이 기다리고 있겠지. 나지 군, 나는 네게 맡기겠다. 콘스의 미래를….<end>"

## 1340175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまんな…勝手に重荷を背負わせて。<line-break>だが、君ならば我が種族の未来を…。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me... for placing this burden on you without asking. But in your hands, our people's future...<end>"
KO: "미안하군… 멋대로 무거운 짐을 떠넘겨서. 하지만 자네라면 우리 종족의 미래를….<end>"

## 1340176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍！<line-break>ジラーク将軍！<end>"
EN: "General! General Zsilak!<end>"
KO: "장군님! 지라크 장군님!<end>"

## 1340177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は将軍を尊敬していました…。<line-break>僕は絶対に同胞種族を守ります…。<line-break>将軍…。<end>"
EN: "I always admired you, General... I swear I'll protect our people, no matter what... General...<end>"
KO: "저는 장군을 존경했습니다…. 저는 반드시 동족을 지키겠습니다…. 장군….<end>"

## 1340178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　うぬは…　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者か…？<line-break><line-break>　　　　　我、運よく目覚めしも、<line-break>　　　　悠久の眠りによりて力は衰え<line-break>　　　意のままに動くことすらかなわぬ…。<end>"
EN: "You... Are you the Bearer of the Infinite Soul...? I was fortunate to awaken, but my long slumber has weakened me. I cannot even move as I wish...<end>"
KO: "그대는… 무한의 소울을 가진 자인가…? 내가 운 좋게 깨어났지만 영겁의 잠으로 힘이 쇠해 뜻대로 움직이는 것조차 어렵다….<end>"

## 1340179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "    感謝するぞ…我が餌食となりに来たを…。<line-break>             目覚めの力を蓄えるに<line-break><line-break>         無限のソウルの持ち主の血肉に<line-break>             まさるものはないわ…。<end>"
EN: "I thank you... for coming to be my prey. Nothing can restore my strength like the flesh and blood of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "감사하마… 내 먹이가 되러 온 것을…. 깨어날 힘을 모으는 데 무한의 소울을 가진 자의 피와 살보다 좋은 것은 없지….<end>"

## 1340180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　我はイシュバアル…すなわち破滅…！<line-break>　　　　　　破壊神により生まれ…<line-break>　　　　この世界に死をもたらす者なり…！<end>"
EN: "I am Ishbaal... Destruction itself! Born of the God of Destruction, I bring death to this world...!<end>"
KO: "나는 이슈바알… 곧 파멸…! 파괴신에게서 태어나… 이 세계에 죽음을 가져오는 자다…!<end>"

## 1340181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　バカな…！<line-break>　　　　　　　竜である我が<line-break>　　　人の子ごときに敗れようとは…。<line-break>　　　　　　…終わらぬぞ…。<line-break>　　　　これしきでは…終わらぬぞオオオオ！<line-break>　　　　　ゴオオオオオオオオオオオ！！！<end>"
EN: "Impossible... That I, a dragon, should be defeated by a mere human child... It is not over... This will not end me... It is not over yet! GRAAAAAAAAAAH!!!<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…! 용인 내가 인간 따위에게 패하다니…. 아직 끝나지 않았다…. 이 정도로는 끝나지 않아아아아! 고오오오오오오오오!!!<end>"

## 1340183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何者だ！<line-break>ディンガル帝国の者かっ！<end>"
EN: "W-who are you? Are you with the Dyneskal Empire?!<end>"
KO: "누, 누구냐! 딩갈 제국의 자냐!<end>"

## 1340184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配は無用だ。<end>"
EN: "There is no cause for alarm.<end>"
KO: "걱정할 것 없다.<end>"

## 1340185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シェムハザ長老！<end>"
EN: "Elder Shemhaza!<end>"
KO: "셈하자 장로님!<end>"

## 1340186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この方のことならば、<line-break>知っている。<end>"
EN: "I know this visitor.<end>"
KO: "이분이라면 알고 있다.<end>"

## 1340188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の家においでになるとよい。<end>"
EN: "Come to my home.<end>"
KO: "내 집으로 오면 된다.<end>"

## 1340189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この方のことならば<line-break>少し聞いている。<line-break>信頼に足る方だ。<end>"
EN: "I've heard a little about this person. This is someone we can trust.<end>"
KO: "이분 이야기는 조금 들었습니다. 신뢰할 만한 분입니다.<end>"

## 1340191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はこのウルカーンの長老シェムハザ。<line-break>私の家においでになるとよい。<line-break>頼みたいこともあるのでな。<end>"
EN: "I am Shemhaza, elder of Ulkarne. Come to my home. There is something I would ask of you.<end>"
KO: "나는 이 울칸의 장로 셈하자다. 내 집으로 오게. 부탁하고 싶은 일도 있으니 말이야.<end>"

## 1340192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこでこの地にあることを知ったか<line-break>闇の神器『束縛の腕輪』を渡せと<line-break>言ってきたのだ。<line-break>束縛の腕輪は、強大な力を持つ<line-break>闇の神器だ。利己的な欲望のために<line-break>使ってはならないのだよ。<end>"
EN: "I do not know where he learned it was here, but he demanded that we hand over the Dark Artifact known as the Bracelet of Binding. The Bracelet of Binding is immensely powerful. It must never be used to satisfy selfish desires.<end>"
KO: "어디서 이곳에 있다는 걸 알았는지 어둠의 신기 '속박의 팔찌'를 내놓으라고 했어. 속박의 팔찌는 강대한 힘을 지닌 어둠의 신기야. 이기적인 욕망을 위해 써선 안 돼.<end>"

## 1340193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神器をあの野心的な簒奪者に渡せば<line-break>いったいどんな災厄を世界に<line-break>もたらすことになるかわからぬ。<end>"
EN: "There is no telling what calamity that ambitious usurper would bring upon the world if we gave him the artifact.<end>"
KO: "신기를 저 야심 많은 찬탈자에게 넘기면 대체 어떤 재앙을 세계에 가져올지 알 수 없다.<end>"

## 1340194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、ネメアとディンガル軍を<line-break>迎え撃つ準備をしていたのだよ。<end>"
EN: "So we have been preparing to meet Nemea and the Dyneskal Army in battle.<end>"
KO: "그래서 네메아와 딘갈군을 맞아 싸울 준비를 하고 있었던 거야.<end>"

## 1340195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配は無用だ。<line-break>古来、ウルカーンは<line-break>防衛戦で負けたことがない。<line-break>火の巫女ウルが遺したマグマゴーレムが<line-break>どのような侵略者も、退けてきたのだ。<line-break>今、魔道士たちが起動している。<end>"
EN: "There is no cause for alarm. Ulkarne has never lost a defensive battle, not once in all its history. The Magma Golems left by Uru, the Priestess of Fire, have repelled every invader to come against us. The mages are activating them now.<end>"
KO: "걱정할 것 없다. 예로부터 울칸은 방어전에서 진 적이 없어. 불의 무녀 울이 남긴 마그마 골렘이 어떤 침략자도 물리쳐 왔지. 지금 마도사들이 기동시키고 있다.<end>"

## 1340196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マグマゴーレムにかかれば、<line-break>どんな大軍も、ただ死体の山を<line-break>築くために進軍してくるようなものだ。<end>"
EN: "Any army that marches against the Magma Golems, no matter how vast, marches only to leave behind a mountain of corpses.<end>"
KO: "마그마 골렘 앞에서는 아무리 대군이라도 시체의 산을 쌓으러 진군하는 거나 마찬가지다.<end>"

## 1340197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何事だ！？<end>"
EN: "W-what is happening?!<end>"
KO: "무, 무슨 일이냐!?<end>"

## 1340198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち、長老様っ！<line-break>て、敵襲っ！<end>"
EN: "E-Elder! We're under attack!<end>"
KO: "자, 장로님! 저, 적습입니다!<end>"

## 1340199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな！　早過ぎるぞ！<line-break>だいたい敵接近の報告は受けておらぬ。<line-break> <line-break>見張りは何をしていた！<line-break>ディンガルの大軍が近づいてきたのに、<line-break>気がつかなかったのか！<end>"
EN: "Impossible! It is too soon! We have received no report of an approaching enemy. What were the sentries doing? Did they fail to notice a vast Dyneskal army bearing down on us?!<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼! 너무 빠르다! 애초에 적이 접근했다는 보고도 받지 못했다. 감시병들은 뭘 하고 있었나! 딩갈의 대군이 다가오는데 눈치도 못 챘단 말이냐!<end>"

## 1340200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵は大軍ではありません！<line-break>て、敵、総勢４騎！<end>"
EN: "It isn't a vast army! Th-there are only four riders in all!<end>"
KO: "적은 대군이 아닙니다! 저, 적은 총 네 기입니다!<end>"

## 1340201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええい、かまわん！　今出ている<line-break>マグマゴーレムでひねりつぶせ！<end>"
EN: "Bah, no matter! Have the Magma Golems already deployed crush them!<end>"
KO: "에잇, 상관없다! 지금 나와 있는 마그마 골렘으로 짓눌러 버려!<end>"

## 1340202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それが…！<end>"
EN: "B-but...!<end>"
KO: "그, 그게…!<end>"

## 1340203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はディンガル帝国皇帝<line-break>ネメア・ランガスター・ディンガル<line-break>ウルカーン長老シェムハザに告ぐ。<line-break>これ以上の抵抗は無駄だ。<line-break>降伏して束縛の腕輪を渡せば、<line-break>生命の安全は保障しよう。<end>"
EN: "I am Nemea Lancaster Dyneskal, Emperor of the Dyneskal Empire. Hear me, Shemhaza, Elder of Ulkarne. Further resistance is futile. Surrender and hand over the Bracelet of Binding, and I shall guarantee your safety.<end>"
KO: "나는 딘갈 제국 황제 네메아 랑가스터 딘갈이다. 울칸 장로 셈하자에게 고한다. 더 이상의 저항은 무의미하다. 항복하고 속박의 팔찌를 넘기면 생명의 안전은 보장하겠다.<end>"

## 1340204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな！　マグマゴーレムが全滅！？<line-break>し、信じられぬ！<end>"
EN: "Impossible! All the Magma Golems were destroyed?! I-I cannot believe it!<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼! 마그마 골렘이 전멸!? 미, 믿을 수 없어!<end>"

## 1340205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディ、ディンガルの騎士？<line-break>しかも、ダークエルフだと！？<end>"
EN: "A-a knight of Dyneskal? And a Dark Elf at that?!<end>"
KO: "디, 딘갈의 기사? 게다가 다크엘프라고!?<end>"

## 1340206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は皇帝直属騎士オイフェ。<end>"
EN: "I am Aoife, a knight in the Emperor's direct service.<end>"
KO: "나는 황제 직속 기사 오이페.<end>"

## 1340207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…同じくゼリグ。<end>"
EN: "Likewise, Zelig.<end>"
KO: "…나도 젤리그다.<end>"

## 1340208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同じくドルドラムじゃ。<line-break>ソリアスに使える神官じゃ。<line-break> <line-break>長老シェムハザ殿、いかな大軍も<line-break>寄せつけぬマグマゴーレムも、<line-break>ただ少数の勇者にはかなわなんだな。<end>"
EN: "And I am Doldrum, a priest in the service of Solias. Elder Shemhaza, your Magma Golems may hold any great army at bay, but they were no match for a mere handful of heroes.<end>"
KO: "나도 돌드람이다. 솔리아스를 섬기는 신관이지. 장로 셈하자 공, 아무리 대군도 막아내는 마그마 골렘도 소수의 용자에게는 당해내지 못했군.<end>"

## 1340209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マグマゴーレムがあっという間に倒され<line-break>町の者は皆、抵抗する気が失せておる。<line-break>長老、降伏なされ。悪いようにはせん。<end>"
EN: "The Magma Golems fell in moments, and everyone in the town has lost the will to resist. Surrender, Elder. No harm will come to you.<end>"
KO: "마그마 골렘이 순식간에 쓰러져 마을 사람들은 모두 저항할 마음을 잃었습니다. 장로님, 항복하십시오. 나쁘게 대하지 않겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…仕方ない、降伏しよう。<line-break>束縛の腕輪はここだ。取りに来い。<end>"
EN: "I have no choice. I surrender. The Bracelet of Binding is here. Come and take it.<end>"
KO: "…어쩔 수 없군, 항복하겠다. 속박의 팔찌는 여기 있다. 가져가라.<end>"

## 1340211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカめ！　うかつに近づいてきたな！<line-break>お前たちに渡す気などないわ！<end>"
EN: "Fools! You came too close! I never had any intention of handing it over!<end>"
KO: "바보 같으니! 경솔하게 다가왔구나! 너희에게 넘길 생각은 없다!<end>"

## 1340212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<end>"
EN: "Damn...<end>"
KO: "큭….<end>"

## 1340213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…不覚。<end>"
EN: "Careless of me.<end>"
KO: "…불찰이다.<end>"

## 1340214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器を使えば、力は得られるが、<line-break>闇に墜ちてしまうのじゃぞ！<end>"
EN: "A Dark Artifact may grant you power, but it will also drag you into darkness!<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기를 쓰면 힘은 얻을 수 있지만 어둠에 떨어져 버린다!<end>"

## 1340215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の力では何も守れん。<line-break>ただ、破滅へと導かれるのみ…。<end>"
EN: "The power of darkness can protect nothing. It leads only to ruin.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 힘으로는 아무것도 지킬 수 없다. 그저 파멸로 이끌릴 뿐….<end>"

## 1340216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>神器を渡してもらおう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, hand over the artifact.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 신기를 넘겨받겠다.<end>"

## 1340217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>私のやり方に、不服か？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, do you object to my methods?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 내 방식이 불만인가?<end>"

## 1340218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "…그런가.<end>"

## 1340219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ならば、私と戦え。<line-break>そして、止めてみせよ。<end>"
EN: "Then fight me. Try to stop me.<end>"
KO: "…그렇다면 나와 싸워라. 그리고 막아 봐라.<end>"

## 1340220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとのことは宰相に任せる。<line-break>行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "I leave the rest to the Chancellor. Let us go.<end>"
KO: "나머지는 재상에게 맡기겠다. 가자.<end>"

## 1340222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、カン違いしないで！<line-break>私は恩に感じたりしないから！<end>"
EN: "But don't get the wrong idea! I don't owe you anything!<end>"
KO: "하지만 착각하지 마! 난 고맙다고 생각하지 않을 테니까!<end>"

## 1340223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はネメア様の戦士。<line-break>今度会ったとき、<line-break>あなたが敵として現れたなら…。<line-break>私は、あなたを倒すわ。<line-break>…それだけ。<end>"
EN: "I am Lord Nemea's warrior. If next we meet, you stand before me as an enemy... I will defeat you. That is all.<end>"
KO: "나는 네메아 님의 전사야. 다음에 만났을 때 네가 적으로 나타난다면… 나는 널 쓰러뜨릴 거야. …그뿐이야.<end>"

## 1340224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エスト・リューガ博士で<line-break>いらっしゃいますね。<end>"
EN: "You are Dr. Est Ryuga, I presume.<end>"
KO: "에스트 류가 박사님이시죠.<end>"

## 1340225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは？<end>"
EN: "Who are you?<end>"
KO: "당신은?<end>"

## 1340226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の行く末を憂える者<line-break>…とでも申しておきましょう。<line-break> <line-break>あなたは古代の魔道器を研究解析し、<line-break>その恩恵によって人類の文明水準を<line-break>向上させようとしていらっしゃる。<end>"
EN: "Let us say I am someone concerned for the fate of the world. You study and analyze ancient magical devices in hopes of using their gifts to advance human civilization.<end>"
KO: "세계의 앞날을 걱정하는 자… 정도로 해 두지요. 당신은 고대의 마도기를 연구·분석하고, 그 혜택으로 인류의 문명 수준을 높이려 하고 계십니다.<end>"

## 1340227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、その通りです。<end>"
EN: "Yes, that's right.<end>"
KO: "네, 그 말씀이 맞습니다.<end>"

## 1340228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼だが、あなたの実力で<line-break>ここまで来られるほど、<line-break>この地下墓地は安全な場所ではない。<line-break>あなたは、魔道器の研究から<line-break>その力を引き出すことに成功し、<line-break>その力を利用してここまで来たのだ。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me, but the Underground Cemetery is far too dangerous for someone of your abilities to have made it this far unaided. You succeeded in drawing power from the magical devices you studied, and used that power to reach this place.<end>"
KO: "실례지만 당신의 실력만으로 여기까지 올 수 있을 만큼 이 지하묘지는 안전한 곳이 아니다. 당신은 마도기 연구로 그 힘을 끌어내는 데 성공했고, 그 힘을 이용해 여기까지 온 것이다.<end>"

## 1340229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それも、その通り。<line-break>だけど、いったい…？<end>"
EN: "That's true too. But what exactly are you getting at?<end>"
KO: "그것도 맞아. 하지만 대체…?<end>"

## 1340230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器…先文明の、究極の魔道器。<line-break>あなたは、ある程度までだが、その力を<line-break>抽出することにも成功している。<line-break>そして、あなたはその力を、<line-break>愚かな人間どもに与えようとしている。<line-break>それをやめていただきたいのです。<end>"
EN: "The Dark Artifact―the ultimate magical device of the former civilization. You have even managed, to some extent, to extract its power. And now you intend to give that power to those foolish humans. I must ask you to stop.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기… 선문명의 궁극적인 마도구. 당신은 어느 정도 그 힘을 추출하는 데도 성공했습니다. 그리고 그 힘을 어리석은 인간들에게 주려 하고 있죠. 그만두어 주십시오.<end>"

## 1340231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして？<line-break>それが僕の目的なのに？<end>"
EN: "Why? That's the whole purpose of my work.<end>"
KO: "왜? 그게 내 목적인데?<end>"

## 1340232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ある偉大なお方の意志なのです。<line-break>愚かで邪悪な人間に<line-break>高度な魔法文明を与えることは、<line-break>ゴブリンに魔剣を与えるよりも<line-break>始末が悪い。<line-break>世界の滅びに直結してしまう。<end>"
EN: "It is the will of a great personage. Giving advanced magical civilization to foolish, wicked humans would be worse than giving a magic sword to a Goblin. It would lead directly to the destruction of the world.<end>"
KO: "어느 위대한 분의 뜻입니다. 어리석고 사악한 인간에게 고도의 마법 문명을 주는 건 고블린에게 마검을 쥐여 주는 것보다 더 위험합니다. 세계의 멸망으로 직결되니까요.<end>"

## 1340233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間は文明を得ることで賢くなるよ！<line-break>いずれ、かつてのような<line-break>満ちたりし時がやって来るんだ！<end>"
EN: "Civilization will make humanity wiser! One day, an age of fulfillment will return, just as it did before!<end>"
KO: "인간은 문명을 얻으면서 현명해져! 언젠가 예전처럼 충만한 시대가 다시 올 거야!<end>"

## 1340234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、愚かな…。<line-break>では、どうあっても<line-break>研究をやめていただけないのですね。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. What a fool. So nothing will persuade you to abandon your research?<end>"
KO: "훗, 어리석군…. 그럼 무슨 일이 있어도연구를 그만둘 생각은 없다는 거군요.<end>"

## 1340235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や、やめるわけには…いかない。<end>"
EN: "I-I can't abandon it.<end>"
KO: "그, 그만둘 수는… 없어.<end>"

## 1340236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>残念です、エスト・リューガ博士。<line-break> <line-break>もっとも、あのお方は、<line-break>あなたが神器に触れた時点で、<line-break>生かしておく気はなかったのですが。<end>"
EN: "I see. A pity, Dr. Est Ryuga. Though in truth, that great one never intended to let you live once you had touched the divine artifact.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까. 유감입니다, 에스트 류가 박사. 물론 그분은, 당신이 신기에 손댄 시점부터, 살려 둘 생각은 없었지만요.<end>"

## 1340237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル！　レイヴン！　カフィン！<end>"
EN: "Noel! Raven! Coffin!<end>"
KO: "노엘! 레이븐! 카핀!<end>"

## 1340238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたの、ナーシェス？<end>"
EN: "What is it, Narscess?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래, 나셰스?<end>"

## 1340239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この邪悪な魔道士は闇の神器を使って<line-break>世界を滅ぼそうとしている。<line-break>彼を倒すのだ！<end>"
EN: "This evil mage intends to use the Dark Artifact to destroy the world. Strike him down!<end>"
KO: "이 사악한 마도사는 어둠의 신기를 써서 세계를 멸망시키려 하고 있다. 그를 쓰러뜨려라!<end>"

## 1340240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、待って！<line-break>そんなの嘘だ！<end>"
EN: "W-wait! That's a lie!<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐! 그런 건 거짓말이야!<end>"

## 1340241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、この動揺ぶりを見ろ。<line-break>神器を持っているのが<line-break>何よりの証拠だ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Look how shaken he is. The fact that he carries the divine artifact is proof enough.<end>"
KO: "후후, 이 동요를 봐라. 신기를 가지고 있다는 게 무엇보다 확실한 증거다.<end>"

## 1340242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナーシェス、待って！<end>"
EN: "Narscess, wait!<end>"
KO: "나셰스, 기다려!<end>"

## 1340244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "警戒しなくても大丈夫です。<line-break>話しあいましょう。<end>"
EN: "There's no need to be wary. Let's talk.<end>"
KO: "경계하지 않아도 됩니다. 이야기로 풀죠.<end>"

## 1340245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、君は…。<end>"
EN: "Y-you...<end>"
KO: "너, 너는….<end>"

## 1340246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしは、ノエル。<line-break>焦燥の耳飾りを渡してくれさえすれば、　　　<line-break>あなたに一切危害は加えません。<line-break>闇の神器は誰の手にも危険なもの…。<line-break>あなたの邪魔がしたいんじゃないんです。<line-break>世界の危機を防ぎたいんです！<end>"
EN: "My name is Noel. If you give me the Earring of Impatience, no harm will come to you. The Dark Artifact is dangerous in anyone's hands. I'm not trying to stand in your way. I'm trying to avert a crisis that threatens the world!<end>"
KO: "저는 노엘입니다. 초조의 귀걸이만 넘겨주시면 당신께 어떤 해도 끼치지 않겠습니다. 어둠의 신기는 누구 손에 있어도 위험한 물건…. 당신을 방해하려는 게 아닙니다. 세계의 위기를 막고 싶을 뿐입니다!<end>"

## 1340247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…ありがとう！<end>"
EN: "Oh... thank you!<end>"
KO: "아… 고마워!<end>"

## 1340248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ノエルさん、って言ったね。<line-break>君でなかったら、<line-break>手放す気にはならなかったはずだよ。<line-break>君なら、大丈夫だと思ったんだ。<line-break>君から感じる、暖かい魂があれば…。<end>"
EN: "You said your name was Noel, didn't you? If it had been anyone else, I don't think I could have let it go. But I felt I could trust you. I could sense the warmth of your soul...<end>"
KO: "…노엘이라고 했지. 네가 아니었다면 넘겨줄 생각이 들지 않았을 거야. 너라면 괜찮다고 생각했어. 네게서 느껴지는 따뜻한 혼이 있다면….<end>"

## 1340250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配しなくていい。　　　　<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>俺は、レイヴン。<line-break>君と似て、<line-break>ノエルの魂に恩を受けた者だ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. I'm Raven. Like you, I owe a debt to Noel's soul.<end>"
KO: "걱정하지 마. 난 레이븐. 너와 마찬가지로 노엘의 영혼에 은혜를 입은 자다.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、一体…。<end>"

## 1340255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、愚かな。<line-break>それがお前の意図か…。　　　　　<line-break> <line-break>私とレイヴンは、<line-break>神器を安全なところまで持っていく。<end>"

## 1340257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "노엘과 카핀은 마도사 에스트와 <value:$28>을 제거해라.<end>"

## 1340258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、でも…。<end>"
EN: "B-but...<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만….<end>"

## 1340259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…目的は、時間稼ぎだ。<line-break>命までは奪う必要はない。<end>"

## 1340260

## 1340261

## 1340262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "音を聞きつけて引き返して来たんだ。<line-break>君が危なければ、俺が加勢しようと…<line-break>だが…<end>"
EN: "I heard the commotion and came back. I meant to help if you were in danger... But...<end>"
KO: "소리를 듣고 돌아온 거야. 네가 위험하다면 내가 도우려고… 하지만…<end>"

## 1340266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。私たち、負けちゃいました。<line-break>正々堂々戦って、負けたんです。<line-break>レイヴン、闇の神器はナーシェスに？<end>"
EN: "Yes. We lost. We fought fair and square, and we lost. Raven, did you give the Dark Artifact to Narscess?<end>"
KO: "네. 저희, 졌어요. 정정당당하게 싸워서 졌습니다. 레이븐, 어둠의 신기는 나셰스에게?<end>"

## 1340267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "파티의 리더는 노엘. 그건 나보다 오히려 당신이 더 잘 실감하고 있겠죠?<end>"

## 1340269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たちよりも<value:$28>の方が<line-break>正しく、力でも大きく勝っている…。<end>"

KO: "우리보다 <value:$28> 쪽이 옳고, 힘에서도 훨씬 앞서 있어….<end>"

## 1340270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "노엘, 너는 그렇게 확신했다는 거지?<end>"

## 1340271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340282

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしもそうは思ったけど…<line-break>この青白いのは、現に闇の神器の力を<line-break>使っていたようじゃないさ。<line-break>それを知ったら、<line-break>ナーシェスの言ったことも、<line-break>まるきり否定できなくなるわ。<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか！　ふふふふっ。<line-break>君があの<value:$28>なんだ。　<line-break> <line-break>兄さんからの手紙で聞いてるよ。<line-break>エリエナイ公レムオン・リューガは<line-break>僕の兄さんなんだ。そうか、君が…。<end>"

KO: "그렇구나! 후후후훗. 네가 그 <value:$28>구나. 형에게 온 편지에서 들었어. 에리에나이 공 레무온 류가는 내 형이야. 그래, 네가…<end>"

## 1340314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>冒険者の君には、これからも<line-break>世界のどこかで会うかもしれないね。<line-break>その時はよろしく。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Since you're an adventurer, we may meet again somewhere in the world. If we do, I hope we can count on each other.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 모험자인 너와는 앞으로도 세계 어딘가에서 만날지도 모르겠네. 그때도 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 1340315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕はもう大丈夫だよ。<line-break>ここの調査も終わったし、<line-break>もう行くよ。…じゃあね。<end>"

## 1340316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのエルフ、たぶん竜王の使者だよ。<line-break>竜王の命で僕を処理しに来たんだ。<line-break> <line-break>君も気をつけて。<line-break>竜王は自分の箱庭の均衡を崩す者を<line-break>許さないから…。<end>"
EN: "That Elf was probably an envoy of the Dragon King, sent on orders to dispose of me. You should be careful too. The Dragon King won't tolerate anyone who upsets the balance of his little garden...<end>"
KO: "그 엘프, 아마 용왕의 사자일 거야. 용왕의 명으로 나를 처리하러 온 거지. 너도 조심해. 용왕은 자기 정원의 균형을 깨는 자를용서하지 않으니까….<end>"

## 1340317
