source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2いきなり襲いかかってくるなんて…<line-break>こいつら、何者なのよ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>3いきなり襲いかかってくるとは、<line-break>物騒な連中じゃな。<line-break>話を聞かれたのがまずかったかの…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>6びっくりしたね、<value:$28>。<line-break>こいつら、いきなりボクらに<line-break>襲いかかってきたけど…<end><if><value:$01><equal>13暗殺術の使い手か…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>おかしな刺青してるよ。<line-break><line-break>ほら、テラネにもある天空神の<line-break>紋章に似てるけど…<line-break>なんか、気味が悪いね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14いきなり襲いかかってくるとは、<line-break>なんとも物騒な連中じゃ。<line-break>話を聞かれたのがまずかったのか…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ねぇ、見て、見て、見てぇ！<line-break>目玉の刺青してるよぉ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、大発見だ！<line-break>こいつ、変な刺青してるぜ。<line-break>まるで目玉みたいな模様だ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4<value:$28>様の顔を見るなり、<line-break>いきなり襲いかかってくるだなんて、<line-break>よっぽど恥ずかしいお話をしてたのね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>5見ろよ！　<value:$28>！<line-break>こいつらみんな同じ刺青してる！<line-break>へへっ、わかったぜ。つまり…<end><if><value:$01><equal>17いやっ！　最悪、最悪よ、最悪よぉ～！<line-break>悪趣味な刺青してるわ！！<line-break>目玉よ、目玉！　め・だ・ま！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193びっくりしました。<line-break>どうして襲ってきたんでしょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191いきなり襲ってくるなんて驚いたわ。<line-break>しかもこの刺青…<line-break>天空神の紋章に似てるわ。<end>いきなり襲いかかってくるなんて、<line-break>まったく、物騒な人たちだねぇ。<line-break>格好もなんだか不気味だしね。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Attacking us out of nowhere... Who are these people?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Attacking without warning? A dangerous lot indeed. Perhaps letting us overhear them was a problem...<end><if><value:$01><equal>6That was a surprise, <value:$28>. They just attacked us out of nowhere...<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Someone trained in assassination...<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, <value:$28>, look at that strange tattoo. It resembles the emblem of the Sky God they have in Terane, but... there's something unsettling about it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Attacking without warning? A dangerous lot indeed. Perhaps letting us overhear them was a problem...<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Hey, look, look, look! There's an eyeball tattoo!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, look what I found! This one has a weird tattoo. The pattern looks just like an eyeball.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4They attacked the moment they saw <value:$28>! They must have been discussing something terribly embarrassing.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Look, <value:$28>! They've all got the same tattoo! Heh, I get it. That means...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Ew! It's awful, awful, simply awful! What a tasteless tattoo! It's an eyeball! An eye-ball!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193That startled me. Why did they attack us?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I wasn't expecting them to attack without warning. And this tattoo... it resembles the emblem of the Sky God.<end>Attacking us out of nowhere... What a dangerous bunch. They look rather sinister too.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2갑자기 덤벼들다니… 이 녀석들 정체가 뭐야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3갑자기 덤벼들다니 험악한 녀석들이군. 이야기를 들은 게 문제였나….<end><if><value:$01><equal>6놀랐지, <value:$28>. 이 녀석들 갑자기 우리한테 덤벼들었는데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>13암살술의 사용자인가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>15저기, <value:$28>, 이상한 문신이 있어. 봐, 테라네에도 있는 천공신 문양과 닮았지만… 왠지 기분 나쁘네….<end><if><value:$01><equal>14갑자기 덤벼들다니 참 험악한 녀석들이군. 이야기를 들은 게 문제였나….<end><if><value:$01><equal>19저기, 봐, 봐, 봐아! 눈알 문신이 있어어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 대발견이야! 이 녀석 이상한 문신이 있어. 마치 눈알 같은 무늬야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4<value:$28> 님 얼굴을 보자마자 갑자기 덤벼들다니, 굉장히 부끄러운 이야기를 하고 있었나 봐.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5봐, <value:$28>! 이 녀석들 전부 같은 문신이 있어! 헤헤, 알겠어. 즉…<end><if><value:$01><equal>17싫어! 최악, 최악이야, 최악! 취향 나쁜 문신을 했잖아! 눈알이야, 눈알! 누, 눈, 알!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193놀랐어요. 왜 공격한 걸까요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191갑자기 공격해 와서 놀랐어. 게다가 이 문신… 천공신의 문양과 닮았네.<end>갑자기 덤벼들다니 정말 험악한 사람들이네. 차림도 왠지 기분 나쁘고.<end>"

## 1340320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2そうね。<line-break>実際に見て、確かめなくちゃ。<line-break>オッシ先生もそう教えてくれたんだし。<line-break>何だか、１人だけ様子がおかしいのが<line-break>いたのも気になるわね。<line-break>行きましょ、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>3そうじゃな、どうも気になる。<line-break>少し近隣を当たってみるとするかの…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>6うん、行ってみよう。<line-break>すっごく気になるしさ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…やめておけ。お前程度のひよっこが<line-break>首を突っ込むべきことではない。<line-break><line-break>…と言っても、<line-break>行く気なのだろう？<line-break>死んでからでは後悔もできんぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15そうだね。<line-break>行ってみよう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14そうじゃな、どうも気になる。<line-break>少し近隣を当たってみるとするか…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>19そうだね、気になるよねぇ。<line-break>行ってみよ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>10そうだね、姉ちゃん。<line-break>行ってみよう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4この危険そうな奴らの正体を　　　　　　　<line-break>探ってみるって…<line-break>どうしよっかな…。<line-break>ついていこうっと！　でもでも<line-break>こんな危険に飛び込もうとするなんて、<line-break>ちょっと、おバカさんかも！　きゃっ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5はやってるのかな？<line-break><line-break><line-break>よーし、行こうぜ！<line-break>とめたって俺ひとりでも<line-break>行くつもりだしな！<end><if><value:$01><equal>17天空神ノトゥーンの紋章に似てる？<line-break>…ちゃ、ちゃんと知っててよ！<line-break>ア、アタクシが言いたいのはぁ、別！<line-break>そ、そうよ、アタクシはあいつらの正体を<line-break>突き止めないとって言いたかったの！<line-break>さっさと急いで出発するのよぉ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193そうですね、気になりますね。<line-break>少し怖いけど行ってみましょう！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191そうね、危険な感じがするけど、<line-break>気になるわね。<line-break>行きましょ。<end>何者かつきとめる？<line-break>こういうのに関わるのってさ、<line-break>か～な～り、危険なんだよ？<line-break>だーかーら、危険だからこそ<line-break>行く価値があるんじゃない。<line-break>それじゃ、捜索、かいし～！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Yes. We need to see for ourselves and find out. That's what Master Ossi taught us, after all. And I'm curious about the one who seemed to be acting strangely. Let's go, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Yes, it is troubling. Let us search the surrounding area...<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Yeah, let's go. I'm dying to know what this is about.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Stay out of it. This is no business for a greenhorn like you. Though I suppose you mean to go anyway. Once you're dead, it'll be too late for regrets.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15All right. Let's go take a look.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Yes, it is troubling. Let us search the surrounding area...<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Yeah, you want to know too, don't you? Shall we go?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Yeah, Sis. Let's go take a look.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Investigate who those dangerous-looking people really are...? What should I do...? Oh, I'll come along! But plunging straight into danger like this might make you a bit of a fool! Eek!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Is this the latest craze or something? All right, let's go! Even if you try to stop me, I'm going alone!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17It resembles the emblem of Notun, the Sky God? Y-you ought to know that already! Th-that's not what I was trying to say! I meant that we must uncover who those people really are! Now hurry up and get moving!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Yes, I am curious. It's a little frightening, but let's go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191It feels dangerous, but I want to know more. Let's go.<end>Find out who they are? Getting involved in something like this is ve-ry dangerous, you know? And that's exactly why it's worth doing! All right, let the search begin!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2그래. 직접 보고 확인해야지. 오시 선생님도 그렇게 가르쳤고. 혼자 상태가 이상한 녀석도 신경 쓰여. 가자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3그래, 아무래도 신경 쓰이는군. 근처를 좀 알아보자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6응, 가 보자. 정말 신경 쓰여.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…그만둬라. 너 같은 풋내기가 끼어들 일이 아니다. …그래도 갈 생각이겠지? 죽고 나서는 후회도 못 한다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15그래. 가 보자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그래, 아무래도 신경 쓰이는군. 근처를 좀 알아보자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그래, 신경 쓰이지. 가 볼까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10그래, 누나. 가 보자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4위험해 보이는 녀석들의 정체를 조사한다니… 어쩌지…. 따라가야겠다! 그래도 이런 위험에 뛰어들다니 좀 바보일지도! 꺅!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5유행인가? 좋아, 가자! 말려도 나 혼자 갈 생각이었어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17천공신 노툰의 문장과 닮았다고? …무, 물론 알고 있었거든! 아타쿠시가 말하려던 건 달라! 그래, 저 녀석들의 정체를 밝혀야 한다는 거야! 어서 출발해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그러네요, 신경 쓰여요. 조금 무섭지만 가 봐요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191그래, 위험한 느낌이지만 신경 쓰이네. 가자.<end>정체를 알아내자고? 이런 일에 끼어드는 건 꽤 위험하거든? 하지만 위험하니까 갈 가치가 있는 거지. 그럼 수색 시작~!<end>"

## 1340321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（初めて見る敵だ。）<line-break><line-break><line-break>（言っていたことも気になる。<line-break>　追いかけてみよう。）<line-break> <end>"
EN: "(I've never seen enemies like these before. What I heard bothers me too. I should follow them.)<end>"
KO: "(처음 보는 적이다.) (한 말도 신경 쓰인다. 쫓아가 보자.)<end>"

## 1340322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいぞ、ノエル～！<line-break>あんたなら、できる！<end>"
EN: "That's it, Noel! You can do it!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 노엘~! 너라면 할 수 있어!<end>"

## 1340323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日こそ、その大剣、<line-break>天動地鳴が抜ける日だ！<end>"
EN: "Today's the day you pull out the greatsword Heaven Moves, Earth Roars!<end>"
KO: "오늘이야말로 그 대검, 천동지명이 뽑히는 날이다!<end>"

## 1340324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて、この地には<line-break>ソウルイーターという<line-break>魔物が住みついておった。　<line-break>人が人である源とも呼ぶべき<line-break>ソウルを食っちまうという、<line-break>まこと恐ろしい魔物じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Long ago, a monster called the Soul Eater made its lair in this land. It devoured the Soul―the very source of what makes us human. A fearsome monster indeed.<end>"
KO: "예전에 이 땅에는 소울 이터라는 마물이 살고 있었다. 사람이 사람인 근원이라 할 소울을 먹어 치운다는 정말 무서운 마물이지.<end>"

## 1340325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ある時、ひとりの勇者が現れ、<line-break>この神殿に魔物を封じた。<line-break>その勇者が使った剣があれじゃ。<line-break>地中深く刺さったその剣は、<line-break>いかなる怪力の持ち主にも<line-break>引き抜くことができん。<end>"
EN: "Then one day, a hero appeared and sealed the monster inside this temple. That is the sword the hero wielded. It lies buried deep in the earth, and no one, however mighty, has ever been able to pull it free.<end>"
KO: "어느 날 한 용사가 나타나 이 신전에 마물을 봉인했다. 그 용사가 사용한 검이 저것이지. 땅속 깊이 꽂힌 그 검은 아무리 괴력을 지닌 자라도 뽑아낼 수 없다.<end>"

## 1340326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世を救う使命を負った勇者だけが、<line-break>あの剣を引き抜くことができるという…<line-break>古くからの言い伝えじゃ。<end>"
EN: "It is said that only the Hero charged with saving the world can draw that sword. So the old legend goes.<end>"
KO: "이 세상을 구할 사명을 짊어진 용사만이 그 검을 뽑을 수 있다는… 오래된 전승이란다.<end>"

## 1340327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3ノエル様は時おり<line-break>怪物退治などにも活躍されている。<line-break> <line-break>剣の腕もたつ、利発なお嬢さんだが、<line-break>しかし、惜しいことだ。<line-break>男だったら、竜騎士にもなれたものを。<end>ここの領主様は、かつて<line-break>ソウルイーターを倒したという　　　<line-break>勇者の子孫だということだ。<line-break>つまり、その娘であるノエル様も、<line-break>勇者の血筋であるということになる。<line-break>きっと、あの剣を抜けると思うよ。<end>数日前、旅の預言者様がいらしてな。<line-break>折れたままだった俺の足を、<line-break>祈るだけで治してしまわれたんだよ。<line-break>あれは全く奇蹟だった。<line-break>そのあと、預言者様は、ノエル様こそが<line-break>あの剣を抜く者だと預言されたそうだ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Lady Noel sometimes distinguishes herself hunting monsters, too. She's a clever young woman and highly skilled with a sword, but it's a pity. If she were a man, she could have become a dragon knight.<end>The lord here is said to be descended from the hero who once defeated the Soul Eater. That means the lord's daughter, Lady Noel, also has the hero's blood in her veins. I'm sure she can pull out that sword.<end>A traveling prophet came here a few days ago. My broken leg was healed with nothing but a prayer. It was nothing short of a miracle. Afterward, apparently, the prophet foretold that Lady Noel would be the one to pull out that sword.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3노엘 님은 때때로 괴물 퇴치에도 나서신다네. 검술도 뛰어나고 총명한 아가씨지만, 참 아쉬운 일이야. 남자였다면 용기사도 될 수 있었을 텐데.<end>이곳 영주님은 예전에 소울 이터를 쓰러뜨렸다는 용사의 후손이라고 하네. 그러니 그 딸인 노엘 님도 용사의 혈통이라는 뜻이지. 분명 그 검을 뽑을 수 있을 거야.<end>며칠 전 여행 중인 예언자님이 오셨는데 말이야. 부러진 채였던 내 다리를 기도만으로 고쳐 버리셨다네. 정말 기적이었어. 그 뒤 예언자님은 노엘 님이야말로 그 검을 뽑을 자라고 예언하셨다더군.<end>"

## 1340328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここの領主様は、かつて<line-break>ソウルイーターを倒したという<line-break>勇者の子孫だということだ。<line-break>つまり、その娘であるノエル様も、<line-break>勇者の血筋であるということになる。<line-break>きっと、あの剣を抜けると思うよ。<end>"
EN: "The lord here is said to be descended from the hero who once defeated the Soul Eater. That means the lord's daughter, Lady Noel, also has the hero's blood in her veins. I'm sure she can pull out that sword.<end>"
KO: "이곳 영주님은 옛날 소울 이터를 쓰러뜨렸다는 용사의 후손이라고 해. 즉 그 딸인 노엘 님도 용사의 혈통이라는 뜻이지. 분명 그 검을 뽑을 수 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1340329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2ときどき、こうやって村の力自慢や<line-break>通りがかりの冒険者が挑戦するのさ。<line-break>それをひやかすのが、わしの道楽でね。<end>今回こそは抜けるとみんな信じてるんだ。<line-break>何せ、旅の預言者様のお墨つきだからな。<line-break>ノエルがあの剣を抜く！　ってなあ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2Every so often, one of the village strongmen or some passing adventurer tries to pull it out. I get my fun heckling them.<end>Everyone believes it'll come out this time. Noel has the traveling prophet's word on it, after all. “Noel will draw the sword!” That's what the prophet said.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2가끔 이렇게 마을의 힘깨나 쓰는 사람이나 지나가던 모험자가 도전하거든. 그걸 구경하며 놀리는 게 내 취미지.<end>이번에는 다들 정말 뽑힐 거라고 믿고 있어. 여행하는 예언자님이 보증했으니까. 노엘이 그 검을 뽑는다고 말이야.<end>"

## 1340330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3今までに何人があの剣に挑んだかねぇ。<line-break>でも成功したためしは一度もないよ。<end>あの娘は領主様の１人娘ノエルさ。<line-break>身体は小さいけど、剣の腕は確かだよ。<end>何日か前に村を通りかかった預言者が<line-break>ノエルならできるって<line-break>太鼓判を押してくれたんだよ。<line-break>まだ若い、線の細い男だったけどね、<line-break>怪我や病気を、祈りで治しちまうのさ。<line-break>ありゃ、まったく奇蹟だったよ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3I wonder how many people have tried to pull that sword out by now. Not one has ever succeeded.<end>That's Noel, the lord's only daughter. She may be small, but she certainly knows how to use a sword.<end>A prophet passing through the village a few days ago declared that Noel could do it. He was a slender young man, but he could heal injuries and illnesses with a prayer. It was nothing short of a miracle.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3지금까지 몇 명이나 저 검에 도전했는지 몰라. 하지만 성공한 사람은 한 명도 없었어.<end>저 아가씨는 영주님의 외동딸 노엘이야. 몸집은 작아도 검 솜씨는 확실하지.<end>며칠 전에 마을을 지나던 예언자가 노엘이라면 할 수 있다고 장담했어. 아직 젊고 가느다란 남자였는데 부상이나 병을 기도로 치료하더라고. 정말 기적이었지.<end>"

## 1340331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの娘は領主様の一人娘ノエルさ。<line-break>身体は小さいけど、剣の腕は確かだよ。<end>"
EN: "That's Noel, the lord's only daughter. She may be small, but she certainly knows how to use a sword.<end>"
KO: "그 아이는 영주님의 외동딸 노엘이야. 몸은 작아도 검 솜씨는 확실하지.<end>"

## 1340332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3地図にも載っていないような<line-break>ヘンピな村だけど、<line-break>こんな珍しい風習があったなんて。<line-break>実際に旅してみないと<line-break>世界はわからない、ってことかな。<end>あ、私の角が珍しいですか？<line-break>無理もない。私たちコーンス族は<line-break>今では少数民族ですからね。<line-break>それにしても、うーん、<line-break>興味深い風習だなあ。<end>君も、惜しいことをしたね。<line-break>もう数日早くここへ来ていれば、<line-break>預言者の奇蹟も見ることができたのに。<line-break>銀色の長い髪をした若者でね、<line-break>私も信者になろうかと考えたほどさ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3This village is so remote it doesn't even appear on the map, yet it has such an unusual custom. Perhaps you can never truly know the world until you travel through it yourself.<end>Oh, do you find my horns unusual? That's understandable. We Konsu are an ethnic minority now. Still... what a fascinating custom.<end>You missed quite a sight yourself. If only you'd arrived a few days earlier, you could have witnessed the prophet's miracle. He was a young man with long silver hair. I was even tempted to become a believer myself.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3지도에도 실리지 않을 만큼 외진 마을인데 이런 희귀한 풍습이 있었다니. 직접 여행해 보지 않으면 세상은 모르는 법인가 봐.<end>아, 제 뿔이 신기합니까? 그럴 만도 하죠. 우리 콘스족은 이제 소수민족이니까요. 그래도 음, 흥미로운 풍습이군요.<end>당신도 아쉽게 됐네요. 며칠만 일찍 왔으면 예언자의 기적도 볼 수 있었을 텐데. 은빛 긴 머리의 젊은이였는데, 저도 신자가 될까 생각했을 정도예요.<end>"

## 1340333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、私の角が珍しいですか？<line-break>無理もない。私たちコーンス族は<line-break>今では少数民族ですからね。<line-break>それにしても、うーん、<line-break>興味深い風習だなあ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, do you find my horns unusual? That's understandable. We Konsu are an ethnic minority now. Still... what a fascinating custom.<end>"
KO: "아, 제 뿔이 신기합니까? 그럴 만도 하죠. 우리 콘스족은 지금은 소수민족이니까요. 그래도 음, 흥미로운 풍습이군요.<end>"

## 1340339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2あの真っ黒い服の男…<line-break>たしかにこの中へ入ったはずだけど…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>3ここは…神殿？<line-break>あの不気味な黒ずくめと<line-break>何か関係があるのかの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>6うん、ボクも見たよ。<line-break>あの黒ずくめ、確かにここへ<line-break>入って行ったはずなんだけど…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…うろたえるな。<line-break>奴は確かにここへ入った。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15おかしいな。<line-break>ここへ入って行ったと<line-break>思ったんだけど…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14ここは…神殿か？<line-break>ううむ、あの不気味な黒ずくめと<line-break>何か関係があるのじゃろうか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ねえ、<value:$28>も見たよねえ？<line-break>あの真っ黒な服の人、<line-break>ここへ入って行ったよねえ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>10あれ、誰もいないぜ。<line-break>確かにここへ入って行ったと<line-break>思ったんだけど…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4きゃっ、見間違いかも？<line-break><value:$28>様も、<line-break>案外オトボケさんなのね！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5何だこりゃ、<line-break>もぬけのカラじゃねえかよ。<line-break>ホントにこんな陰気なとこに…<end><if><value:$01><equal>17ちょっと、何よこれ！<line-break>誰もいないじゃないのよ！<line-break>アタクシを馬鹿にするつもりなの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193ここって神殿ですよね？<line-break>確か、ここに入っていきましたよね？<end><if><value:$01><equal>191誰もいないわ。<line-break>確かここに入ったはずなのに。<end>おかしいな、誰もいないね。<line-break>表の騒ぎに気を取られて、<line-break>見失っちゃったのかな？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2That man dressed all in black... I'm sure he came in here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Is this... a temple? Could it have something to do with that sinister figure in black?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Yeah, I saw that too. That figure in black definitely came in here...<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Don't lose your head. That figure definitely came in here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15That's strange. I thought that figure came in here...<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Is this... a temple? Hmm. Could it have something to do with that sinister figure in black?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19You saw it too, didn't you, <value:$28>? That person dressed all in black came in here, right?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Huh? Nobody's here. I could've sworn someone came in this way...<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Eek! Perhaps we both imagined it? It seems even <value:$28> can be a little scatterbrained!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5What the hell? This place is completely empty. Did that figure really come into this gloomy hole...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17What is this?! Nobody's here! Is this some attempt to make a fool of me?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193This is a temple, isn't it? Someone came in here, didn't they?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191No one's here. I'm sure that figure came in this way.<end>That's strange. No one's here. Did the commotion outside distract us long enough to lose sight of that figure?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2그 새까만 옷의 남자… 분명 이 안으로 들어갔을 텐데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>3여기는… 신전인가? 그 기분 나쁜 검은 옷 사내와 무슨 관계가 있는 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6응, 나도 봤어. 그 검은 옷, 분명 여기로 들어갔는데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…당황하지 마라. 놈은 분명 여기로 들어왔다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15이상하네. 여기로 들어갔다고 생각했는데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>14여긴… 신전인가? 음, 그 기분 나쁜 검은 옷 사내와 뭔가 관계가 있는 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19있잖아, <value:$28>도 봤지? 그 새까만 옷 입은 사람, 여기로 들어갔지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10어라, 아무도 없잖아. 분명 여기로 들어갔다고 생각했는데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>4꺅, 잘못 본 걸지도? <value:$28> 님도 의외로 허당이네!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5뭐야 이거, 텅 비었잖아. 정말 이런 음침한 데로…<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐야 이게! 아무도 없잖아! 날 바보 취급하는 거야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193여기 신전이죠? 분명 여기로 들어갔죠?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191아무도 없네. 분명 여기로 들어왔을 텐데.<end>이상하네, 아무도 없어. 밖의 소동에 정신이 팔려 놓친 걸까?<end>"

## 1340340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2読みは外れてなかったみたいね、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>行くわよっ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3これが狙いか！<line-break>突破するぞ、<value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6こ、こいつらっ、<line-break>ボクたちをやっつける気！？<line-break><value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>13そう来ると思っていた。<line-break>俺の足手まといになるなよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…初めからこれが狙いだったのかな。<line-break>できるならやりたくないけど、<line-break>仕方ないみたいだね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14うぬっ、囲まれたか！<line-break>ならば仕方あるまい。<line-break>突破するぞ、<value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19どうしてなの？<line-break>ルルアンタたちは、何もしてないのに！<end><if><value:$01><equal>10ちっくしょう、挟み撃ちか！<line-break>気をつけろよ、姉ちゃん！<end><if><value:$01><equal>4見失ったうえ、挟み撃ちだなんて、<line-break>オマヌケさんの汚名からは<line-break>もはや逃れられないかも！　きゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5…って、何やってんだ、<line-break><value:$28>！<line-break>囲まれてんじゃねえかよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>17よ、呼んだら出てきたじゃないのよ！<line-break>な、何とかしなさいよっ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193キャ！？<line-break>周りを囲まれちゃいました！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191やっぱりいたわね。<line-break><value:$28>、行くわよ！<end>こいつら、僕たちを始末する気？<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Looks like our hunch was right, <value:$28>. Let's go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3So this was their plan! We're breaking through, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Th-they're trying to take us down! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13I expected as much. Don't slow me down, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Was this their plan from the start? I'd rather not fight, but it seems we have no choice.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Gah, we're surrounded! Then we have no choice. We break through, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Why? Lulluantha and the others haven't done anything!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Damn it, it's a pincer attack! Watch yourself, Sis!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4First we lose our quarry, then we get caught in a pincer attack. We may never live down looking this foolish! Eek!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Wait, what the hell are you doing, <value:$28>?! We're surrounded!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Th-they actually came when I called! D-do something, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Eek! We're surrounded!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I knew they were here. Come on, <value:$28>!<end>Are they planning to finish us off? <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2예상은 빗나가지 않은 것 같네, <value:$28>. 가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3이게 노림수였나! 돌파한다, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6이, 이 녀석들! 우리를 해치려는 거야!? <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13이렇게 나올 줄 알았다. 내 발목 잡지 마라, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…처음부터 이게 목적이었던 걸까. 할 수 있으면 싸우고 싶진 않지만 어쩔 수 없나 봐.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14으윽, 포위당했나! 그렇다면 어쩔 수 없지. 돌파한다, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19왜 그러는 거야? 루루안타들은 아무것도 안 했는데!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10제길, 협공이냐! 조심해, 누나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4놓친 데다 협공이라니, 얼간이라는 오명에선 이제 벗어나기 힘들지도! 꺅!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5…야, 뭐 하는 거야, <value:$28>! 포위됐잖아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17부, 부르니까 정말 나왔잖아! 어, 어떻게 좀 해 봐, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193꺅!? 주위가 포위됐어요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191역시 있었네. <value:$28>, 간다!<end>이 녀석들, 우리를 없애려는 거야? <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1340344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2…剣が抜けそうなのね。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>おかしいわ。急に空気が変わった…。<line-break>外へ行くわよ、<value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3…感じたか？<line-break>何かが起きようとしている…。<line-break>行くぞ、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>6あのノエルって子が、剣を…。<line-break>でも…いきなり、<line-break>空気がすうっと冷たくなったみたい…。<line-break>嫌な予感がする。<line-break>行こう、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13お前も感じたか？<line-break>何かが起きようとしている…。<line-break>ボヤボヤするな。行くぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>…<line-break>何か、嫌な予感がするよ。<line-break>外へ行って確かめよう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14感じたか？　この空気の変化…。<line-break>並みの剣ではないと見ていたが、<line-break>何か関係があるのかもしれんぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>19何だかおかしい…。<line-break>とっても嫌な感じがするよう…。<line-break>外へ行こう、<value:$28>？<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、どうしたんだ？<line-break>いきなり変な顔して？<line-break>外が気になるのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>4ノエル様、剣が抜けそうなのね！<line-break>きゃ！　見に行きたいかも！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5抜ケンが抜けるのか。<line-break>何だかちっと気になるな。<line-break>行ってみようぜ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17剣よ！　剣が抜けるのよっ！<line-break>ボヤボヤしてないで、<line-break>見に行くのよ、下等生物！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193どうしたんですか、<line-break><value:$28>さん？<line-break>外が気になるんですか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>191あなたも気づいた、<value:$28>？<line-break>空気が変わったわね。<line-break>急いで外に出ましょ。<end>ねえ、なぜだろう。急に空気が<line-break>冷たくなったみたいだ。<line-break><line-break>あのノエルって子が剣を抜くことに<line-break>関係があるのかな？<line-break>外へ行ってみようよ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2It looks as though the sword is about to come free. Something's wrong. The air suddenly changed... Let's go, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You felt it too? Something is about to happen... Stop dawdling. We're going.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, <value:$28>... The sword is coming free! Stop dawdling and go see, you lower life-form!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193What's wrong, <value:$28>? Is something outside bothering you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191You noticed too, <value:$28>? The air has changed. Let's hurry outside.<end>Why does the air suddenly feel so cold? Could it have something to do with that girl Noel drawing the sword? Let's go outside and see.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2…검이 뽑힐 것 같네. 이상해. 갑자기 공기가 달라졌어…. 밖으로 가자, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3…느꼈나? 뭔가 일어나려 한다…. 간다, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6저 노엘이라는 애가 검을…. 그런데… 갑자기 공기가 싸늘해진 것 같아…. 불길한 예감이 들어. 가자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13너도 느꼈나? 뭔가 일어나려 한다…. 멍하니 있지 마. 간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15저기, <value:$28>… 뭔가 불길한 예감이 들어. 밖으로 나가 확인하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14느꼈나? 이 공기의 변화…. 평범한 검은 아니라고 봤지만 뭔가 관계가 있을지도 모르겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19뭔가 이상해…. 정말 싫은 느낌이 들어어…. 밖으로 가자, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 왜 그래? 갑자기 이상한 표정 하고? 밖이 신경 쓰여?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4노엘 님, 검을 뽑을 것 같네! 꺅! 보러 가고 싶을지도!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5검이 뽑히는 건가. 왠지 좀 신경 쓰이네. 가 보자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17검이야! 검이 뽑히는 거야! 멍하니 있지 말고 보러 가는 거야, 하등생물!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193무슨 일이세요, <value:$28> 씨? 밖이 신경 쓰이세요?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191너도 눈치챘구나, <value:$28>? 공기가 변했네. 서둘러 밖으로 나가자.<end>저기, 왜일까. 갑자기 공기가 차가워진 것 같아. 저 노엘이라는 애가 검을 뽑는 것과 관계가 있는 걸까? 밖으로 가 보자.<end>"

## 1340345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっかりしろ。<line-break>自分がなぜ助かったかを、考えるんだ。<line-break>君は…無限の…<line-break>うっ…！<end>"
EN: "Pull yourself together. Think about why you were spared. You are... an infinite... Ngh...!<end>"
KO: "정신 차려. 자신이 왜 살아났는지 생각해. 너는… 무한의… 윽…!<end>"

## 1340346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者だ！　勇者の再臨だ！<end>"
EN: "It's the Hero! The Hero has returned!<end>"
KO: "용사다! 용사의 재림이다!<end>"

## 1340347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つ、ついに<line-break>剣が抜けるときが訪れたか…。<end>"
EN: "C-could the time have finally come for the sword to be drawn...?<end>"
KO: "드, 드디어 검을 뽑을 때가 온 건가….<end>"

## 1340348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（急に空気が変わった…。）<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>（嫌な予感だ。<line-break>　外へ行ってみよう。）<end>"
EN: "(The air suddenly changed... I have a bad feeling about this. I should go outside.)<end>"
KO: "(갑자기 분위기가 바뀌었어….) (불길한 예감이 든다. 밖에 나가 보자.)<end>"

## 1340349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…に、逃げろ…。<line-break>今の…うちに…！<end>"
EN: "Ngh... r-run... while you... still can...!<end>"
KO: "큭… 도, 도망쳐…. 지금… 당장…!<end>"

## 1340350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わたしが剣を抜いた。　　　　　　　<line-break><line-break><line-break>そしたら、ソウルイーターが復活して…<line-break>みんなからソウルを奪って、<line-break>み、みんなは消滅してしまった…。<end>"
EN: "I... pulled out the sword. Then the Soul Eater came back... It took everyone's Souls, and th-they all vanished...<end>"
KO: "…내가 검을 뽑았어. 그랬더니 소울 이터가 부활해서… 모두에게서 소울을 빼앗고, 모, 모두 사라져버렸어…<end>"

## 1340351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしだけは、<line-break>なぜか何ともなかった…。<line-break><line-break>わ、わたし…<line-break>わたし…何をしたの…？<line-break>悪いのは…わたし…？ <end>"
EN: "For some reason, I was the only one left unharmed... I-I... What have I done...? Was it... my fault...?<end>"
KO: "나만은 왜인지 아무렇지도 않았어…. 나, 나… 내가 뭘 한 거지…? 잘못한 건… 나야…?<end>"

## 1340352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルイーターを追い払ってくれて<line-break>ありがとうございました。　<line-break> <line-break>…<value:$28>さん、<line-break>でしたね。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for driving away the Soul Eater. You are... <value:$28>, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "소울 이터를 쫓아내 주셔서 감사합니다. …<value:$28>님, 맞으시죠.<end>"

## 1340353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜあなたが生きてここにいるのか…<line-break>それは聞きません。<line-break>だから…<line-break>…よろしければ、<line-break>あなたのその武器で、<line-break>わたしを殺してください。<end>"
EN: "I won't ask why you're still alive and here. So... if you are willing, please kill me with that weapon.<end>"
KO: "왜 당신이 살아서 여기 있는지는 묻지 않겠습니다. 그러니… 괜찮다면 그 무기로 저를 죽여 주세요.<end>"

## 1340354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかたがない…<end>"
EN: "I have no choice…<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없군…<end>"

## 1340355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "馬鹿なことは言うな<end>"
EN: "Don't be ridiculous.<end>"
KO: "바보 같은 소리 하지 마<end>"

## 1340356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい…覚悟はできています。<line-break><line-break><line-break>わたしがあの剣を抜いたせいで、<line-break>村のみんなが消えてしまった…。<end>"
EN: "Yes... I'm ready. Everyone in the village vanished because I pulled out that sword...<end>"
KO: "네… 각오는 되어 있습니다. 제가 그 검을 뽑는 바람에, 마을 사람들이 모두 사라졌어요….<end>"

## 1340357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いっそ、あの時、<line-break>わたしも魂を抜かれて<line-break>消えてしまえばよかったのに…　　<line-break>でも、でも、どうしてかわたしは、<line-break>消えていません！<line-break>今、こうして、ここにいます。<end>"
EN: "I wish my soul had been taken then too, so I could have vanished with everyone else... But... for some reason, I didn't vanish! I'm still here. I'm alive!<end>"
KO: "차라리 그때 나도 영혼을 빼앗겨 사라졌으면 좋았을 텐데…. 하지만, 하지만 어째서인지 저는 사라지지 않았어요! 지금 이렇게 여기에 있어요.<end>"

## 1340358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです、<value:$28>さん！<line-break>わたしを、みんなと同じところへ！<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>! Send me where everyone else has gone!<end>"
KO: "부탁이에요, <value:$28> 씨! 저를 모두와 같은 곳으로 보내 주세요!<end>"

## 1340359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしのせいでみんなが　　　　<line-break>消えてしまった…。<line-break><line-break>けれど、わたしだけは、なぜか、<line-break>今もここに生きているんです！<end>"
EN: "Everyone vanished because of me... And yet, for some reason, I'm the only one still alive!<end>"
KO: "내 탓에 모두 사라져 버렸어…. 그런데 나만은 왜인지 지금도 여기 살아 있어요!<end>"

## 1340360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしだけが生き残った…<line-break>そのことに何か意味が<line-break>あるって言うんですか？<line-break>わたしを、みんなと同じところへ！<line-break>許されなくとも、せめて、<line-break>謝りに行かせてください…！<end>"
EN: "I'm the only one who survived... Are you saying that means something? Send me where everyone else has gone! Even if I can never be forgiven, at least let me go and apologize...!<end>"
KO: "나만 살아남았어… 그게 무슨 의미가 있다는 건가요? 저를 모두가 있는 곳으로 보내 주세요! 용서받지 못하더라도 적어도 사과하러 가게 해 주세요…!<end>"

## 1340361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしだけが生きているということ…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>つまりそれは、<line-break>わたしにはやらねばならないことが<line-break>あるということ…？<end>"
EN: "I'm the only one still alive... Does that mean there's something I have to do...?<end>"
KO: "나만 살아 있다는 것…. 그건 곧 내가 해야 할 일이 있다는 뜻…?<end>"

## 1340362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あきらめなければ、道が開ける？<line-break>私の前にも…？<end>"
EN: "If I don't give up, a path will open? Even for me...?<end>"
KO: "포기하지 않으면 길이 열린다고? 내 앞에도…?<end>"

## 1340363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんとうに、<line-break>ありがとう…ございました…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you... truly...<end>"
KO: "정말로… 감사…했습니다….<end>"

## 1340364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…やってみます。<line-break>大変かもしれないけど、きっと、<line-break>みんなのソウルを取り戻してみせます！<end>"
EN: "I'll try. It may be difficult, but I know I'll recover everyone's Souls!<end>"
KO: "저… 해 볼게요. 힘들지도 모르지만 반드시 모두의 소울을 되찾아 보이겠어요!<end>"

## 1340365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、あの、<line-break><value:$28>さんっ。<end>"
EN: "Um... <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…저, 저기, <value:$28> 씨.<end>"

## 1340366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目の前に死ぬ運命の男がいたとする。<line-break>お前さんはその男を予定より<line-break>早く殺すことも、逆にその男を<line-break>救ってやることもできる。<end>"
EN: "Suppose a man stood before you, fated to die. You could kill him before his time, or you could save him instead.<end>"
KO: "눈앞에 죽을 운명의 남자가 있다고 치자. 넌 그 남자를 예정된 때보다 빨리 죽일 수도 있고, 반대로 구할 수도 있어.<end>"

## 1340367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言葉にすると簡単だが…<line-break>その男は敵で剣の達人かもしれない。<line-break>味方でも、義理や人情とかのしがらみで<line-break>死ぬ覚悟をしているのかもしれん。<line-break>どっちにしろ助けるのは不可能に近い。<end>"
EN: "It sounds simple enough, but... he might be an enemy, and a master swordsman at that. Or he might be an ally who's resigned himself to death, bound by duty and ties of affection. Either way, saving him would be all but impossible.<end>"
KO: "말로 하면 간단하지만… 그 남자는 적이고 검의 달인일지도 몰라. 아군이라도 의리나 정 때문에 죽을 각오를 하고 있을 수도 있지. 어느 쪽이든 구해 내는 건 거의 불가능해.<end>"

## 1340368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも、お前さんなら…<line-break>無限のソウルなら…<line-break>そんなことができるかもしれん。<line-break>倒すことや、救うこと、そんな領域を<line-break>遥かに超えた、第三の選択肢すら<line-break>勝ち取ることができるかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "Even so, if it were you... if it were the Infinite Soul... maybe you could do it. Maybe you could even seize a third choice, far beyond defeating him or saving him.<end>"
KO: "그래도 너라면… 무한의 소울이라면… 그런 일이 가능할지도 모른다. 쓰러뜨리는 것과 구하는 것, 그런 영역을 훨씬 넘어선 제3의 선택지마저 쟁취할 수 있을지도 모르지.<end>"

## 1340369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが…<line-break>無限のソウルってことじゃないか？<line-break>んー。言葉にするとズレちまったが<line-break>…ま、そう思ったわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Maybe that's what the Infinite Soul is all about. Hmm. It didn't come out quite right when I put it into words, but... well, that's what I think.<end>"
KO: "그게… 무한의 소울이라는 거 아닐까? 음. 말로 하니까 좀 어긋난 것 같지만… 뭐, 그렇게 생각했어.<end>"

## 1340370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "痛テテテテテテテ！<end>"
EN: "Ow-ow-ow-ow-ow-ow!<end>"
KO: "아야야야야야야!<end>"

## 1340371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…てなわけで、ま<line-break>テキトーにな、テキトーに。<end>"
EN: "So there you have it. Just take it easy. Nice and easy.<end>"
KO: "…그런 거니까, 뭐 적당히 해. 적당히.<end>"

## 1340372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お待ちしておりました。<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "We have been awaiting you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있었습니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1340373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来たのぉ。<line-break>ほんとは来てほしくなかったがのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Good of you to come. Truth be told, I was hoping you wouldn't.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔구먼. 사실 오지 않았으면 했지만 말이야.<end>"

## 1340374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 1340375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー様、そのようなことを<line-break>おっしゃってはいけません。<line-break><line-break>やっとご先祖様のご遺言を<line-break>果たせるかもしれないのですから。<end>"
EN: "Master Hugo, you mustn't say such things. At long last, we may be able to fulfill your ancestor's dying wish.<end>"
KO: "휴고 님, 그런 말씀은 하시면 안 됩니다. 드디어 선조님의 유언을 이룰 수 있을지도 모르니까요.<end>"

## 1340376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とまどわれるのも無理はありません。<line-break>私の話をお聞きください。<end>"
EN: "Your confusion is understandable. Please, hear me out.<end>"
KO: "당황하시는 것도 무리는 아닙니다. 제 이야기를 들어 주세요.<end>"

## 1340377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー家の伝承に、こうあります。<end>"
EN: "The Hugo family tradition tells us this:<end>"
KO: "휴고 가문의 전승에는 이렇게 적혀 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340378
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "『力・英知・博愛心、<line-break>すべてを備えし者に、<line-break>我が遺品ストラスエッジを与えよ』<end>"
EN: "“Unto one possessed of strength, wisdom, and benevolence, bestow Stra's Edge, the keepsake I leave behind.”<end>"
KO: "『힘·지혜·박애심, 모든 것을 갖춘 자에게 나의 유품 스트라스 엣지를 주어라』<end>"

## 1340379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この伝承を遺されたのは、<line-break>フゴー様のご先祖、ストラ＝フゴー様。<line-break> <line-break>そしてストラスエッジもやはり<line-break>ストラ様の遺された<line-break>フゴー家の家宝たる短剣でございます。<end>"
EN: "These words were left by Master Hugo's ancestor, Stra-Hugo. Stra's Edge is likewise a dagger left by Master Stra, and a treasured heirloom of the Hugo family.<end>"
KO: "이 전승을 남긴 분은 휴고 님의 조상, 스트라=휴고 님입니다. 그리고 스트라스 엣지도 역시 스트라 님이 남긴 휴고 가문의 가보인 단검입니다.<end>"

## 1340380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしのご先祖様の名を冠した宝刀。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ストラスエッジは、<line-break>遺言とともに実はわしが<line-break>預かっておったんじゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "A treasured blade bearing my ancestor's name. The truth is, Stra's Edge has been in my keeping all this time, along with the will.<end>"
KO: "내 조상의 이름을 딴 보도, 스트라스 엣지는 유언과 함께 사실 내가 맡고 있었지.<end>"

## 1340381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちは、あなた様を<line-break>この遺言に値する人物と<line-break>見込んだのでございます。<end>"
EN: "We believe you are worthy of this bequest.<end>"
KO: "저희는 당신을 이 유언에 걸맞은 인물이라고 판단한 것입니다.<end>"

## 1340382
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たくさん珍しい品を<line-break>持ってきてくれたのぉ。<end>"
EN: "You've brought us a great many rare items.<end>"
KO: "희귀한 물건을 많이 가져왔구나.<end>"

## 1340383
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは、私たちに<line-break>英知を示してくれました。<end>"
EN: "You have shown us your wisdom.<end>"
KO: "당신은 우리에게 지혜를 보여 주었습니다.<end>"

## 1340384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのタルテュバを<line-break>助けようとしたのぉ。<end>"
EN: "You tried to save Tartuba.<end>"
KO: "그 타르튜바를 구하려고 한 거야.<end>"

## 1340385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、博愛心も。<end>"
EN: "And in doing so, you showed benevolence.<end>"
KO: "그리고 박애심도.<end>"

## 1340386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、残りの力については、<line-break>ここで示していただきます。<line-break>私たちと戦って、勝つことで。<end>"
EN: "That leaves strength, which you will demonstrate here by fighting us―and winning.<end>"
KO: "그리고 나머지 힘은 여기서 보여 주십시오. 우리와 싸워 이기는 것으로.<end>"

## 1340387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうわけで勝負じゃのぉ。<line-break>あれは、いい短剣じゃからのぉ。<line-break>ほんとは、渡したくないんじゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "So, a contest it is. That's a fine dagger, you know. Truth be told, I don't want to give it up.<end>"
KO: "그런 이유로 승부다. 저건 좋은 단검이거든. 사실 넘겨주고 싶지 않아.<end>"

## 1340388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 1340389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃから、悪いが、<line-break>ここで倒しちゃおうかと<line-break>思っとるんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "So, sorry about this, but I think we'll defeat you here.<end>"
KO: "그래서 미안하지만 여기서 쓰러뜨릴까 생각 중이야.<end>"

## 1340390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340395

## 1340399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様。<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Please, go on through.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님이시군요. 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 1340401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>ロストール復興のための組織を<line-break>作ったのです。<line-break>今回の事件でこの街も<line-break>ずいぶん傷つきました。<line-break>みなのためにできることはないかと…<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. I've founded an organization to help rebuild Rostorl. This city suffered greatly in the recent incident as well, and I wanted to see if there was anything I could do for everyone...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 님. 로스톨 복구를 위한 조직을 만들었어요. 이번 사건으로 이 도시도 많이 다쳤습니다. 모두를 위해 할 수 있는 일이 없을까 해서….<end>"

## 1340402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私財を投じて運営していますが<line-break>なにぶん大きな街のこと、<line-break>資金がなかなか追いつきません。<line-break>いたらない点も多いですが、<line-break>困っているロストールの人たちのため、<line-break>がんばろうと思います。<end>"
EN: "I've been funding it out of my own pocket, but in a city this large, the funds simply can't keep up. We still have a long way to go, but I intend to do my best for the people of Rostorl who are in need.<end>"
KO: "사재를 털어 운영하고 있지만 워낙 큰 도시라 자금이 좀처럼 따라주지 않습니다. 부족한 점도 많지만 곤란한 로스톨 사람들을 위해 힘내려고 합니다.<end>"

## 1340403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたらない点も多いですが、<line-break>困っているロストールの人たちのため、<line-break>がんばろうと思います。<end>"
EN: "I still have much to learn, but I intend to do my best for the people of Rostorl who are suffering.<end>"
KO: "부족한 점도 많지만 어려움에 처한 로스톨 사람들을 위해 열심히 해 보려고 합니다.<end>"

## 1340404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>私もここで、ロストール復興のために<line-break>働かせていただいております。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I too have been given the opportunity to work here toward Rostorl's recovery.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 저도 여기서 로스톨 부흥을 위해 일하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、リューガ家の執事を務めていた者で<line-break>セバスチャンと申します。<line-break>以後、お見知り置きを。<end>"
EN: "My name is Sebastian. I formerly served as butler to the Ryuga family. I hope you will remember me.<end>"
KO: "저는 류가 가문의 집사를 맡았던 세바스찬이라고 합니다. 앞으로 기억해 주십시오.<end>"

## 1340406
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 1340407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、父の様子がおかしいのです。<end>"
EN: "My father has been acting strangely of late.<end>"
KO: "요즘 아버지 상태가 이상합니다.<end>"

## 1340408
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もともと、人との接触を<line-break>好まれない方でしたが…。<line-break> <line-break>近頃は誰ともお会いになろうと<line-break>なさりません。<end>"
EN: "He never cared much for company to begin with... but lately, he refuses to see anyone at all.<end>"
KO: "원래 사람과 접촉하는 걸 좋아하지 않는 분이셨지만…. 요즘은 누구도 만나려 하지 않으십니다.<end>"

## 1340409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらに、衛兵たちの間では、<line-break>王宮内にうろつく怪しい影を目撃する<line-break>というような噂も立っているらしく、<line-break>口さがない者は、<line-break>母の亡霊の仕業だと…。<end>"
EN: "What's more, there are apparently rumors among the guards about sightings of a strange shadow roaming the palace. Some of the more malicious gossips say it's the work of my mother's ghost...<end>"
KO: "게다가 위병들 사이에서는 왕궁 안을 떠도는 수상한 그림자를 봤다는 소문도 도는 모양이고, 입이 가벼운 자들은 어머니의 망령 짓이라고…<end>"

## 1340410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "口さがない者は母の亡霊の仕業だと…<end>"
EN: "Some of the more malicious gossips say it's the work of my mother's ghost...<end>"
KO: "입이 험한 자들은 어머니의 망령 짓이라고…<end>"

## 1340411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ…すみません。<line-break>せっかく来ていただいたのに<line-break>こんな話を…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I'm sorry. You came to see me, and here I am talking about such things...<end>"
KO: "아… 죄송합니다. 모처럼 와주셨는데 이런 이야기를…<end>"

## 1340412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すみません。<line-break>せっかく来ていただいたのに…<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. You came all this way, and...<end>"
KO: "…죄송합니다. 모처럼 와 주셨는데….<end>"

## 1340413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ…<end>"
EN: "Oh...<end>"
KO: "하아…<end>"

## 1340414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>また、いらしてくださったのですね。<line-break>うれしく思います。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. You've come back. I'm very glad.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 또 와 주셨군요. 기쁩니다.<end>"

## 1340415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、順調…と言いたいのですが…<line-break>残念ながら、<line-break>なかなかそうとも行っていません。<line-break>なにぶん、<line-break>この広いロストールの復興ですから、<line-break>巨額の資金が必要となるのです。<end>"
EN: "I wish I could say things were going smoothly, but unfortunately, they aren't. Rebuilding a place as vast as Rostorl requires an enormous amount of money.<end>"
KO: "네, 순조롭다고 말씀드리고 싶지만…. 안타깝게도 그렇게 잘 풀리진 않습니다. 워낙 넓은 로스톨을 복구하는 일이니 막대한 자금이 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1340416
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（資金援助すべきかな…？）<end>"
EN: "(Should I make a donation...?)<end>"
KO: "(자금 지원을 해야 하나…?)<end>"

## 1340417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "１００００ギアほど援助する<end>"
EN: "Provide 10,000 Gea<end>"
KO: "10000기어 정도 지원한다<end>"

## 1340418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、こんなに<line-break>援助してくださるのですか？<line-break>ありがとうございます！<end>"
EN: "What? You're donating this much? Thank you!<end>"
KO: "어, 이렇게나 지원해 주시는 겁니까? 감사합니다!<end>"

## 1340419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "匿名を使って私たち復興団に<line-break>援助をしてくださる方も<line-break>いらっしゃいます。<line-break>とてもありがたいことです。<end>"
EN: "Some people have even made anonymous donations to our Restoration League. We're very grateful for their support.<end>"
KO: "익명으로 저희 부흥단을 도와주시는 분도 계십니다. 정말 감사한 일입니다.<end>"

## 1340420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…と思ったけど<line-break>　お金が足りないな。）<end>"
EN: "(I was going to, but I don't have enough money.)<end>"
KO: "(…라고 생각했지만 돈이 부족하네.)<end>"

## 1340421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…そうだな。<line-break>　そんな余裕はないな。）<end>"
EN: "(Right. I can't afford that.)<end>"
KO: "(…그래. 그럴 여유는 없겠네.)<end>"

## 1340422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、エルファス様が<line-break>物心両面から、ロストールの人を<line-break>助けてくださっています。<line-break>それでロストールの民衆は<line-break>エルファス様のことを<line-break>聖者ノクサの再来と讃えています。<end>"
EN: "Recently, Lord Elfas has been helping the people of Rostorl, providing both material aid and moral support. The people now hail him as the second coming of Saint Noksa.<end>"
KO: "최근 엘파스 님께서 물심양면으로 로스톨 사람들을 도와주고 계십니다. 그래서 로스톨 민중은 엘파스 님을 성자 녹사의 재림이라 칭송하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様は貧しい人に<line-break>食料を施し、奇跡を起こして<line-break>病を治していますので、<line-break>みなは救世主と讃えています。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas gives food to the poor and works miracles to cure the sick, so everyone hails him as the Savior.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님은 가난한 사람들에게 식량을 나눠 주고 기적을 일으켜 병을 고치시니, 모두 구세주라고 칭송합니다.<end>"

## 1340424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "終末の到来を不安に思う必要など<line-break>ありません。<line-break> <line-break>少しずつ復興していくこの街を<line-break>ご覧ください。<line-break>私めは希望を感じずにいられません。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to fear the coming of the end. Look upon this city, rebuilding itself little by little. I cannot help but feel hope.<end>"
KO: "종말의 도래를 불안해할 필요는 없습니다. 조금씩 부흥해 가는 이 거리를 보십시오. 저는 희망을 느끼지 않을 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1340425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>タルテュバ様に、生命のかけらを<line-break>持ってきてくださったのですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, have you brought Lord Tartuba a Life Fragment?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 타르튜바 님께 생명의 조각을 가져오신 건가요?<end>"

## 1340426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい<end>"
EN: "Yes<end>"
KO: "네<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ<end>"
EN: "No<end>"
KO: "아니요<end>"

## 1340428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、おかげで復興の方も<line-break>軌道に乗ってまいりました。<line-break>人の笑顔はいいものですね。<end>"
EN: "...Is that so? Ah, thankfully, the reconstruction is well under way now. It is good to see people smiling again.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까. 아, 덕분에 부흥도 궤도에 오르고 있습니다. 사람들의 웃는 얼굴은 좋군요.<end>"

## 1340429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様も<line-break>復興にはひとかたならぬ<line-break>お力を割いてくださっています。<line-break>エルファス様は、ロストールだけでなく<line-break>世界中の都市で復興のため働いて、<line-break>神のようにあがめられているのです。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas has also devoted himself tirelessly to the reconstruction. He has been helping cities rebuild not only in Rostorl, but all over the world, and is worshiped like a god.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님도 부흥을 위해 큰 힘을 보태고 계십니다. 엘파스 님은 로스톨뿐 아니라 세계 각지의 도시에서 부흥을 위해 일하며 신처럼 숭배받고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…、<value:$28>様。<line-break>あの…<line-break>ティアナのお願いをきいてください。<line-break>寝室に…来ていただきたいのです…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>. Um... I have a favor to ask. Would you please come to the bedchamber?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이시군요. 저… 티아나의 부탁을 들어 주세요. 침실로… 와 주셨으면 합니다….<end>"

## 1340431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様です。<line-break>倒れていらっしゃったのを<line-break>お運びしたのです。<end>"
EN: "It's Lord Tartuba. He was found collapsed and brought here.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님입니다. 쓰러져 계신 것을 이곳으로 모셔왔습니다.<end>"

## 1340432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。あのとき確かに<line-break>タルテュバ様は怪物になられ、<line-break>退治されました。<line-break>それが、どういうわけか、<line-break>元のお姿で、衰弱しきって<line-break>発見されたのです。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Lord Tartuba did indeed become a monster that day and was slain. Yet somehow, he was later found back in his original form, severely weakened.<end>"
KO: "네. 그때 분명 타르튜바 님은 괴물이 되셨고 퇴치당했습니다. 그런데 어찌 된 일인지 원래 모습으로, 완전히 쇠약해진 채 발견되셨습니다.<end>"

## 1340433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様のお体はボロボロです。<line-break>日頃から大酒を飲まれ、<line-break>不摂生をしていたこともあって…。<line-break>その上、今までどうなさっていたのか。<line-break>とにかく、衰弱がひどくて<line-break>かなり危険な容態です。<end>"
EN: "Lord Tartuba's body is in a terrible state. He was always a heavy drinker and took little care of himself... and there is no telling what happened to him after that. In any case, he is severely weakened, and his condition is critical.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님의 몸은 만신창이입니다. 평소에도 술을 많이 드시고 생활도 불규칙하셨는데…. 게다가 지금까지 어떻게 지내셨는지. 아무튼 쇠약이 심해 상당히 위험한 상태입니다.<end>"

## 1340434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意識も…戻りません。<line-break>いつどうなってもおかしくないのです。<line-break>ですから…。<line-break>タルテュバ様の療治のため、<line-break>生命のかけらを<line-break>分けていただきたいのです。<end>"
EN: "He still has not regained consciousness. He could take a turn for the worse at any moment. And so... I ask that you spare a Life Fragment to treat Lord Tartuba.<end>"
KO: "의식도… 돌아오지 않습니다. 언제 어떻게 되어도 이상하지 않아요. 그래서… 타르튜바 님의 치료를 위해 생명의 조각을 나눠 주셨으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1340435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様、<line-break>確かにタルテュバ様は<line-break>善人ではありませんでした。<line-break>ですが、こうなってしまっては<line-break>もう、悪人も善人もありません。<line-break>違うでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... it is true that Lord Tartuba was not a good man. But now that he has come to this, what does it matter whether he was good or wicked? Am I wrong?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님, 타르튜바 님이 선인은 아니셨던 건 사실입니다. 하지만 이렇게 된 이상 악인도 선인도 없습니다. 그렇지 않습니까?<end>"

## 1340436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>どうか、お願いします…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please... I beg you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 부디 부탁드립니다….<end>"

## 1340437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生命のかけらをタルテュバに与える<end>"
EN: "Give the Life Fragment to Tartuba<end>"
KO: "생명의 조각을 타르튜바에게 준다<end>"

## 1340438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生命のかけらをタルテュバに与えない<end>"
EN: "Do not give the Life Fragment to Tartuba<end>"
KO: "생명의 조각을 타르튜바에게 주지 않는다<end>"

## 1340439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>タルテュバに<line-break>生命のかけらを与えた。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> gave Tartuba a Life Fragment.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 타르튜바에게 생명의 조각을 주었다.<end>"

## 1340440
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…でも、衰弱がひどい。<line-break>　生命のかけらを与えても<line-break>　治るだろうか…？）<end>"
EN: "(But he's so weak... Will even a Life Fragment be enough to heal him?)<end>"
KO: "(…하지만 쇠약이 너무 심해. 생명의 조각을 줘도 나을 수 있을까…?)<end>"

## 1340441
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…待て。<end>"
EN: "...Wait.<end>"
KO: "…기다려.<end>"

## 1340442
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺みたいな奴を<line-break>なぜ、助ける？<end>"
EN: "Why... would you save a man like me?<end>"
KO: "…나 같은 놈을 왜 돕는 거지?<end>"

## 1340443
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺で…さえ、俺自身で…さえ、<line-break>俺のことが…好きに…なれなかった。<line-break>だのに…なぜ…？　ゲホッ、ゲホッ！<end>"
EN: "Even I... even I couldn't bring myself to like me. So why...? Cough! Cough!<end>"
KO: "…나조차… 나 자신조차… 나를 좋아할 수 없었어. 그런데… 왜…? 콜록, 콜록!<end>"

## 1340444
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ…あいにくだな。<line-break>まだ…もうしばらくは…くたばらん。<line-break>もう少しは…持つ。フフ…。<end>"
EN: "Too bad... I'm not going to drop dead just yet. I've got a little longer in me. Heh...<end>"
KO: "…아… 유감이군. 아직… 한동안은… 죽지 않아. 조금은 더… 버틸 수 있어. 후후….<end>"

## 1340445
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き…気休めか…？<line-break>フン…、俺はもう貴族様では…ない。<line-break>へつらう…必要は…ゲホッ！　ゲホッ！<end>"
EN: "J-Just trying to make me feel better...? Hmph... I'm no fine nobleman anymore. No need... to flatter me... Cough! Cough!<end>"
KO: "기… 기분 풀어 주려는 말인가…? 흥… 나는 이제 귀족 나리도… 아니야. 아첨할… 필요는… 콜록! 콜록!<end>"

## 1340446
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なぜだ？　なぜ？<line-break>打算も見返りもなしにやさしくなれる？<line-break>どうして、…俺みたいな…奴に？　<line-break>ティアナ…あ、あんたもだ。<end>"
EN: "Why? Why would you show kindness without some scheme or reward in mind? Why... to someone like me? Tiana... y-you too.<end>"
KO: "…왜지? 왜? 계산도 대가도 없이 다정할 수 있는 거지? 어째서… 나 같은 놈에게? 티아나… 너도 그래.<end>"

## 1340447
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺は…最低な…男だ。<line-break>いとこたちの…ような才もない。<line-break>愛されるところなど…何も…ない。<end>"
EN: "I'm... the lowest sort of man. I have none of my cousins' gifts. There is nothing in me... anyone could love.<end>"
KO: "…나는… 최악의… 남자다. 사촌들… 같은 재능도 없어. 사랑받을 만한 건… 아무것도… 없다.<end>"

## 1340448
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと…。<end>"
EN: "That isn't...<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가….<end>"

## 1340449
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、慰めはいい。<line-break>俺だって自分のことぐらいはわかる。<end>"
EN: "N-No, spare me the consolation. I know myself well enough.<end>"
KO: "위, 위로는 됐어. 나도 내 처지 정도는 알아.<end>"

## 1340450
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人より優れたところなど…家柄だけ。<line-break>俺は、だからその家柄にだけすがって<line-break>かろうじて自尊心を維持していた。<line-break>はたから見れば、イヤな貴族の典型だ。<line-break>俺はそれがわかっていたから<line-break>いっそう荒れずにいられなかった。<end>"
EN: "The only thing I had over anyone else... was my lineage. So I clung to that alone, barely keeping my pride intact. To anyone looking on, I was the very picture of an insufferable nobleman. And because I knew it, I only became more bitter and unruly.<end>"
KO: "남보다 나은 건… 가문뿐이었어. 그래서 난 그 가문에만 매달려 간신히 자존심을 지켰지. 남이 보면 싫은 귀족의 전형이야. 나도 그걸 알고 있었기에 더 거칠어질 수밖에 없었어.<end>"

## 1340451
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "酒におぼれ…弱いものをいじめ…<line-break>乱暴を働く…皆、俺を嫌いぬいていた。<line-break>そんな俺を…誰よりも俺が憎んでいた。<line-break>…意外か？　俺に内省力があったなど…<line-break>誰でもない自分のことだ、<line-break>気にしない…わけがない。<end>"
EN: "I drowned myself in drink... bullied the weak... lashed out violently. Everyone despised me. And no one hated me more than I hated myself. Surprised? That I was capable of that much self-reflection...? It was me, after all. How could I not care?<end>"
KO: "술에 빠지고… 약한 자를 괴롭히고… 난폭하게 굴고… 모두가 나를 싫어했다. 그런 나를… 누구보다 내가 미워했다. …의외인가? 나에게 자성할 힘이 있었다는 게…. 누구도 아닌 내 일이니 신경 쓰지 않을 수가 없지.<end>"

## 1340452
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だが、…俺はそれを他人のせいにして<line-break>ただ、荒れた…。お前の師匠のオッシは<line-break>かつてエリスにたてついた貴族なんだ。<line-break>俺は奴が、のうのうと平民街で幸せに<line-break>暮らしているのを見て腹を立てていた。<line-break>な…なんで、奴は奴は奴は…って…。<end>"
EN: "But... I blamed everyone else and simply raged at the world. Your master, Ossi, was once a noble who defied Eris. It infuriated me to see him living happily and without a care in the commoners' quarter. Why... why should he, why should he, why should he...? That was all I could think.<end>"
KO: "…하지만 난 그걸 남 탓으로 돌리고 그저 거칠어졌어…. 네 스승 오시는 예전에 에리스에게 맞선 귀족이야. 난 그가 아무렇지도 않게 평민가에서 행복하게 사는 걸 보며 화가 났지. 왜… 왜 저놈은, 저놈은… 하면서….<end>"

## 1340453
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺は、俺が大事にしている剣を<line-break>盗まれて、…カッと来て<line-break>平民街にモンスターを…放した…。<line-break>お前も、…そこにいたろう？<line-break>お前は俺が憎く…ないわけはない…。<line-break>…だのに、俺を…助ける。<end>"
EN: "Someone stole a sword I treasured... and in my rage, I unleashed monsters on the commoners' quarter. You were there too... weren't you? There's no way you don't hate me... Yet you save me.<end>"
KO: "…나는 내가 소중히 여기던 검을 도둑맞고… 욱해서 평민가에 몬스터를… 풀었다…. 너도… 거기 있었지? 네가 나를 미워하지 않을 리 없는데…. …그런데도 날… 구하는군.<end>"

## 1340454
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前…農民なんだ…ろ…？<line-break>レ、レムオンの領地で暴れ回ってた…。<line-break>　<line-break>俺は…お前のことを…貴族だから嫌い、<line-break>貴族だから…憎むっていう…奴だと…<line-break>思ってた…。だのに俺を…助ける…。<end>"
EN: "You're... a peasant, aren't you...? You went on a rampage through Lemghon's lands... I thought you were the sort who hated me just because I was a noble... who despised me for being one. Yet you save me...<end>"
KO: "너… 농민이잖아…? 레, 레무온의 영지에서 날뛰던…. 나는… 네가 나를… 귀족이라서 싫어하고, 귀족이라서… 미워하는… 그런 녀석인 줄 알았어…. 그런데 나를… 구해 주다니….<end>"

## 1340455
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ…にだって<line-break>俺はひどいことをした。<line-break>覚えているだろう？　<line-break>俺には助けられるだけの価値はない…<line-break>…だのに、なぜ…<line-break>あんたらは、俺を助ける？<end>"
EN: "I treated Tiana horribly too. You remember, don't you? I am not worth saving... So why... why would any of you save me?<end>"
KO: "티아나에게도 난 끔찍한 짓을 했어. 기억하지? 난 도움받을 가치가 없어… 그런데도 왜… 너희는 날 구하는 거지?<end>"

## 1340456
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は何もかも…決めてかかっていた。<line-break>憎けりゃ、殺したくなる。<line-break>愛されない奴は憎まれるしかない。<line-break>…そう、思っていた。<line-break>だが、すべては…人の心にあるんだな。<line-break>人の心が状況を変えるのだな。<end>"
EN: "I'd made up my mind about everything. Hate someone, and you want to kill. Anyone unloved can only be hated. That was what I believed. But it all comes down to the human heart, doesn't it? The heart can change circumstances.<end>"
KO: "난 모든 걸… 미리 단정하고 있었어. 미우면 죽이고 싶어지고, 사랑받지 못한 자는 미움받을 수밖에 없다고…. 그렇게 생각했지. 하지만 모든 건… 사람의 마음에 있는 거였구나. 사람의 마음이 상황을 바꾸는 거구나.<end>"

## 1340457
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も…、変われたのかもしれない。<line-break>もっと早く…このことに気づいていれば<line-break>俺が…お前の…ゲホッゴホッ！<line-break>き、気づいたときには時間切れか…。<line-break>ふ、ふふ…、俺らしい…。<end>"
EN: "Maybe I could have changed too. If only I'd realized this sooner, I might have become... your... Cough! Cough! By the time I understand, I'm out of time... Heh... just like me.<end>"
KO: "나도… 변할 수 있었던 걸지도 몰라. 좀 더 일찍… 이걸 깨달았더라면 내가… 네… 콜록, 콜록! 깨달았을 때는 이미 늦은 건가…. 후, 후후… 나답네….<end>"

## 1340458
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あばよ。<line-break>…ロクな人生じゃなかった…が…<line-break>おかげで…少しは救われ…た…。<end>"
EN: "So long... It wasn't much of a life... but thanks to you... I found some measure of salvation...<end>"
KO: "…잘 있어. …변변찮은 인생이었…지만… 덕분에… 조금은 구원받았…어….<end>"

## 1340459
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様…。<end>"
EN: "Lord Tartuba...<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님….<end>"

## 1340460
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の死に慣れてしまったのでしょうか。<line-break>幼いときから一緒にいた方の死なのに<line-break>感慨がありません…<line-break>でも、安らかなお顔でしたね。<end>"
EN: "Have I grown accustomed to death? Someone who had been with me since childhood is dead, yet I feel nothing... Still, his face looked peaceful.<end>"
KO: "사람의 죽음에 익숙해져 버린 걸까요. 어릴 때부터 함께한 분의 죽음인데도 아무 감정이 없어요…. 그래도 편안한 얼굴이셨네요.<end>"

## 1340461
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様も思えば、<line-break>おかわいそうな方です。<line-break>　<line-break>何かにつけて、いとこの<line-break>レムオン様、エスト様に比べられ、<line-break>あげつらわれてきたのですから。<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, Lord Tartuba was a pitiable man. He was forever being compared with his cousins, Lord Lemghon and Lord Est, and criticized for falling short.<end>"
KO: "생각해보면 타르튜바 님도 참 가엾은 분입니다. 사사건건 사촌인 레무온 님, 에스트 님과 비교당하고 비난받아왔으니까요.<end>"

## 1340462
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様も貴族に生まれなければ…<line-break>このような悲しいことには<line-break>ならなかったのかもしれません<end>"
EN: "Had Lord Tartuba not been born a noble... perhaps none of this would have ended so sadly.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님도 귀족으로 태어나지 않았다면… 이렇게 슬픈 일은 없었을지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1340463
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…<line-break>少しだけ、一人にさせてください<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... please, let me be alone for a little while.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 잠시만 혼자 있게 해 주세요.<end>"

## 1340464
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様の身にいったい何が…。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What could have happened to Lord Lemghon...?<end>"
KO: "레무온 님께 대체 무슨 일이….<end>"

## 1340465
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…生命のかけらが手元にない…<line-break>　どうにかして手に入れなければ…）<end>"
EN: "(I don't have a Life Fragment... I need to get one somehow...)<end>"
KO: "(…생명의 조각이 수중에 없어… 어떻게든 구해야 해…)<end>"

## 1340466
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님?<end>"

## 1340467
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様。<line-break>ようこそ、いらっしゃいました。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Welcome.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 어서 오십시오.<end>"

## 1340468
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様も<line-break>復興にはひとかたならぬ<line-break>お力を割いてくださっています。<line-break>エルファス様は、ロストールだけでなく<line-break>世界中の都市で復興のため働いて、<line-break>神のようにあがめられているのです。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas has also devoted himself tirelessly to the rebuilding. He is helping cities recover not only in Rostorl, but all over the world, and people have come to worship him like a god.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님도 부흥을 위해 큰 힘을 보태고 계십니다. 엘파스 님은 로스톨뿐 아니라 세계 각지의 도시에서 부흥을 위해 일하며 신처럼 숭배받고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340469
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール復興のため、<line-break>身分の上下のへだてなく、<line-break>皆が働き、それを喜んでおります。<line-break>レムオン様にも<line-break>たまにはここにいらっしゃって<line-break>働かれるよう、おっしゃってください。<end>"
EN: "Highborn and lowborn alike are working to rebuild Rostorl, and all are glad to do so. Please tell Lord Lemghon that he ought to come here and lend a hand once in a while.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 부흥을 위해 신분의 높고 낮음을 가리지 않고 모두가 일하며 기뻐하고 있습니다. 레무온 님께도 가끔 이곳에 오셔서 일하시라고 말씀해 주세요.<end>"

## 1340470
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールは少しずつ復興し、<line-break>身分の上下のへだてなく、<line-break>それを喜んでおります。<line-break>やはり、レムオン様にも、<line-break>この場にいていただきたかった…。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl is slowly recovering, and highborn and lowborn alike rejoice to see it. I only wish Lord Lemghon could have been here with us...<end>"
KO: "로스톨은 조금씩 복구되고 신분의 높고 낮음 없이 모두 기뻐하고 있습니다. 역시 레무온 님도 이 자리에 계셨으면 했는데…<end>"

## 1340471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>ようこそ、いらっしゃいました。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, welcome. I'm so glad you've come.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 어서 오십시오.<end>"

## 1340472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<line-break>私がここにいるのが不思議ですか？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Are you surprised to find me here?<end>"
KO: "후후후. 제가 여기 있는 게 이상한가요?<end>"

## 1340473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は確かに王位を受け継ぎました。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、今、この国に必要なのは、<line-break>王位に座って指図する人ではなくて、<line-break>民と共に国を再建していく人なのです。<end>"
EN: "It is true that I inherited the throne. But what this country needs now is not someone who sits upon it and gives orders. It needs someone who will rebuild the country alongside its people.<end>"
KO: "저는 분명 왕위를 이어받았습니다. 하지만 지금 이 나라에 필요한 건 왕좌에 앉아 명령하는 사람이 아니라 백성과 함께 나라를 다시 세워 갈 사람입니다.<end>"

## 1340474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>これからも、復興団の皆さんと一緒に<line-break>頑張っていきます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I will continue doing all I can alongside everyone in the Restoration League.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 앞으로도 복구단 여러분과 함께 힘내겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あと、お父様の遺品なのですが…<line-break>これを受け取っていただきたいのです。<end>"
EN: "There is one more thing. This is one of my late father's belongings, and I'd like you to have it.<end>"
KO: "그리고 아버님의 유품인데요… 이것을 받아주셨으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1340476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様に<line-break>使っていただいた方がお父様も<line-break>喜ぶと思うのです。<end>"
EN: "I think Father would be glad to know you were using it, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님이 써 주시는 편이 아버님도 기뻐하실 것 같아요.<end>"

## 1340477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女王として…<line-break>この国に生きる民の<line-break>一人として…<line-break>これからも、頑張っていこうと<line-break>思います。<end>"
EN: "As queen, and as one of the people of this country, I mean to keep doing all I can.<end>"
KO: "여왕으로서… 이 나라에서 살아가는 한 사람으로서… 앞으로도 최선을 다하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様。<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Please, go on through.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님이시군요. 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 1340479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>ロストール復興のための組織を<line-break>作ったのです。<line-break>今回の事件でこの街も<line-break>ずいぶん傷を負いました<line-break>みなのためにできることはないかと…<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. I've formed an organization to help rebuild Rostorl. This city was badly damaged in the recent incident, and I wanted to see if there was anything I could do for everyone...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 님, 로스톨 부흥을 위한 조직을 만들었습니다. 이번 사건으로 이 도시도 큰 상처를 입었으니 모두를 위해 할 수 있는 일이 없을까 해서요…<end>"

## 1340480
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたらない点も多いですが、<line-break>困っているロストールの人たちのため、<line-break>がんばろうと思います。<end>"
EN: "I know I still have much to learn, but I mean to do my best for the people of Rostorl who are in need.<end>"
KO: "부족한 점도 많지만 어려움에 처한 로스톨 사람들을 위해 열심히 해 보려고 합니다.<end>"

## 1340481
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたらない点も多いですが、<line-break>困っているロストールの人たちのため、<line-break>がんばろうと思います。<end>"
EN: "I still have much to learn, but I intend to do my best for the people of Rostorl who are suffering.<end>"
KO: "부족한 점도 많지만 어려움에 처한 로스톨 사람들을 위해 열심히 해 보려고 합니다.<end>"

## 1340482
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>私もここで、ロストール復興のために<line-break>働かせていただいております。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I too have been given the opportunity to work here toward Rostorl's recovery.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 저도 여기서 로스톨 부흥을 위해 일하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340483
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、リューガ家の執事を務めていた者で<line-break>セバスチャンと申します。<line-break>以後、お見知り置きを。<end>"
EN: "My name is Sebastian. I formerly served as butler to the Ryuga family. I hope you will remember me.<end>"
KO: "저는 류가 가문의 집사를 맡았던 세바스찬이라고 합니다. 앞으로 기억해 주십시오.<end>"

## 1340484
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3む。お、お、お前も<line-break>アレをねらっているゴブか？<line-break>　<line-break>フゴーのところにある<line-break>アレを手に入れるのは<line-break>このガントだゴブ！<end>お前には負けないゴブよ！<line-break>たとえ、聖杯がこの手になくっても…<line-break> <line-break>た、たとえばの話ゴブよ！<end>道具の力に頼る時期は終わったゴブ！<line-break>ゴブゴブ団は実力で前進するゴブ！<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Hmph. A-are you after that thing too, gob? Gant will be the one to claim the thing Hugo has, gob!<end>I won't lose to you, gob! Even without the Grail in my hands... Th-that was only an example, gob!<end>The time for relying on tools is over, gob! The Gob-Gob Gang will forge ahead by its own strength, gob!<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3으, 너, 너도 그걸 노리는 고브인가? 휴고에게 있는 그걸 손에 넣는 건 이 간트다 고브!<end>너한테는 지지 않는다 고브! 설령 성배가 이 손에 없어도… 그, 그냥 예를 든 거다 고브!<end>도구의 힘에 의지하던 시대는 끝났다 고브! 고브고브단은 실력으로 전진한다 고브!<end>"

## 1340485
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3団長はエライでゴブ。<line-break>ロストールの人たちのために<line-break>ガンバル姿には感動するゴブ！<line-break>こうやって、みんなでみんなで<line-break>しやあせになるゴブよ！<end>自分たちゴブゴブ団も<line-break>一生懸命働いているから<line-break>復興団も、１００人力ゴブ！<end>聖杯がなくなってしまったのは、<line-break>誰が悪いせいでもないゴブ。<line-break> <line-break>きっと、ゴブゴブ団には<line-break>もう必要がなくなっただけゴブ。<line-break>団長を責めないでやってほしいゴブよ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3The boss is great, gob. Seeing the boss work so hard for the people of Rostorl is inspiring, gob! This way, everyone can be happy together, gob!<end>The Gob-Gob Gang is working as hard as it can too, so the Restoration League has the strength of a hundred, gob!<end>It isn't anyone's fault that the Grail is gone, gob. Maybe the Gob-Gob Gang just doesn't need it anymore, gob. Please don't blame the boss, gob.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3단장은 훌륭하다 고브. 로스톨 사람들을 위해 힘내는 모습에 감동한다 고브! 이렇게 모두가 모두 행복해지는 거다 고브!<end>우리 고브고브단도 열심히 일하고 있으니 복구단도 백 명 몫이다 고브!<end>성배가 없어진 건 누구 잘못도 아니다 고브. 분명 고브고브단에게 이제 필요 없어졌을 뿐이다 고브. 단장을 탓하지 말아 줬으면 한다 고브.<end>"

## 1340486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3腹減ったゴブ。<end>なんか食いたいゴブ。<end>聖杯がなくなっても、<line-break>メシのうまさは変わらないゴブ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3I'm hungry, gob.<end>I want something to eat, gob.<end>Even without the Grail, food tastes just as good, gob.<end>"

## 1340489
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml

## 1340490

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "제 것과는 다릅니다. 티아나 님의 것과도….<end>"

## 1340491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです。私やティアナ様のように<line-break>闇に捕らわれようとしている方が<line-break>いるのであれば、助けてほしいのです。<end>"
EN: "Please. If anyone else is about to be claimed by darkness, as Tiana and I were, I want you to help.<end>"
KO: "부탁드립니다. 저나 티아나 님처럼 어둠에 붙잡히려는 분이 있다면 구해 주세요.<end>"

## 1340495
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "順調とは言いかねます…。<line-break>セバスチャンに助けてもらっていますが<line-break>私が慣れないせいもあって…<end>"
EN: "I can't say it's going smoothly. Sebastian has been helping me, but I'm still not used to the work...<end>"
KO: "순조롭다고 하긴 어렵습니다…. 세바스찬의 도움을 받고 있지만 제가 익숙하지 않아서….<end>"

## 1340496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340497
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それでも、なかなか…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お金が足りずに、<line-break>手が回らないところが<line-break>たくさん出てきてしまっています…。<end>"
EN: "But even with that, we're still struggling... We don't have enough money, and more and more things are being left undone.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그래도 좀처럼…. 돈이 부족해서 손이 미치지 않는 곳이 많이 생기고 있어요….<end>"

## 1340498
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（資金援助すべきかな…？）<end>"
EN: "(Maybe I should make a donation...)<end>"
KO: "(자금 지원을 해야 하나…?)<end>"

## 1340499
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Oh... Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1340500
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お姫様、こんなケチに<line-break>お礼を言っちゃだめゴブ！<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>！<line-break>まったく、同じ冒険者として<line-break>恥ずかしいゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "Princess, don't thank a cheapskate like this, gob! <value:$28>! As a fellow adventurer, I'm ashamed of you, gob!<end>"
KO: "공주님, 이런 구두쇠에게 감사하면 안 돼 고브! <value:$28>! 정말 같은 모험자로서 부끄럽다 고브!<end>"

## 1340501
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなに援助していただいて、<line-break>よろしいのですか？<line-break>ありがとうございます！<end>"
EN: "Are you sure it's all right for you to give us this much? Thank you!<end>"
KO: "이렇게까지 지원해 주셔도, 정말 괜찮은가요? 감사합니다!<end>"

## 1340502
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなに援助して<line-break>大丈夫なんでゴブか！？<line-break> <line-break>ただのカッコつけゴブ！<line-break>そ、それしきでは、<line-break>博愛心は認められないゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "A-are you sure you can afford to give that much, gob?! You're just showing off, gob! Th-that paltry sum isn't enough to prove your charitable spirit, gob!<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇게 지원해도 괜찮은 고브!? 그냥 멋부리는 거 고브! 그 정도로는 박애심을 인정할 수 없는 고브!<end>"

## 1340503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、匿名で資金を援助して<line-break>くださる方もいらっしゃるんです。<line-break>ありがたいことです。<end>"
EN: "We've also been receiving anonymous financial support lately. We're very grateful.<end>"
KO: "요즘은 익명으로 자금을 지원해 주시는 분도 계십니다. 감사한 일이죠.<end>"

## 1340504

## 1340505

## 1340506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんか、今、<line-break>セコ～い計算<line-break>しなかったでゴブか？<end>"
EN: "Did you just do some stingy little calculation in your head, gob?<end>"
KO: "…뭔가 방금 치사한 계산을 한 거 아닌가 고브?<end>"

## 1340507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメゴブ！　ダメゴブ！<line-break>そんなことでは<line-break>大物になれないでゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "No, gob! No, gob! You'll never amount to anything like that, gob!<end>"
KO: "안 돼 고브! 안 돼 고브! 그러고서는 거물이 될 수 없다 고브!<end>"

## 1340508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、救世主エルファス様が<line-break>物心両面から、ロストールの人を<line-break>助けてくださっています。<line-break>でも…<end>"
EN: "Lately, Elfas the Savior has been giving the people of Rostorl both material aid and moral support. But...<end>"
KO: "요즘 구세주 엘파스 님이 물심양면으로 로스톨 사람들을 돕고 계십니다. 하지만….<end>"

## 1340509
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様は貧しい人に<line-break>食料を施し、奇跡を起こして<line-break>病を治していますので、<line-break>みな、救世主と讃えています。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas gives food to the poor and works miracles to cure the sick, so everyone hails him as the Savior.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님은 가난한 사람들에게 식량을 나눠 주고 기적을 일으켜 병을 고치시니, 모두 구세주라고 칭송합니다.<end>"
