## 1340519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "終末の到来を不安に思う必要など<line-break>ありません。<line-break> <line-break>少しずつ復興していくこの街を<line-break>ご覧ください。<line-break>私めは希望を感じずにいられません。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to fear the coming of the end. Look at this city, rebuilding little by little. I cannot help but feel hopeful.<end>"
KO: "종말의 도래를 불안해할 필요는 없습니다. 조금씩 부흥해 가는 이 거리를 보십시오. 저는 희망을 느끼지 않을 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1340520
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "『力・英知・博愛心、<line-break>　すべてを備えし者に、<line-break>　我が遺品ストラスエッジを与えよ』<end>"
EN: "“To one who possesses strength, wisdom, and benevolence, bestow Stra's Edge, the relic I leave behind.”<end>"
KO: "『힘·지혜·박애심, 모든 것을 갖춘 자에게 나의 유품 스트라스 엣지를 주어라』<end>"

## 1340521
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハッ！<end>"
EN: "Hah!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1340522
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いちゃダメゴブ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>別にフゴーの宝物がほしくて<line-break>復興に手を貸しているんじゃ<line-break>ないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Don't listen, gob! I'm not helping with the rebuilding because I want Hugo's treasure, gob!<end>"
KO: "들으면 안 된다 고브! 휴고의 보물이 탐나서 부흥을 돕는 게 아니다 고브!<end>"

## 1340523
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3団長はエライでゴブ。<line-break>ロストールの人たちのために<line-break>ガンバル姿には感動するゴブ！<end>こうやって、みんなでみんなで<line-break>しやあせになるゴブ！<end>自分たちゴブゴブ団も<line-break>一生懸命働いているから<line-break>復興団も、１００人力ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3The boss is great, gob. Seeing the boss work so hard for the people of Rostorl is inspiring, gob!<end>This way, everyone can be happy together, gob!<end>The Gob-Gob Gang is working as hard as it can too, so the Restoration League has the strength of a hundred, gob!<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3단장님은 훌륭하다 고브. 로스톨 사람들을 위해 힘내는 모습에 감동한다 고브!<end>이렇게 모두 함께 모두 함께 행복해지는 거다 고브!<end>우리 고브고브단도 열심히 일하고 있으니 복구단도 백 명 몫이다 고브!<end>"

## 1340524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3腹減ったゴブ。<end>なんか食いたいゴブ。<end>ガント親分、腹黒いゴブ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3I'm hungry, gob.<end>I want something to eat, gob.<end>Boss Gant is a schemer, gob.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3배고프다 고브.<end>뭔가 먹고 싶다 고브.<end>간트 두목, 속이 시커멓다 고브.<end>"

## 1340525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>タルテュバ様にお会いになりますか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, would you like to see Lord Tartuba?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 타르튜바 님을 만나시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1340526
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい<end>"
EN: "Yes<end>"
KO: "네<end>"

## 1340527
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ<end>"
EN: "No<end>"
KO: "아니요<end>"

## 1340528
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。<line-break>では、こちらに…。<end>"
EN: "Very well. This way, please...<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 그럼 이쪽으로….<end>"

## 1340529
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ようやく、復興の方も<line-break>軌道に乗ってまいりました。<line-break>人の笑顔って素敵なものですね。<end>"
EN: "I see... At long last, the rebuilding is beginning to find its footing. There is something wonderful about seeing people smile.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요. 드디어 복구도 궤도에 올랐습니다. 사람의 웃는 얼굴은 참 좋네요.<end>"

## 1340530
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様も<line-break>復興にはひとかたならぬ<line-break>お力をお貸しくださっています。<line-break>エルファス様は、ロストールだけでなく<line-break>世界中の都市で復興のため働いて、<line-break>神のようにあがめられているのです。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas has also been a tremendous help with the rebuilding. He is working to restore cities not only in Rostorl, but all over the world, and people worship him like a god.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님도 부흥에 큰 힘을 보태 주고 계십니다. 엘파스 님은 로스톨뿐 아니라 세계 각지의 도시에서 부흥을 위해 일하며 신처럼 숭배받고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340531
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>あ、あの…、<line-break>私のお願いを聞いてくださいますか？<line-break>寝室に…来ていただきたいのです…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Um, may I ask you a favor? I would like you to come to the bedchamber...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 저, 저기… 제 부탁을 들어주시겠어요? 침실로… 와 주셨으면 합니다….<end>"

## 1340532
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様です。<line-break>倒れていらっしゃったのを<line-break>介抱したのです。<end>"
EN: "It's Lord Tartuba. I found him collapsed and have been tending to him.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님입니다. 쓰러져 계신 걸 간호했습니다.<end>"

## 1340533
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。あのとき確かに<line-break>タルテュバ様は怪物になられ、<line-break>退治されました。<line-break>それが、どういうわけか、<line-break>元のお姿で、衰弱しきって<line-break>発見されたのです。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Lord Tartuba did become a monster that day and was slain. Yet somehow, he was found restored to his original form and severely weakened.<end>"
KO: "네. 그때 분명 타르튜바 님은 괴물이 되셨고 퇴치당했습니다. 그런데 어찌 된 일인지 원래 모습으로, 완전히 쇠약해진 채 발견되셨습니다.<end>"

## 1340534
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様のお体はボロボロです。<line-break>日頃から大酒を飲まれ、<line-break>不摂生をなさっていたこともあって…。<line-break>その上、今までどうなさっていたのか。<line-break>とにかく、衰弱がひどくて<line-break>かなり深刻な容態です。<end>"
EN: "Lord Tartuba's body is in a terrible state. His heavy drinking and unhealthy habits have taken their toll... and we don't know what happened to him in the meantime. In any case, he is extremely weak. His condition is quite grave.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님의 몸은 완전히 망가져 있습니다. 평소 과음하고 몸을 돌보지 않으셨던 데다… 그동안 어떻게 지내셨는지도 모르겠고요. 아무튼 쇠약이 심해 상태가 상당히 위중합니다.<end>"

## 1340535
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意識も…戻りません。<line-break>本当に危険な状態です。<line-break>ですから…。<line-break>タルテュバ様の治療のため、<line-break>生命のかけらを<line-break>分けていただきたいのです。<end>"
EN: "He still hasn't regained consciousness. His condition is truly critical. And so... to treat Lord Tartuba, I would like you to share a Life Fragment with him.<end>"
KO: "의식도… 돌아오지 않습니다. 정말 위험한 상태예요. 그래서… 타르튜바 님을 치료하기 위해 생명의 조각을 나눠 주셨으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1340536
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>どうか、お願いします…。<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>... I'm begging you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 부디 부탁드립니다….<end>"

## 1340537
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生命のかけらを与える<end>"
EN: "Give the Life Fragment<end>"
KO: "생명의 조각을 준다<end>"

## 1340538
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生命のかけらをタルテュバに与えない<end>"
EN: "Do not give the Life Fragment to Tartuba<end>"
KO: "생명의 조각을 타르튜바에게 주지 않는다<end>"

## 1340539
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>タルテュバに<line-break>生命のかけらを与えた。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> gave Tartuba a Life Fragment.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 타르튜바에게 생명의 조각을 주었다.<end>"

## 1340540
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…でも、衰弱がひどすぎる。<line-break>　生命のかけらを与えても<line-break>　治るだろうか…？）<end>"
EN: "(But he's far too weak... Will even a Life Fragment be enough for him to recover?)<end>"
KO: "(…하지만 너무 쇠약해졌어. 생명의 조각을 줘도 나을 수 있을까…?)<end>"

## 1340541
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…待て。<end>"
EN: "...Wait.<end>"
KO: "…기다려.<end>"

## 1340542
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺みたいな奴を<line-break>なぜ、助ける？<end>"
EN: "Why... save someone like me?<end>"
KO: "…나 같은 놈을 왜 돕는 거지?<end>"

## 1340543
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺で…さえ、俺自身で…さえ、<line-break>俺のことが好きになれなかった。<line-break>だのに…なぜ？　ゲホッ、ゲホッ！<end>"
EN: "Even I... even I couldn't bring myself to like myself. So why...? Cough! Cough!<end>"
KO: "나조차도… 나 자신조차도… 나를 좋아할 수 없었어. 그런데… 왜? 콜록, 콜록!<end>"

## 1340544
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あいにくだな。<line-break>まだ…もうしばらくは…くたばらん。<line-break>もう少しは…もつ。フフ…。<end>"
EN: "S-sorry to disappoint you. I won't... be dropping dead just yet. I've still got... a little time. Heh...<end>"
KO: "아, 유감이군. 아직… 한동안은… 죽지 않아. 조금은 더… 버틸 수 있어. 후후….<end>"

## 1340545
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き…気休めか…？<line-break>フン…、俺はもう貴族様ではない。<line-break>へつらう…必要は…ゲホッ！　ゲホッ！<end>"
EN: "A-are you just... trying to make me feel better? Hmph... I'm no noble anymore. There's no need... to flatter me... Cough! Cough!<end>"
KO: "기… 기분 풀어 주려고 하는 말인가…? 흥… 난 이제 귀족 나리도 아니야. 아첨할… 필요는… 콜록! 콜록!<end>"

## 1340546
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜだ？　なぜ？<line-break>打算も見返りもなしにやさしくなれる？<line-break>どうして、…俺みたいな…奴に？　<line-break>あ、あんたもだ。<line-break>姫さん…。<end>"
EN: "Why? Why would you show kindness without hoping to gain anything in return? Why... to someone like me? Y-you too, Princess...<end>"
KO: "왜지? 왜? 계산도 보답도 없이 친절할 수 있어? 왜… 나 같은 녀석에게? 너도 그래. 공주님….<end>"

## 1340547
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は…最低な…男だ。<line-break>従兄弟たちの…ような才もない。<line-break>愛されるべきものなど…何も…ない。<end>"
EN: "I'm... a wretched man. I have none of my cousins' talent. There is nothing in me... worth loving.<end>"
KO: "나는… 최악의… 남자다. 사촌들… 같은 재능도 없어. 사랑받을 만한 것도… 아무것도… 없다.<end>"

## 1340548
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ、そんなことはありません！<end>"
EN: "No, that isn't true!<end>"
KO: "아니요, 그렇지 않습니다!<end>"

## 1340549
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "慰めはいい。<line-break>俺だって自分のことぐらいはわかる。<end>"
EN: "Spare me the consolation. I know what I am.<end>"
KO: "위로는 됐어. 나도 내 일 정도는 알아.<end>"

## 1340550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人に優れたところなど…家柄だけ。<line-break>俺は、だからその家柄にすがって<line-break>かろうじて自尊心を維持していた。<line-break>はたから見れば、イヤな貴族の典型だ。<line-break>俺はそれがわかっていたから<line-break>いっそう荒れずにいられなかった。<end>"
EN: "The only thing I had over anyone else... was my family name. So I clung to it, barely managing to preserve my pride. To anyone looking on, I was the very picture of an obnoxious noble. I knew it, and that only made me behave even worse.<end>"
KO: "남들보다 나은 거라곤… 집안뿐이었다. 그래서 나는 그 집안에 매달려 겨우 자존심을 지켰지. 남들이 보면 싫은 귀족의 전형이었어. 나도 그걸 알고 있었기에 더더욱 삐뚤어질 수밖에 없었다.<end>"

## 1340551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "酒におぼれ…弱いものをいじめ…<line-break>乱暴を働く、みな、俺を嫌っていた。<line-break>そんな俺を…誰よりも俺が憎んでいた。<line-break>…意外か？<line-break>誰でもない自分のことだ、<line-break>気にしない…わけがない。<end>"
EN: "I drowned myself in drink... bullied the weak... committed acts of violence. Everyone hated me. And no one hated me more than I hated myself. Surprised? It was me, not anyone else. How could I not care?<end>"
KO: "술에 빠지고… 약한 자를 괴롭히고… 난폭하게 굴었지. 모두가 날 싫어했어. 그런 나를… 누구보다 내가 미워했다. …의외냐? 남도 아닌 내 자신 이야기야. 신경 쓰이지 않을… 리가 없지.<end>"

## 1340552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、…俺はそれを他人のせいにして<line-break>ただ、荒れた…。お前の師匠のオッシは<line-break>かつてエリスにたてついた貴族なんだ。<line-break>俺は奴が、のうのうと平民街で幸せに<line-break>暮らしているのを見て腹を立てていた。<line-break>なんで、奴は奴は奴は…って…。<end>"
EN: "But I blamed everyone else and just kept lashing out... Your master, Ossi, is a noble who once defied Eris. Seeing him living happily and without a care in the commoners' quarter made me furious. Why him? Why him, why him...? That was all I could think.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 나는 그걸 남 탓으로 돌리고 그저 삐뚤어졌어…. 네 스승 오시는 과거 에리스에게 맞선 귀족이야. 나는 그자가 아무렇지도 않게 평민가에서 행복하게 사는 걸 보고 화가 났지. 왜 저자는, 왜 저자는, 왜 저자는… 하면서….<end>"

## 1340553
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺は、俺が大事にしている剣を<line-break>盗まれて、カッと来て<line-break>平民街にモンスターを放した…。<line-break>お前も、そこにいたろう？<line-break>お前は俺が憎くないわけはない…。<line-break>だのに、俺を…助ける。<end>"
EN: "Someone stole a sword I treasured, and in my rage I unleashed monsters upon the commoners' quarter... You were there too, weren't you? You have every reason to hate me... Yet you're saving me.<end>"
KO: "…나는 아끼던 검을 도둑맞고 화가 나서 평민가에 몬스터를 풀어놨다…. 너도 거기 있었지? 네가 날 미워하지 않을 리가 없어…. 그런데도 날… 구해 주는군.<end>"

## 1340554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前…農民なんだろ…？<line-break>レ、レムオンの領地で暴れ回ってた…。<line-break>　<line-break>俺は…お前のことを…貴族だから嫌い、<line-break>貴族だから…憎むっていう…奴だと…<line-break>思ってた…。なのに俺を…助ける…。<end>"
EN: "You're... a peasant, aren't you? The one who was rampaging through L-Lemghon's domain... I thought you hated me because I was a noble... hated me simply for being one... Yet you're saving me.<end>"
KO: "너… 농민이잖아…? 레, 레무온의 영지에서 날뛰던…. 난… 네가… 귀족이라서 싫어하고, 귀족이라서… 미워하는… 그런 녀석인 줄 알았어…. 그런데 나를… 구해 주다니….<end>"

## 1340555
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姫さん…あんたにだって<line-break>俺はひどいことをした。<line-break>覚えているだろう？　<line-break>俺には助けられるだけの価値はない…<line-break>なのに、なぜ…<line-break>あんたらは、俺を助ける？<end>"
EN: "Princess... I did terrible things to you as well. You remember, don't you? I'm not worth saving... So why? Why are the two of you saving me?<end>"
KO: "공주님… 당신한테도 나는 끔찍한 짓을 했어. 기억하고 있겠지? 난 구원받을 가치도 없어… 그런데 왜… 당신들은 나를 구하는 거지?<end>"

## 1340556
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は何もかも…決めてかかっていた。<line-break>憎けりゃ、殺したくなる。<line-break>愛されない奴は憎まれるしかない。<line-break>そう、思っていた。<line-break>だが、すべては…人の心にあるんだな。<line-break>人の心が状況を変えるのだな。<end>"
EN: "I thought I knew how everything worked. If you hate someone, you want to kill them. If no one loves you, then you can only be hated. That was what I believed. But it all lies... within the human heart, doesn't it? A person's heart can change how things turn out.<end>"
KO: "난 모든 걸… 단정 짓고 있었어. 미우면 죽이고 싶어진다. 사랑받지 못한 자는 미움받을 수밖에 없다. …그렇게 생각했지. 하지만 모든 것은… 사람의 마음에 달린 거구나. 사람의 마음이 상황을 바꾸는 거야.<end>"

## 1340557
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も…、変われたのかもしれない。<line-break>もっと早く…このことに気づいていれば<line-break>俺がお前の…ゲホッゴホッ！<line-break>き、気づいたときには時間切れか…。<line-break>ふふ…、俺らしい…。<end>"
EN: "Maybe I could have changed too. If only I'd realized sooner, I might have been your... Cough! Cough! S-so I finally understand, just as my time runs out... Heh. How like me...<end>"
KO: "나도… 변할 수 있었을지도 몰라. 좀 더 일찍… 이걸 깨달았다면 내가 네… 콜록, 콜록! 깨, 깨달았을 땐 이미 시간 끝인가…. 후후… 나답군….<end>"

## 1340558
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あばよ。<line-break>…ロクな人生じゃなかった…が…<line-break>お前のおかげで…少しは救われ…た…。<end>"
EN: "So long. It wasn't much of a life... but thanks to you... I found a little salvation...<end>"
KO: "잘 있어. …변변한 인생은 아니었지만… 네 덕분에… 조금은 구원받았…다….<end>"

## 1340559
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様…。<end>"
EN: "Lord Tartuba...<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님….<end>"

## 1340560
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの方は、私と同じでした。<line-break>すべてを憎み、心で闇を育て…<line-break>あなたが私を救い出してくれて…。<line-break>あなたがいなければ、<line-break>私もきっと…。<end>"
EN: "He was like me. He hated everything and nurtured darkness in his heart... Then you rescued me. If not for you, I surely would have...<end>"
KO: "그분은 저와 같았습니다. 모든 것을 증오하고 마음속에서 어둠을 키우고…. 당신이 저를 구해 주셨고…. 당신이 없었다면 저도 분명….<end>"

## 1340561
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…安らかな、お顔でした…。<end>"
EN: "But... his face was peaceful in the end.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 편안한 얼굴이었습니다….<end>"

## 1340562
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私からもお礼を言わせてください。<line-break><value:$28>様が<line-break>いらっしゃらなければ、<line-break>あのようなお顔は<line-break>できなかったでしょう。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Please allow me to thank you as well. Without you, <value:$28>, he could never have looked so peaceful. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "저도 감사드리겠습니다. <value:$28>님이 계시지 않았다면 그런 표정은 지으실 수 없었겠지요. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1340563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다.<end>"

## 1340564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 1340565
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…はい。<line-break>タルテュバ様のためにも<line-break>がんばってみます。<end>"
EN: "...Yes. I'll do my best, for Lord Tartuba's sake as well.<end>"
KO: "…네. 타르튜바 님을 위해서라도 힘내 보겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340566
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"

## 1340567
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…生命のかけらが手元にない…<line-break>　どうにかして手にいれなければ…）<end>"
EN: "(I don't have a Life Fragment... I have to find some way to get one.)<end>"
KO: "(…생명의 조각이 수중에 없어… 어떻게든 구해야 해…)<end>"

## 1340568
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "저기… <value:$28> 님?<end>"

## 1340569
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>ようこそ、いらっしゃいました。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Welcome. I'm so glad you've come.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 어서 오십시오.<end>"

## 1340570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主エルファス様も<line-break>復興にはひとかたならぬ<line-break>お力をお貸しくださっています。<line-break>エルファス様は、ロストールだけでなく<line-break>世界中の都市で復興のため働いて、<line-break>神のようにあがめられているのです。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas, the Savior, has also lent his extraordinary aid to the rebuilding. He is helping cities recover not only in Rostorl, but all over the world, and people have come to worship him like a god.<end>"
KO: "구세주 엘파스 님도 복구에 큰 힘을 보태고 계십니다. 엘파스 님은 로스톨뿐 아니라 세계 각지의 도시에서 복구를 위해 일하며 신처럼 숭배받고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340571
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の心配は杞憂だったのでしょうか？<line-break>それに…<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様の<line-break>お力だって大きいのに…。<line-break>私、少し不満です。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps my fears were unfounded? And yet... <value:$28> has done so much as well. I'm a little unhappy about that.<end>"
KO: "제 걱정은 기우였던 걸까요? 게다가… <value:$28>님의 힘도 큰데…. 저, 조금 불만이에요.<end>"

## 1340572
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様、<line-break>お帰りなさいませ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Welcome back.<end>"
KO: "이런, <value:$28>님. 돌아오셨군요.<end>"

## 1340573
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールは少しずつ復興し、<line-break>身分の上下の関係なく、<line-break>それを喜んでおります。<line-break>やはり、レムオン様にも、<line-break>この場にいていただきたかった…<end>"
EN: "Rostorl is recovering little by little, and everyone, highborn and lowborn alike, rejoices. I only wish Lord Lemghon could have been here with us...<end>"
KO: "로스톨은 조금씩 부흥하고 신분의 높고 낮음과 관계없이 모두가 기뻐하고 있습니다. 역시 레무온 님도 이 자리에 계셨으면 했는데….<end>"

## 1340574
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "『力・英知・博愛心、<line-break>　すべてを備えしものに、<line-break>　我が遺品ストラスエッジを与えよ』<end>"
EN: "“To one who possesses strength, wisdom, and benevolence, bestow Stra's Edge, the relic I leave behind.”<end>"
KO: "『힘·지혜·박애심, 모든 것을 갖춘 자에게 나의 유품 스트라스 엣지를 주어라』<end>"

## 1340575
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハッ！<end>"
EN: "Hah!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1340576
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いちゃダメゴブ！<line-break>別にフゴーの宝物がほしくて<line-break>復興に手を貸しているんじゃないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Don't listen, gob! I'm not helping with the rebuilding because I want Hugo's treasure, gob!<end>"
KO: "들으면 안 된다 고브! 휴고의 보물이 탐나서 부흥을 돕는 게 아니다 고브!<end>"

## 1340577
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むむむっ…<end>"
EN: "Grrr...<end>"
KO: "으으음…<end>"

## 1340578
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハッ！<end>"
EN: "Hah!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1340579
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別にフゴーの宝物がほしくて<line-break>復興に手を貸しているんじゃないゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>だから、先を越された…とか、<line-break>全然思ってないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "I'm not helping with the rebuilding because I want Hugo's treasure, gob! And I definitely don't think someone beat me to it... or anything like that, gob!<end>"
KO: "휴고의 보물이 탐나서 복구를 돕는 게 아니다 고브! 그러니까 선수를 빼앗겼다거나 전혀 생각 안 한다 고브!<end>"

## 1340580
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3団長はエライでゴブ。<line-break>ロストールの人たちのために<line-break>ガンバル姿には感動するゴブ！<end>こうやって、みんなでみんなで<line-break>しやあせになるゴブ！<end>自分たちゴブゴブ団も<line-break>一生懸命働いているから<line-break>復興団も、１００人力ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3The boss is great, gob. Seeing the boss work so hard for the people of Rostorl is inspiring, gob!<end>This way, everyone can be happy together, gob!<end>The Gob-Gob Gang is working as hard as it can too, so the Restoration League has the strength of a hundred, gob!<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3단장님은 훌륭하다 고브. 로스톨 사람들을 위해 힘내는 모습에 감동한다 고브!<end>이렇게 모두 함께 모두 함께 행복해지는 거다 고브!<end>우리 고브고브단도 열심히 일하고 있으니 복구단도 백 명 몫이다 고브!<end>"

## 1340581
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3腹減ったゴブ。<end>なんか食いたいゴブ。<end>ガント親分、腹黒いゴブ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3I'm hungry, gob.<end>I want something to eat, gob.<end>Boss Gant is a schemer, gob.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3배고프다 고브.<end>뭔가 먹고 싶다 고브.<end>간트 두목, 속이 시커멓다 고브.<end>"

## 1340582
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>ようこそ、いらっしゃいました。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Welcome. I'm so glad you came.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 어서 오십시오.<end>"

## 1340583
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？<line-break>私がここにいるのが不思議ですか？<end>"
EN: "What? Are you surprised to find me here?<end>"
KO: "어? 제가 여기 있는 게 이상한가요?<end>"

## 1340584
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<line-break>私は確かに王位を受け継ぎました。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I did inherit the throne.<end>"
KO: "네. 저는 분명 왕위를 이어받았습니다.<end>"

## 1340585
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、今、<line-break>この国に必要なのは、<line-break>玉座に座って指図する人ではなく、<line-break>みなさんと一緒に歩んでいける人だと<line-break>思うのです。<end>"
EN: "But right now, I don't believe this country needs someone who sits on the throne and gives orders. It needs someone who can move forward alongside everyone.<end>"
KO: "하지만 지금 이 나라에 필요한 건 왕좌에 앉아 지시하는 사람이 아니라 모두와 함께 걸어갈 사람이라고 생각합니다.<end>"

## 1340586
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとうございます。<line-break>これからも、みなさんと一緒に<line-break>がんばっていこうと思います。<end>"
EN: "…Thank you. I'll keep doing my best alongside all of you.<end>"
KO: "…감사합니다. 앞으로도 여러분과 함께 힘내 보겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340587
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、それから…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ガントさんに<line-break>冒険者さんにお仕事をお願いしたときは<line-break>報酬を払うと教わりました。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and one more thing... Gant taught me that adventurers should be paid when asked to take on a job.<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고… 간트 씨에게 모험자에게 일을 부탁했을 때는 보수를 줘야 한다고 배웠습니다.<end>"

## 1340588
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王家に伝わる宝物の１つです。<line-break>闇の力から守ってくれるそうです。<end>"
EN: "This is one of the treasures passed down through the royal family. It's said to protect against the power of darkness.<end>"
KO: "왕가에 전해지는 보물 중 하나입니다. 어둠의 힘에서 지켜 준다고 합니다.<end>"

## 1340589
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからも、みなさんと一緒に<line-break>がんばっていこうと思います。<end>"
EN: "I'll keep doing my best, together with all of you.<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 여러분과 함께 힘내겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340590
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちくたびれたぞ！<line-break>我が宿敵、永遠のライバルよ！<end>"
EN: "I've been waiting forever! My sworn enemy, my eternal rival!<end>"
KO: "기다리다 지쳤다! 나의 숙적, 영원한 라이벌이여!<end>"

## 1340591
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3なんじゃ、お主は？<line-break>今はそれどころではない！<line-break>どけ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4きゃっ！<line-break>また、この人！<line-break>かなりおジャマかも！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5おおお！<line-break>テンション高いぜ！<line-break>ソウルが燃えるぜ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6…ガルドラン！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…貴様と遊んでいるヒマはない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14道を開けるんじゃ！<line-break>おのれと遊んどるヒマなど<line-break>ないわい！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ガルドラン！<line-break>ジャマしないで。<line-break>今はそれどころじゃないんだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17キャー、なによ！　この下等生物！<line-break>高貴なエルフであるこのアタクシの<line-break>ジャマするなんて１００年早くてよぉ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ああーっ！　また変なおじさーん！<line-break>ルルアンタたち、急いでるの！<line-break>ジャマしないでよぉ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…消えろ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26なんじゃ、なんじゃ？<line-break>この熱い…というより<line-break>暑苦しいソウルの持ち主は？<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…みっともない男。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27彼はいったい…？<line-break>これも世界の不安を象徴する<line-break>社会のひずみの現れなのだろうか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>192………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>…暑苦しい。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194うっ。<line-break>…目障りだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191…仮装行列ならどいて。<line-break>エリス様が死んだ日にふさわしくない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…ガキ、どけよ。<line-break>俺は最高に機嫌が悪いんだ。<end>そこをどいてよ。<line-break>急いでるんだよ。<line-break>ジャマしないでよぉ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3Who are you? This is no time for this! Out of my way.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Eek! Him again! He could be quite a nuisance!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Whoa! Now that's some energy! It sets my soul on fire!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6...Girldlan?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13I have no time to play with you.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Clear the way! I've no time to waste playing with you!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Girldlan! Don't get in the way. We don't have time for this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Eek! What is this lowly creature?! You dare stand in the way of a noble Elf like me? You're a hundred years too early for that!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Aah! Not that strange old man again! Lulluantha and the others are in a hurry! Don't get in our way!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Vanish.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Well now, what's this? Who has such a passionate... no, oppressively intense soul?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11What a disgraceful man.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Who is he...? Is this yet another manifestation of the social distortions that symbolize the world's unrest?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192...So overbearing.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Ugh. What an eyesore.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191If you're with the costume parade, move. This isn't fitting for the day Lady Eris died.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Out of the way, brat. I'm in a seriously foul mood.<end>Get out of the way. I'm in a hurry. Stop getting in my way!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3뭐냐, 네놈은? 지금은 그럴 때가 아니다! 비켜.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4꺅! 또 이 사람! 상당히 방해일지도!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5오오오! 텐션 높아진다! 소울이 불타오른다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6…갈드란!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…네놈과 놀아 줄 시간 없다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14길을 비켜라! 네놈과 놀 시간 따위 없다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15갈드란! 방해하지 마. 지금은 그럴 때가 아니야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17꺄ー, 뭐야! 이 하등생물! 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸을 방해하려 하다니 100년은 일러!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19아아ー! 또 이상한 아저씨ー! 루루안타들은 급해! 방해하지 마아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…사라져라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26뭐냐, 뭐야? 이 뜨거운… 아니, 숨 막히게 더운 소울의 주인은?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…꼴사나운 남자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27그는 대체…? 이것도 세계의 불안을 상징하는 사회적 뒤틀림의 발현인가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192………………. …더워.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194욱. …거슬린다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191…가장행렬이라면 비켜. 에리스 님이 죽은 날에 어울리지 않아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…꼬마, 비켜. 난 지금 기분이 최악이야.<end>거기 비켜 줘. 급하단 말이야. 방해하지 마아.<end>"

## 1340592
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、未来の勇者の世界観を<line-break>目をつぶって思う存分<line-break>想像するがいい！<end>"
EN: "Now close your eyes and let your imagination run wild with the future Hero's vision of the world!<end>"
KO: "자, 미래의 용사의 세계관을 눈을 감고 마음껏 상상해 봐라!<end>"

## 1340593
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様の世界は一面の花畑。<line-break>そこにほほえむフェルムちゃんがいる。<line-break>そして、その横に…！<end>"
EN: "My world is an endless field of flowers. There stands sweet Ferme, smiling. And beside her...!<end>"
KO: "나님의 세계는 온통 꽃밭. 그곳에서 페름 짱이 웃고 있어. 그리고 그 옆에는…!<end>"

## 1340594
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪悪な笑みを浮かべる悪の使者、<line-break>貴様がいる！<end>"
EN: "There you stand, an agent of evil with a wicked grin!<end>"
KO: "사악한 미소를 짓는 악의 사자, 바로 네놈이다!<end>"

## 1340595
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…悲しいが、それが現実だ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>フェルムちゃんは、<line-break>このいかがわしい悪魔と<line-break>りりしい俺様の間で迷っている！<end>"
EN: "It pains me, but such is the truth. Ferme is torn between this disreputable devil and my own dashing self!<end>"
KO: "…슬프지만 그게 현실이야. 페름은 이 수상한 악마와 늠름한 이 몸 사이에서 고민하고 있어!<end>"

## 1340596
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "小さなハートを痛めている！<end>"
EN: "Her tender little heart is aching!<end>"
KO: "작은 가슴을 아파하고 있어!<end>"

## 1340597
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、ここで決着をつける！<line-break>悪魔が勝つか！　正義の味方が勝つか！<line-break>フェルムちゃんをかけた決闘だ！<end>"
EN: "So we shall settle this here and now! Will the devil prevail, or the champion of justice? We duel for Ferme!<end>"
KO: "그러니까 여기서 결판을 낸다! 악마가 이길지! 정의의 편이 이길지! 페름 짱을 건 결투다!<end>"

## 1340598
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、俺様の英雄伝説は<line-break>２４回目のクライマックスに突入する！<line-break>さぁ、勝負だ！<end>"
EN: "My heroic legend now enters its twenty-fourth climax! Come, let battle commence!<end>"
KO: "지금 이 몸의 영웅전설은 24번째 클라이맥스에 돌입한다! 자, 승부다!<end>"

## 1340599
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はそれどころじゃない！<end>"
EN: "This isn't the time for that!<end>"
KO: "지금은 그럴 때가 아니야!<end>"

## 1340600
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェルムは渡さない！<end>"
EN: "You can't have Ferme!<end>"
KO: "페름은 넘겨주지 않아!<end>"

## 1340601
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぬわにいっ！？<line-break>フェルムちゃんの幸せが<line-break>かかっているんだぞ！<line-break>フェルムちゃんの幸せより大事なものが<line-break>この世界にあるというのか！<line-break>貴様、それでも俺様のライバルか！<end>"
EN: "Whaaat!? Ferme's happiness is at stake! Are you saying anything in this world matters more than her happiness? And you call yourself my rival?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고오!? 페름의 행복이 걸려 있다고! 페름의 행복보다 중요한 게 이 세상에 있단 말이냐! 네놈, 그래도 내 라이벌이냐!<end>"

## 1340602
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし、わかった。つまり貴様は<line-break>俺様に恐れをなして戦意喪失。<line-break>俺様の完全勝利ということだな。<line-break>ならばもう貴様ごときに用はない！<line-break>早くフェルムちゃんに報告して<line-break>勝利の熱い抱擁を交わさなければ！<end>"
EN: "Very well, I understand. The sight of me has struck terror into your heart and robbed you of the will to fight. In other words, my victory is complete! I have no further business with the likes of you. I must hurry to tell Ferme and share a passionate embrace of victory with her!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 알았다. 즉 네놈은 이 몸이 두려워 전의를 상실했다는 거군. 이 몸의 완전 승리라는 뜻이다! 그렇다면 이제 네놈 따위에게 볼일 없다! 빨리 페름에게 보고하고 뜨거운 승리의 포옹을 나눠야지!<end>"

## 1340603
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3………………。<line-break>…ほれた女ならば、<line-break>悔いのないようにするがよかろう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4うふふ、<value:$28>も<line-break>ガルドランの同類かも！<line-break>暑苦しい男同士の戦い…帰りたいな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>5へっ、そうだよな。<line-break>売られたケンカは買わねえとな！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6…<value:$28>。<line-break>そんなに、フェルムちゃんが<line-break>大事なんだね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…クッ、女か。<line-break>つまらんことで…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14この非常時に、<line-break>何をやっとるんじゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…そうだね。<line-break>フェルムちゃんて娘が大切なら、<line-break>戦わないといけないよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17な、な、なによ！　信じられないわぁ！<line-break>なんで、そんな下等生物の女のために<line-break>なんで、そんなことまでするのよぉお！<end><if><value:$01><equal>20戦うか…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>19…もう、どうでもいいけど<line-break>２人ともフェルムさんの<line-break>気持ちはちゃんと確かめたの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…まさか、<value:$28><line-break>お前まで？<end><if><value:$01><equal>26<value:$28>もまた<line-break>若いのう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…つきあいがいいのね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192<value:$28>は<line-break>フェルムのことが大切なのですね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28>さん…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194うう…。<line-break>まだ…あいつの暑苦しい顔を<line-break>見ていないといけないのか…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191…いいわ。<line-break>このゴミを<line-break>さっさと処分しましょ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…わかった。<line-break>一瞬でぶちのめすぞ。<line-break>時間が惜しい。<end>…そうだね。<line-break>何が大事かなんて<line-break>人によって違うんだよ。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>フェルムちゃんて娘が大切なら、<line-break>戦わないといけないよ。<end>"

Ferme really means that much to you, doesn't she?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Tch, over a woman? If this girl Ferme matters to you, then you have to fight.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17W-W-What!? I don't believe this! Why would you go so far for some lowly female creature? Why?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Fight, huh...<end><if><value:$01><equal>19I hardly care anymore, but have you two actually asked Ferme how she feels?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Surely not... <value:$28>, you too?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26You are young as well, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11You certainly are obliging.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Ferme is important to you, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Ugh... Do I still have to look at that obnoxious face of his...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Very well. Let's dispose of this trash and be done with it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Fine. I'll take him down in an instant. We have no time to waste.<end>Yes... What matters most is different for everyone. <value:$28>, if this girl Ferme matters to you, then you have to fight.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3………………. …반한 여자라면 후회 없도록 하는 게 좋겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4우후후, <value:$28>도 갈드란과 같은 부류일지도! 뜨거운 남자들끼리의 싸움… 돌아가고 싶다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5헤, 그렇지. 걸어온 싸움은 받아 줘야지!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6…<value:$28>. 페름이 그렇게 소중한 거구나….<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…큭, 여자냐. 하찮은 일로….<end><if><value:$01><equal>14이 비상시에 뭘 하는 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…그렇지. 페름이라는 아이가 소중하다면 싸워야 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 뭐, 뭐야! 믿을 수 없어! 왜 그런 하등생물 여자 때문에 왜 그런 짓까지 하는 거야아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20싸우는 건가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>19…이젠 아무래도 좋지만 둘 다 페름 씨 마음은 제대로 확인했어?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…설마, <value:$28> 너까지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26<value:$28>도 참 젊구나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…참 잘도 어울려 주네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192<value:$28>는 페름을 소중하게 생각하는군요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28> 씨….<end><if><value:$01><equal>194으으…. 아직… 저 녀석의 숨 막히는 얼굴을 봐야 하는 건가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>191…좋아. 이 쓰레기를 얼른 치워 버리자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…알았다. 단숨에 박살 낸다. 시간이 아까워.<end>…그래. 무엇이 소중한지는 사람마다 달라. <value:$28>, 페름이라는 아이가 소중하다면 싸워야 해.<end>"

## 1340604
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺様の負けだ。<line-break>未来の勇者から<line-break>歌を送るぜ…、くくぅっ…！<end>"
EN: "I... have lost. The future Hero dedicates this song to you... Ngh...!<end>"
KO: "…내가 졌다. 미래의 용자가 노래를 보내 주지… 크윽…!<end>"

## 1340605
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪英雄街道をひた走る～<line-break>♪ちょっといなせな俺様に～<line-break>♪痛いぜ～、運命の裏切りの刃～<end>"
EN: "♪ Racing down the road of heroes～ ♪ So gallant and dashing am I～ ♪ How it hurts―the treacherous blade of fate～<end>"
KO: "♪영웅 가도를 힘차게 달리는~ ♪조금 멋진 나님에게~ ♪아프구나~, 운명이 배신한 칼날~<end>"

## 1340606
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪運命の女神さえ嫉妬する～<line-break>♪俺様とフェルムちゃんの純愛を～<line-break>♪壊したのはキ・サ・マ～！<line-break>♪そう、貴様は２人を引き裂くオニ～！<line-break>♪ヒ・ド・イ・奴～！　オニ～！<line-break>…フェルムちゃん、ゴメンね。 <end>"
EN: "♪ Even the Goddess of Fate envies～ ♪ The pure love that Ferme and I share～ ♪ And the one who destroyed it was Y-O-U～! ♪ Yes, you are the fiend who tore us apart～! ♪ You heartless brute～! You fiend～! ...Forgive me, Ferme.<end>"
KO: "♪운명의 여신조차 질투하는~ ♪나와 페름 짱의 순수한 사랑을~ ♪깨뜨린 건 바·로·너~! ♪그래, 네놈은 두 사람을 갈라놓은 악귀~! ♪너·무·한·놈~! 악귀~! …페름 짱, 미안해.<end>"

## 1340607
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…、フェルムちゃんを<line-break>幸せに…してやって…くれ…<line-break>…俺様には、孤独が似合う…<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Make Ferme... happy for me... Solitude... suits me best...<end>"
KO: "으으… 페름을 행복하게… 해 줘…. 나님에겐 고독이 어울려….<end>"

## 1340608
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハッハッハッハッハ…！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>高らかにこだまする<line-break>正義の味方の笑い声！<line-break>君の耳にも届いているかい？<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha ha ha...! The triumphant laughter of justice's champion rings out on high! Can you hear it too?<end>"
KO: "하하하하하…! 우렁차게 울려 퍼지는 정의의 편의 웃음소리! 네 귀에도 들리나?<end>"

## 1340609

## 1340624
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様、ご無事でしたか。<line-break>私たちがお部屋にうかがったときには<line-break>おられなかったので、探しました。<end>"
EN: "Lady Tiana, you're safe. You weren't in your room when we came for you, so we've been looking for you.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님, 무사하셨군요. 저희가 방에 찾아갔을 때 안 계셔서 찾고 있었습니다.<end>"

## 1340625
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫。エリス様に命じられて<line-break>あなたを拘束しに来たと思ったなら、<line-break>それは間違いよ。<line-break>エリス様は、もういらっしゃらない。<line-break>…死んだわ。<line-break>レムオンの兵士に襲われてね。<end>"
EN: "It's all right. If you thought Lady Eris sent us to detain you, you were mistaken. Lady Eris is gone. She's dead. Lemghon's soldiers attacked her.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 에리스 님의 명령으로 널 구속하러 왔다고 생각했다면 그건 틀렸어. 에리스 님은 이제 안 계셔. …죽었어. 레무온의 병사들에게 습격당해서.<end>"

## 1340626
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "D-dead?<end>"
KO: "주, 죽었어?<end>"

## 1340627
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様がファーロス家の兵を<line-break>残らず率いてゆかれましたので、<line-break>王妃様を守る者がなかったのです。<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes led every soldier of House Pharos away, leaving no one to protect the Queen.<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님께서 파로스 가문의 병사들을 모두 이끌고 가셔서 왕비님을 지킬 사람이 없었습니다.<end>"

## 1340628
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこへきて、ゼネテスは<line-break>カルラを相手にまたも奇跡の大勝利…<line-break>　<line-break>ファーロス家の力が拡大するのを恐れた<line-break>レムオンがクーデターを<line-break>起こしたってワケ。<end>"
EN: "And then Xenetes won yet another miraculous victory over Karula. Fearing the growing power of House Pharos, Lemghon staged a coup. That's what happened.<end>"
KO: "거기에 제네테스가 카를라를 상대로 또 기적 같은 대승을 거뒀지…. 파로스 가문의 세력이 커지는 걸 두려워한 레무온이 쿠데타를 일으킨 거야.<end>"

## 1340629
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス様は最期に、私どもに<line-break>ティアナ様をお救いするようにと<line-break>申しつけられ…、ご自身は…。<end>"
EN: "With her final command, Lady Eris ordered us to save Lady Tiana... while she herself...<end>"
KO: "에리스 님께서는 마지막으로 저희에게 티아나 님을 구하라 명하시고… 본인은….<end>"

## 1340630
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様も戦場で捕われ、<line-break>今、謁見の間で処刑を待って<line-break>おられます。<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes was also captured on the battlefield. He is now in the audience chamber, awaiting execution.<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님도 전장에서 붙잡혀 지금 알현실에서 처형을 기다리고 계십니다.<end>"

## 1340631
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですって？<line-break>ゼネテス様まで？<end>"
EN: "What? Lord Xenetes too?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고요? 제네테스 님까지?<end>"

## 1340632
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "千年樹に刻まれている神聖法によれば<line-break>反乱貴族たちはあと少ししか<line-break>ゼネテスを拘束できない。<line-break>つまり、裏を返せば、<line-break>そのときまでには無理矢理にでも<line-break>罪をでっち上げなければならない。<end>"
EN: "Under the sacred law carved into the Thousand-Year Tree, the rebel nobles can hold Xenetes for only a little longer. That means they must concoct a charge against him by then, no matter what it takes.<end>"
KO: "천년수에 새겨진 신성법에 따르면 반란 귀족들이 제네테스를 구속할 수 있는 시간은 이제 얼마 남지 않았다. 뒤집어 말하면 그때까지는 억지로라도 죄를 꾸며내야 한다는 뜻이지.<end>"

## 1340633
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まもなく、<line-break>ゼネテス様も<line-break>エリス様と同じように…。<end>"
EN: "Soon Lord Xenetes will meet the same fate as Lady Eris...<end>"
KO: "곧 제네테스 님도 에리스 님과 마찬가지로….<end>"

## 1340636
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "티아나 님, 실례지만 당신이 계시면 <value:$28>님이 마음껏….<end>"

## 1340637
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1340641
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3おい、ゼネテスを助けんでも<line-break>いいのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>4うん、逃げるのが正解かも！<line-break>ゼネテス様を救おうとしても<line-break>これじゃ、きっと間に合わないもん。<line-break>間に合わなくて、目の前で<line-break>ゼネテス様が殺されるの見るより<line-break>ずっとマシかも。だよね？<end><if><value:$01><equal>5逃げんのは気にいらねえけど、<line-break>ま、ロストールの貴族なんて<line-break>どうでもいいか。<line-break>逃げんのは気に入らねえけどよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6こんなの間違ってる！<line-break>ゼネテスさんを助けようよっ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…逃げる気か？<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん！<line-break>ゼネテスさんを<line-break>助けないのかよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14待たんか！<line-break>ゼネテスを助けにゆかんで<line-break>ええのか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、ゼネテスさんを<line-break>助けにいかなくちゃ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17ゼネテスを助けないでいいの？<line-break>…って、べ、べ、別に、アタクシ<line-break>下等生物の心配なんかしてないのよ。<line-break>あ、あなたが後悔して傷つくかなんて<line-break>まったく関係ないわ！　そうよ！<line-break>ただ、逃げるのが気に入らないだけ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ゼネテスが死んじゃうんだよぉ！<line-break><value:$28>は<line-break>それでいいのぉ！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…うぬは、それでよいのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>26ゼネテスという御仁を<line-break>助けんでよいのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…やはりゼネテスは救えないのね。<line-break>悲しいけれど、<line-break>それが現実なのかもしれないわね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27ゼネテスを<line-break>助けに行かないでいいのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>192ゼネテスを助けなくていいのですか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193ま、待ってください！<line-break>ゼネテスさん、助けないと<line-break>処刑されちゃうんですよ！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>194逃げるのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>191待って！<line-break>逃げるのはゼネテスを<line-break>助けてからでも遅くないわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…逃げる気か？<line-break>奴を…見捨てて…。<end>おおぅい、ゼネテスさんを<line-break>まだ助けてないよぉ。<line-break>謁見の間に行こうよぉ！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3Hey, are you sure we can leave Xenetes behind?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Yeah, maybe running away is the right choice! Even if we tried to save Lord Xenetes, we'd never make it in time now. This might be much better than arriving too late and watching him get killed right in front of us. Right?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5I don't like running away, but who cares about some Rostorl noble? Still don't like running, though!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6This is wrong! Let's go save Xenetes, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You're running away?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis! Aren't you going to save Xenetes?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Hold it! Are you truly going to leave without saving Xenetes?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, we have to go save Xenetes.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Are you really going to leave Xenetes? N-not that I care what happens to that lowly creature! And whether you regret it and get hurt has absolutely nothing to do with me! That's right! I simply don't like running away!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Xenetes is going to die! Are you really all right with that, <value:$28>?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Is that truly your wish?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Are you certain we need not save the gentleman called Xenetes?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11So Xenetes can't be saved after all. It won't be too late to flee after we've saved Xenetes.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190You're going to run... and abandon him?<end>Heeey, we still haven't saved Xenetes! Let's go to the audience chamber!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3이봐, 제네테스를 구하지 않아도 되는 거냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4응, 도망치는 게 정답일지도! 제네테스 님을 구하려 해도 이제는 늦을 거야. 눈앞에서 죽는 걸 보는 것보단 낫겠지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5도망치는 건 마음에 안 들지만, 뭐 로스톨 귀족 따윈 상관없나. 그래도 도망치는 건 싫다고!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6이건 잘못됐어! 제네테스 씨를 구하자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…도망칠 생각인가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나! 제네테스 씨를 안 구할 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14기다려라! 제네테스를 구하러 가지 않아도 되는 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15제네테스 씨를 구하러 가야 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17제네테스를 안 구해도 돼? …벼, 별로 아타쿠시가 하등생물을 걱정하는 건 아니야. 네가 후회하고 상처받든 아무 상관없어! 그냥 도망치는 게 마음에 안 들 뿐이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19제네테스가 죽는다고오! <value:$28>는 그래도 괜찮아아!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…너는 그래도 좋은가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26제네테스라는 사람을 구하지 않아도 되는가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11…역시 제네테스를 구할 수 없는 거구나. 슬프지만 그게 현실일지도 모르겠네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27제네테스를 구하러 가지 않아도 되는가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192제네테스를 구하지 않아도 됩니까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193기, 기다려 주세요! 제네테스 씨를 안 구하면 처형당한다고요!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194도망치는 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191기다려! 도망치는 건 제네테스를 구한 뒤라도 늦지 않아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…도망칠 생각인가? 그를… 버리고….<end>이봐아, 제네테스 씨를 아직 안 구했어. 알현실로 가자아!<end>"

## 1340652
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<line-break>あ、あの、お気持ちだけ…<line-break>ありがたくいただいておきますわ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Oh, um... I truly appreciate the thought.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님…. 저, 그 마음만은 감사히 받을게요.<end>"

## 1340653
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、援助してくださるのですね。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break> <line-break>しっかりと役立てさせて<line-break>いただきますわ、<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you're making a donation? Thank you. I promise we'll put it to good use, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 지원해 주시는군요. 감사합니다. 제대로 도움이 되게 쓰겠습니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"
