## 1340658
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます、<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break> <line-break>ロストールの建て直しのために、<line-break>有意義に使わせていただきますわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. I'll put it to good use in rebuilding Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다, <value:$28>님. 로스톨 재건을 위해 뜻깊게 사용하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1340659
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むむっ、イチ冒険者の身で<line-break>復興団に援助するゴブか！<line-break>我々ゴブゴブ団も負けてられないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "What? A mere adventurer donating to the Restoration League, gob? The Gob-Gob Gang can't let you outdo us, gob!<end>"
KO: "뭣, 한낱 모험가가 부흥단을 지원하는 고브인가! 우리 고브고브단도 질 수 없는 고브!<end>"

## 1340660
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無用な侵入者はしかるべく処理するぞ！<line-break>命が惜しくば、立ち去れい！<end>"
EN: "Unwanted intruders will be dealt with accordingly! If you value your life, leave now!<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 침입자는 적절히 처리하겠다! 목숨이 아깝다면 물러가라!<end>"

## 1340661
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "立ち去らない<end>"
EN: "Stay<end>"
KO: "떠나지 않는다<end>"

## 1340662
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "立ち去る<end>"
EN: "Leave<end>"
KO: "떠난다<end>"

## 1340663
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールは、現在、戒厳下にある！<line-break>エリエナイ公が王家をないがしろにする<line-break>ファーロス家を討伐すべく立ったのだ！<end>"
EN: "Rostorl is now under martial law! Duke Elienai has risen to crush House Pharos for its contempt of the royal family!<end>"
KO: "로스톨은 현재 계엄 상태다! 엘리에나이 공이 왕가를 업신여기는 파로스 가문을 토벌하기 위해 일어선 것이다!<end>"

## 1340664
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "毒婦エリスはすでに処刑され、<line-break>奸臣ゼネテスも、まもなく処刑される！<line-break><line-break>邪魔するなら貴様も粛正する！<end>"
EN: "The venomous Eris has already been executed, and the treacherous minister Xenetes will soon follow! Interfere, and you too will be purged!<end>"
KO: "독부 에리스는 이미 처형됐고, 간신 제네테스도 곧 처형된다! 방해한다면 네놈도 숙청하겠다!<end>"

## 1340665
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よいのですか？<line-break>あれは確かゼネテスの側近、<line-break><value:$28>将軍ですぞ？<end>"
EN: "Are you sure that's wise? If I'm not mistaken, that's General <value:$28>, Xenetes's aide.<end>"
KO: "괜찮으십니까? 저자는 분명 제네테스의 측근, <value:$28> 장군입니다만?<end>"

## 1340666
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かまわん。主君の危機に<line-break>しっぽを巻いて逃げる奴など、<line-break>生かしておいても、禍根にはならん。<end>"
EN: "Let it go. Turning tail and fleeing while one's lord is in peril marks a coward as no threat, alive or otherwise.<end>"
KO: "상관없다. 주군의 위기에 꼬리를 말고 도망치는 놈 따윈 살려 둬도 후환이 되지 않아.<end>"

## 1340667
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ファーロス家に連なる者などあの程度。<line-break>しょせんは、権勢目当て<line-break>腰抜けのおべっか使いよ。ガハハ！<end>"
EN: "So that's the caliber of those tied to House Pharos. Nothing but cowardly sycophants chasing power! Gahaha!<end>"
KO: "파로스 가문에 딸린 자들 따윈 그 정도지. 결국 권세만 노리는 겁쟁이 아첨꾼들이야. 가하하!<end>"

## 1340668
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2行き止まりね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>3どうやら、<line-break>坑道はここまでのようだな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4行き止まりみたいですね～。<line-break>ここから飛び降りたら、<line-break>どうなるのかしら？<end><if><value:$01><equal>5おい、ガケみたいだぜ。<line-break>押すなよ、<value:$28>。<line-break> <line-break>こんなガケから落ちたら、<line-break>ケガどころじゃ<line-break>済みそうにねえからな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>6行き止まりまで来ちゃったね。<line-break>でも、この、重苦しい空気は…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>7最深部か…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>8息をつくには早そうですね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>9どーやら、<line-break>ここが終点みたいよん。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12最深部まで来たようだが…<line-break>どうやら、<line-break>安心するには早いようだぜ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…待て。<line-break>息をつくにはまだ早そうだぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、押さないでくれよな！<line-break>…って。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14ふむ、どうやらここが最深部じゃ。<line-break>それにしても、<line-break>この鉱石のさざめきはどうだ…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、ここが一番奥みたいだよ。<line-break>何もないなら、もう引き返して…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17なによ、くたくたに<line-break>なるまで歩かせておいて、<line-break>ドン詰まりじゃないのよお！<line-break>どう責任とって…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>19行き止まりみたいだよねぇ。<line-break>でも…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…待て。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21ここで行き止まりのようだが、<line-break>どうもおかしいぞ…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26来るところまで来たようじゃな。<line-break>しかし…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>11ここが最深部のようね。<line-break>でも…待って！<end><if><value:$01><equal>23…待て。<line-break>息をつくにはまだ早いぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27行き止まり…。<line-break>安心するには早いようだな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>24行き止まりですね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25行き止まりね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192行き止まり…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>…何か感じます。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193ここで行き止まりですけど<line-break>何もいませんね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194行き止まりか。<line-break>…いや、この気配。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191ここが最深部みたいだけど…。<line-break>でも…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190行き止まりだな。<line-break>だが、この重苦しい空気は…。<end>洞窟はここで<line-break>行き止まりみたいだけど…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2A dead end...<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Looks like this is as far as the tunnel goes.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Looks like a dead end. I wonder what would happen if someone jumped down from here?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Hey, looks like a cliff. Don't push me, <value:$28>. Falling from a cliff like this would mean a lot worse than getting hurt.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6We've come all the way to a dead end. But this oppressive air...<end><if><value:$01><equal>7The deepest part...<end><if><value:$01><equal>8It seems a little early to relax.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Well, looks like this is the end of the line.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Looks like we've reached the deepest point... but I wouldn't relax just yet.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13...Wait. It looks like it's too soon to relax.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Hey, lady, don't push me! ...Huh.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Hmm. This appears to be the deepest part. Still, what is that murmuring from the ore...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, it looks like this is as far as it goes. If there's nothing here, let's head back...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17What? You make me walk until I'm exhausted, and all that's here is a dead end! How are you going to make this up to me...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Looks like a dead end, doesn't it? But...<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...Wait.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21It seems to end here, but something isn't right...<end><if><value:$01><equal>26It seems we've come as far as we can. And yet...<end><if><value:$01><equal>11This seems to be the deepest part. But... wait!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23...Wait. It's too soon to relax.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27A dead end... but it's too soon to relax.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24It's a dead end...<end><if><value:$01><equal>25A dead end...<end><if><value:$01><equal>192A dead end... I sense something.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193It's a dead end, but there's nothing here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194A dead end. ...No, this presence...<end><if><value:$01><equal>191It looks like this is the deepest part... But...<end><if><value:$01><equal>190A dead end. But this oppressive air...<end>It looks like the cave ends here, but...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2막다른 길이네….<end><if><value:$01><equal>3아무래도 갱도는 여기까지인 것 같군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4막다른 길인 것 같네요~. 여기서 뛰어내리면 어떻게 될까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5야, 절벽 같은데. 밀지 마, <value:$28>. 이런 절벽에서 떨어지면 다치는 정도로 안 끝난다고.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6막다른 곳까지 왔네. 그런데 이 무거운 공기는….<end><if><value:$01><equal>7최심부인가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>8한숨 돌리긴 이른 것 같네요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9아무래도 여기가 끝인 것 같네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12최심부까지 온 것 같은데… 안심하긴 이른 것 같군?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…기다려. 한숨 돌리긴 아직 이른 것 같다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 밀지 마! …어?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14흠, 여기가 최심부인 듯하군. 그런데 이 광석의 웅성거림은….<end><if><value:$01><equal>15여기가 가장 안쪽인 것 같아. 아무것도 없으면 돌아가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐야, 녹초가 되도록 걸어왔는데 막다른 길이잖아! 어떻게 책임질….<end><if><value:$01><equal>19막다른 길 같네. 그런데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…기다려.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21여기서 막다른 것 같지만, 뭔가 이상하다….<end><if><value:$01><equal>26올 데까지 온 것 같군. 하지만….<end><if><value:$01><equal>11여기가 최심부 같네. 하지만… 잠깐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23…기다려. 한숨 돌리긴 아직 이르다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27막다른 길…. 안심하긴 이른 것 같군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24막다른 길이네요….<end><if><value:$01><equal>25막다른 길이네….<end><if><value:$01><equal>192막다른 길…. …뭔가 느껴집니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193여기서 막다른 길인데 아무것도 없네요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194막다른 길인가. …아니, 이 기척은.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191여기가 최심부인 것 같은데…. 하지만….<end><if><value:$01><equal>190막다른 길이군. 하지만 이 무거운 공기는….<end>동굴은 여기서 막다른 길인 것 같은데….<end>"

## 1340669
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2あ、あれは…！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3ぬ、ぬぬっ、あれは！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>4あらら、きゃっ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5って、ありゃなんだ！？<line-break>冗談じゃねえぞ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6こ、こんなのって！<end><if><value:$01><equal>7ちっ、こんな…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>8来たようですね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>9へ～、こりゃ確かにドえらいわ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>12くっ、こいつは<line-break>滅多にお目にかかれねえ<line-break>大立ち回りになりそうだ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>13山を揺り動かすうめき声…<line-break>ふん、なるほどな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10こ、こんなのってあるかよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14あ、あやつか！<line-break>あやつが原因か！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15う、うわああっ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>17な、なんなのよー、あれはっ！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>19きゃああっ、<value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…来たな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21なるほど、そういうことか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26やはりこやつか！<line-break>こやつが…目覚めておったのか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>11くっ…！<line-break>こんな…巨大な闇が！<end><if><value:$01><equal>23…来たか。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27こ、これは…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>24あ、あれは…！<end><if><value:$01><equal>25あ、あれは…！<end><if><value:$01><equal>192現れたようですね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193あ、あれは…！<end><if><value:$01><equal>194く、やっぱりか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191あれは！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190こいつが原因か！<end>やっぱり、ここで終わりじゃないのか！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Th-that's...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3N-ngh, that's...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Oh dear―eek!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5What the hell is that?! You've got to be kidding me!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Th-this can't be!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7Tch, this is...<end><if><value:$01><equal>8It seems to be here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Well, that really is something!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Gah, this is going to be one hell of a fight―the kind you don't see every day!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13A groan that shakes the mountain... Hmph. That explains it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10H-how can something like this exist?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Th-that thing! Is that what caused all this?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15A-aaaaah!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Wh-what is that?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Aaaah, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...It's here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21So that's what was going on!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26So it was this thing! It... had awakened!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Damn...! Such... immense darkness!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23...So it has come.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Th-this is...<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Th-that's...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Th-that's...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192It seems to have appeared.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Th-that's...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Gah, I knew it!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191That's...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190So this is what caused it!<end>So this isn't the end after all!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2아, 저건…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3으, 으으, 저건!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4어머나, 꺅!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5저게 뭐야!? 장난 아니잖아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6이, 이런 게!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7칫, 이런….<end><if><value:$01><equal>8나타난 모양이군요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9오, 이거 정말 엄청난데!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12큭, 이건 좀처럼 보기 힘든 대난전이 되겠는데!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13산을 뒤흔드는 신음 소리… 흥, 과연.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10이런 게 말이 돼!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14저, 저놈인가! 저놈이 원인이었나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15우, 우와아아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 뭐야 저건ー!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19꺄아아, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…왔군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21과연, 그런 거였나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26역시 이놈인가! 이놈이… 깨어나 있었던 건가!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11큭…! 이런… 거대한 어둠이!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23…왔나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이, 이건….<end><if><value:$01><equal>24아, 저건…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25아, 저건…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192나타난 모양이군요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193아, 저건…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194큭, 역시나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191저건!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190이놈이 원인이었나!<end>역시 이걸로 끝이 아니었던 건가!<end>"

## 1340670
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　 よくぞ来たな…　　<line-break>　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。　<end>"
EN: "You have done well to come... Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다… 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여….<end>"

## 1340671
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　 ククク…笑止！　　　<line-break>　　　汝が我を討つだと…？　　　<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... How absurd! You would slay me...?<end>"
KO: "크크크… 가소롭군! 네놈이 나를 쓰러뜨리겠다고…?<end>"

## 1340672
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　これこそ我が幸運に他ならぬ…！　　<line-break> <line-break> <line-break> <line-break>　　　　　　悠久の眠りにより衰えた　　　<line-break>　　　　我が身にとって、無限のソウルほど　　<line-break>　　　　　　優れた糧はありはせぬ…。　　　<end>"
EN: "What extraordinary good fortune...! For this body, weakened by an eternity of Sleep, there can be no finer sustenance than the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "이것이야말로 나의 행운이 아니겠는가…! 영겁의 잠으로 쇠약해진 이 몸에 무한의 소울만큼 훌륭한 양식은 없지….<end>"

## 1340673
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　肉を引き裂き、骨をしゃぶり、　　<line-break>　　　　　魂まですすってくれよう…！　　<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>　　　　　我はイシュバアル…すなわち破滅…！　<line-break>　　　　　　　　破壊神により生を受け…　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　この世に死をもたらす者なり…！　<end>"
EN: "I shall tear your flesh, suck the marrow from your bones, and drink down even your soul...! I am Ishbaal... ruin itself...! Given life by the God of Destruction, I am the one who brings death to this world...!<end>"
KO: "살을 찢고, 뼈를 핥고, 영혼까지 빨아들여 주마…! 나는 이슈바알… 곧 파멸…! 파괴신에게서 생명을 받아… 이 세상에 죽음을 가져오는 자다…!<end>"

## 1340674
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　　　　バカな…！　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　ひ、人の子ごときに敗れようとは…！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>　　　　　　　　　　終わらぬぞ…。　　　<line-break>　　　　　　こ、これしきでは…終わらぬ！　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　グオオオオオオオッ！　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Impossible...! To think I could be defeated by a m-mere human...! It is not over... S-such a trifle... will not be my end! GRAAAAAAAAAH!<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…! 겨, 겨우 인간의 아이 따위에게 패하다니…! 끝나지 않았다…. 이, 이 정도로는… 끝나지 않아! 그오오오오오오옷!<end>"

## 1340676
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "門の方に向かったぞ！<line-break>追え！<line-break>殺しても構わん！<end>"
EN: "The intruder's heading for the gate! Give chase! Lethal force is authorized!<end>"
KO: "문 쪽으로 갔다! 쫓아라! 죽여도 상관없다!<end>"

## 1340677
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님!<end>"

## 1340678
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "門の方に向かったぞ！<line-break>追え！<line-break>殺しても構わん！<end>"
EN: "The intruder's heading for the gate! Give chase! Lethal force is authorized!<end>"
KO: "문 쪽으로 갔다! 쫓아라! 죽여도 상관없다!<end>"

## 1340679
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、おかげで復興の方も<line-break>軌道に乗ってまいりました。<line-break>人の笑顔はいいものですね。<end>"
EN: "I see... Oh, and the reconstruction is well under way now. It is good to see people smiling again.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까. 아, 덕분에 부흥도 궤도에 오르고 있습니다. 사람들의 웃는 얼굴은 좋군요.<end>"

## 1340680
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様も<line-break>復興にはひとかたならぬ<line-break>お力を与えてくださっています。<line-break>エルファス様は、ロストールだけでなく<line-break>世界中の都市で復興のため働いて、<line-break>神のようにあがめられているのです。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas has also been a tremendous help with the rebuilding. He is working to restore cities not only in Rostorl, but all over the world, and people worship him like a god.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님도 부흥에 큰 힘을 보태 주고 계십니다. 엘파스 님은 로스톨뿐 아니라 세계 각지의 도시에서 부흥을 위해 일하며 신처럼 숭배받고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1340681
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の死に慣れてしまったのでしょうか。<line-break>幼いときから一緒にいた方の死なのに<line-break>感慨がありません…。<line-break>でも、安らかなお顔でしたね。<end>"
EN: "Have I grown accustomed to death? Someone who had been with me since childhood is dead, yet I feel nothing... Still, his face looked peaceful.<end>"
KO: "사람의 죽음에 익숙해진 걸까요. 어릴 때부터 함께한 분의 죽음인데도 아무 느낌이 없어요…. 그래도 편안한 얼굴이셨네요.<end>"

## 1340682
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様も思えば、<line-break>おかわいそうな方です。<line-break>　<line-break>何かにつけて、いとこの<line-break>レムオン様、エスト様に比べられ、<line-break>あげつらわれてきたのですから。<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, Lord Tartuba was a pitiable man. He was forever being compared with his cousins, Lord Lemghon and Lord Est, and criticized for falling short.<end>"
KO: "생각해보면 타르튜바 님도 참 가엾은 분입니다. 사사건건 사촌인 레무온 님, 에스트 님과 비교당하고 비난받아왔으니까요.<end>"

## 1340683
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様も貴族に生まれなければ…<line-break>このような悲しいことには<line-break>ならなかったのかもしれません…。<end>"
EN: "Had Lord Tartuba not been born a noble... perhaps none of this would have ended so sadly.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님도 귀족으로 태어나지 않았다면… 이렇게 슬픈 일은 없었을지도 모릅니다….<end>"

## 1340684
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…<line-break>少しだけ、一人にさせてください…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... please, let me be alone for a little while.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 잠시만 혼자 있게 해 주세요….<end>"

## 1340685
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様…。<end>"
EN: "Lord Tartuba...<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님….<end>"

## 1340686
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>無限のソウルについて<line-break>俺なりに考えてみたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I've been doing some thinking of my own about the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 무한의 소울에 대해 나름대로 생각해 봤다.<end>"

## 1340687
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1340688
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、寝室の方に…。<end>"
EN: "Then, please come to the bedchamber...<end>"
KO: "그럼 침실 쪽으로….<end>"

## 1340689
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がらにもないご託を並べたもんだから<line-break>舌がツっちまったよ。<line-break>分を越えることはするもんじゃねえな。<end>"
EN: "I started spouting all that high-minded nonsense, which isn't like me, and my tongue cramped up. That's what I get for overreaching.<end>"
KO: "나답지 않게 어려운 소리를 늘어놨더니 혀가 꼬였네. 분수에 넘는 짓은 하는 게 아니군.<end>"

## 1340690
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…あの方からは<line-break>かすかですが、闇の気配を<line-break>感じるのです。<line-break>私はそれが少し気にかかるのです…<end>"
EN: "But... faint though it is, I sense the presence of darkness about him. That troubles me somewhat...<end>"
KO: "하지만… 그분에게서 희미하긴 해도 어둠의 기운이 느껴집니다. 그게 조금 마음에 걸려요….<end>"

## 1340691
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、終末が来るとおっしゃって、<line-break>みなの不安感をあおっているのが、<line-break>ティアナには気にかかるのですが…<end>"
EN: "Still, Tiana cannot help but worry that his warnings of the coming end are feeding everyone's fears...<end>"
KO: "다만 종말이 온다고 말씀하시며 모두의 불안감을 부추기고 있는 점이 티아나는 마음에 걸립니다만…<end>"

## 1340692
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、行くぜ、ナッジ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, Nadge. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "자, 가자, 나지.<end>"

## 1340693
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先日、ディンガル皇帝ネメアが<line-break>ウルカーンまで来おってな。<end>"
EN: "The other day, Nemea, Emperor of Dyneskal, came all the way to Ulkarne.<end>"
KO: "얼마 전 딘갈 황제 네메아가 울칸까지 왔었지.<end>"

## 1340694
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙れ、わき役！<line-break>俺様の英雄伝説にお前の出番はない。<line-break>俺様の世界に、お前の居場所はないっ！<end>"
EN: "Silence, bit player! You have no part in my heroic legend. There is no place for you in my world!<end>"
KO: "닥쳐, 조연! 나님의 영웅전설에 네 차례는 없다. 나님의 세계에 네 자리는 없어!<end>"

## 1340695
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3な、何を言っとるんじゃ？<line-break>こやつは…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4この人、相当…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>5なにぉおおおおお！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6時間がないんだ！<line-break>お願い！<end><if><value:$01><equal>13………………。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14何を言うとるか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…ダメだ。<line-break>やっぱり話が通じないや。<line-break>早く終わってくれないかなあ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17ちょっとあんた、ナ・ニ・サ・マ！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ゼネテスが！<line-break>ゼネテスが死んじゃうよぉ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>20………………。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26なるほど、面白い御仁のようじゃな。<line-break>ま、人間若いころは<line-break>まま、こういうことをしがちじゃわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>11そうね。その方がありがたいわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…かわいそうに、<line-break>事ここに至っては、もう…<line-break>眠りの神パーシィよ、彼にやすらぎを。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192…はい、居場所などありません。<line-break>私は役割を失った道具ですから…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…うぷ。<line-break>ダメだ…。限界だ…。<line-break>気持ちが悪い…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191今の私、悪い冗談にも<line-break>過剰に対応してしまいそうなの。<line-break>もう一度言うわ。どいて。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190おい、ガキ。<line-break>俺は前のクーデターで<line-break>ある人と出会い、救われた。<line-break>今日、その人が死んだ。<line-break>その人の甥まで殺させやしない。<line-break>邪魔するなら…死ぬほど後悔させる。<end>ああ…。どうして、<line-break>こんな時にこんな奴が<line-break>出てきちゃうんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3W-What are you talking about? This fellow...<end><if><value:$01><equal>4This man is really...<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Whaaaaaat?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6We don't have time! Please!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13...<end><if><value:$01><equal>14What nonsense are you spouting?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15It's no use. I knew there was no reasoning with him. I wish he'd just get this over with.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Just who do you think you are!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Xenetes! Xenetes is going to die!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...<end><if><value:$01><equal>26I see. He seems an amusing fellow. Well, humans are often prone to this sort of behavior in their youth.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Yes. I'd be grateful for that.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Poor man. Now that it has come to this, there is nothing more... O Percy, god of Sleep, grant him peace.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Yes... There is no place for me. I am merely a tool that has lost its role.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Urp... I can't... I've reached my limit... I'm going to be sick.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Right now, I may overreact even to a bad joke. I'll say it once more: move.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Listen, brat. During the last coup, I met someone who saved me. Today, that person died. I won't let that person's nephew die too. Get in my way... and I'll make you regret it so much you'll wish you were dead.<end>Oh... Why did someone like this have to show up now, of all times?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3무, 무슨 소릴 하는 게냐? 이 녀석은….<end><if><value:$01><equal>4이 사람, 상당히….<end><if><value:$01><equal>5뭐어어어어어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6시간이 없어! 부탁이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>14무슨 소릴 하는 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…안 되겠어. 역시 말이 안 통해. 빨리 끝났으면 좋겠는데.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17잠깐 너, 뭐 하는 자야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19제네테스가! 제네테스가 죽는다고오!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>26과연, 재미있는 분인 모양이군. 뭐, 젊을 때는 종종 이런 일을 하곤 하지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11그래. 그쪽이 고맙겠네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…가엾군. 일이 이 지경에 이르렀으니 이제는…. 잠의 신 파시여, 그에게 안식을.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192…네, 제가 있을 곳 따윈 없습니다. 저는 역할을 잃은 도구니까요….<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…욱. 안 돼…. 한계다…. 속이 안 좋아….<end><if><value:$01><equal>191지금의 난 나쁜 농담에도 과민하게 반응할 것 같아. 다시 말할게. 비켜.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190어이, 꼬마. 나는 지난 쿠데타에서 어떤 사람을 만나 구원받았다. 오늘 그 사람이 죽었다. 그 사람의 조카까지 죽게 두진 않겠다. 방해한다면… 죽을 만큼 후회하게 해 주지.<end>아아…. 왜 하필 이런 때 이런 녀석이 나오는 거야아.<end>"

## 1340696
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨….<end>"

## 1340697
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それでも、なかなか…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お恥ずかしながら、資金の不足から、<line-break>手が回らないところが<line-break>たくさん出てきてしまっています…。<end>"
EN: "But even with that, we're still struggling. I'm ashamed to admit that our lack of funds has left many areas beyond our reach.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그래도 쉽지가 않네요…. 부끄럽지만 자금이 부족해서 손이 미치지 못하는 곳이 많이 생기고 있습니다….<end>"

## 1340698
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec134-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク用メッセージ終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질라크용 메시지 끝<end>"

## 1350000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク、ボリューム４開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질라크 볼륨 4 시작<end>"

## 1350001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知っていると思うが、<line-break>俺とエストは母親が違う。<line-break>実の母はダルケニスだった。<line-break>義母はダルケニスの俺に<line-break>よくしてくれ、俺を当主にまでした。<line-break>実の子のエストを差し置いてな。<end>"
EN: "You may already know this, but Est and I had different mothers. My birth mother was a Darkenith. My stepmother treated me well despite my being Darkenith, and even made me head of the family instead of her own son, Est.<end>"
KO: "알고 있겠지만 나와 에스트는 어머니가 달라. 친어머니는 다르케니스였지. 새어머니는 다르케니스인 나에게 잘해 주었고 나를 당주 자리까지 올려 줬어. 친아들 에스트를 제쳐 두고 말이야.<end>"

## 1350002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺がダルケニスであることなど関係なく<line-break>義母は、俺のことを才能があると<line-break>よく、はげましてくれた。<line-break>その義母が死ぬとき、<line-break>初めて実の母の消息を話してくれた。<line-break>母が冒険者に討たれたことを…。<end>"
EN: "My stepmother never cared that I was Darkenith. She often encouraged me, saying I had talent. Only as she lay dying did she finally tell me what had become of my birth mother: adventurers had slain her.<end>"
KO: "내가 다르케니스라는 건 상관없이 양어머니는 내게 재능이 있다며 자주 격려해 줬다. 그 양어머니가 죽을 때 처음으로 친어머니의 소식을 말해 줬지. 어머니가 모험자에게 죽었다는 걸….<end>"

## 1350003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "義母は、ダルケニスだからと<line-break>俺が自信をなくすのを恐れて、<line-break>死の間際まで言い出せなかったのだ。<line-break>だが、俺にはそんな義母が偽善に思えた。<line-break>そして、俺は義母の心配に違わず<line-break>心を閉ざした、かたくなな男になった。<end>"
EN: "My stepmother feared I would lose faith in myself because I was Darkenith, so she kept silent until her dying breath. But her concern struck me as hypocrisy. And just as she had feared, I closed my heart and became a bitter, obstinate man.<end>"
KO: "양어머니는 내가 다르케니스라는 이유로 자신감을 잃을까 두려워 죽기 직전까지 말하지 못했다. 하지만 나는 그런 양어머니가 위선적으로 보였다. 그리고 나는 그 걱정대로 마음을 닫은 완고한 남자가 됐지.<end>"

## 1350004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前に会うまでは、な…<end>"
EN: "Until I met you...<end>"
KO: "너를 만나기 전까진 말이지….<end>"

## 1350005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダルケニスだからというだけで<line-break>無条件に殺される、そんな世の中で<line-break>愛や正義を語る奴が気に入らなかった。<line-break>無論、冒険者もな。<line-break>貴様のような正義ぶった<line-break>甘っちょろい冒険者は大嫌いだった。<end>"
EN: "In a world where one could be killed without question merely for being Darkenith, I despised anyone who spoke of love or justice. Adventurers included, of course. I especially hated naive, self-righteous adventurers like you.<end>"
KO: "다르케니스라는 이유만으로 무조건 죽임당하는 세상에서 사랑이니 정의니 떠드는 놈들이 마음에 들지 않았다. 물론 모험자도 마찬가지였지. 너처럼 정의로운 척하는 물러터진 모험자를 정말 싫어했다.<end>"

## 1350006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なのに、妙な話だな。<line-break>貴様のような甘っちょろい冒険者に<line-break>俺は救われている。<line-break>貴様はつまらない正義感の持ち主なのに<line-break>ダルケニスの俺を殺すどころか<line-break>自分を犠牲にして助けてくれている。<end>"
EN: "And yet, how strange. I have been saved by just such a naive adventurer. For all your petty sense of justice, instead of killing me for being Darkenith, you have sacrificed yourself to help me.<end>"
KO: "…그런데도 참 묘한 일이지. 너처럼 물러터진 모험자에게 내가 구원받고 있으니. 너는 시시한 정의감의 소유자인데도 다르케니스인 나를 죽이기는커녕 자신을 희생해서 도와주고 있다.<end>"

## 1350007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結局、自分ひとりで<line-break>こだわって<line-break>世界をゆがめていたんだな。<end>"
EN: "In the end, I was the only one clinging to it, twisting the world to fit my view.<end>"
KO: "결국 혼자 집착해서 세계를 일그러뜨리고 있었던 거구나.<end>"

## 1350008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "教えてくれ<line-break>あの晩のことをゼネテスが<line-break>知っているようだったのは、<line-break>奴があそこにいたからなのだろう？<line-break>そして貴様と一緒に、<line-break>俺を救ったからなのだろう？<end>"
EN: "Tell me. Xenetes seemed to know what happened that night because he was there, didn't he? Because he helped you save me?<end>"
KO: "말해 줘. 그날 밤 일을 제네테스가 알고 있었던 건 녀석이 거기 있었기 때문이지? 그리고 너와 함께 나를 구했기 때문이지?<end>"

## 1350009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。<end>"
EN: "So that's how it was.<end>"
KO: "…그런가.<end>"

## 1350010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…仲間に入れてくれ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>冒険者になって、<line-break>お前とともに歩きたい、そして<line-break>あの男が見てきた世界を見たいのだ。<end>"
EN: "Let me join you. I want to become an adventurer and walk beside you. I want to see the world that man saw.<end>"
KO: "…동료로 받아 줘. 모험자가 되어 너와 함께 걷고 싶다. 그리고 그 남자가 보았던 세계를 보고 싶어.<end>"

## 1350011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼を言う、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "You have my thanks, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "고맙다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1350012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、では、その猫屋敷とやらで<line-break>声がかかるのを待てばよいのだな？<end>"
EN: "I see. Then I should wait at this Cat Manor of yours until I am called for, yes?<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 그럼 그 고양이 저택이라는 곳에서 연락이 오길 기다리면 되는 거군?<end>"

## 1350013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いろいろなことが起きた…。<line-break>これから俺にも整理せねばならん考えが<line-break>いろいろと浮かんでくるのかもしれん。<line-break>それらをまとめつつ、<line-break>冒険への旅立ちを待つとしよう。<line-break>では、先に行く。<end>"
EN: "Much has happened. I expect many thoughts will come to me now, thoughts I must put in order. I shall sort through them as I await the day we set out. I will go on ahead.<end>"
KO: "여러 가지 일이 있었지…. 이제 나도 정리해야 할 생각들이 이것저것 떠오를지도 모르겠군. 그것들을 정리하면서 모험을 떠날 때를 기다리도록 하지. 그럼 먼저 가겠다.<end>"

## 1350014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あのとき、<line-break>俺がダルケニスとして覚醒したとき<line-break>貴様もいたのか、ゼネテス。<end>"
EN: "When I awakened as a Darkenith... you were there too, Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "…그때 내가 다르케니스로 각성했을 때 네놈도 거기 있었던 건가, 제네테스.<end>"

## 1350015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んー、まあ、な。<end>"
EN: "Mm... yeah, I was.<end>"
KO: "음, 뭐, 그렇지.<end>"

## 1350016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そして、俺を救った。<line-break>今もだ…<line-break>なぜだ？<end>"
EN: "And you saved me. You still are... Why?<end>"
KO: "…그리고 나를 구했지. 지금도 그렇고…. 왜지?<end>"

## 1350017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜって？<end>"
EN: "Why, you ask?<end>"
KO: "왜냐고?<end>"

## 1350018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はファーロス家とは敵対していた。<line-break>お前たちを滅ぼそうとした。<line-break>いや、それ以前に俺はダルケニスだ。<end>"
EN: "I was an enemy of House Pharos. I tried to destroy all of you. More than that, I am Darkenith.<end>"
KO: "나는 파로스 가문과 적대했다. 너희를 멸망시키려 했다. 아니, 그 이전에 나는 다르케니스다.<end>"

## 1350019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん。<line-break>そりゃ、見解の相違だな。<line-break> <line-break>俺には、お前さんが<line-break>助けを求めてるような気がしたんだ。<line-break>それだけさ。<end>"
EN: "Huh. I suppose we saw things differently. It seemed to me like you were asking for help. That's all.<end>"
KO: "흐음. 그건 견해 차이군. 내 눈에는 네가 도움을 청하는 것처럼 보였어. 그뿐이야.<end>"

## 1350020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "許してくれ…とは言わぬ。<line-break>許されることではないからな。<line-break>ただ、一緒に旅をさせてくれ。<line-break>…俺も、<line-break>お前たちの見てきた世界が<line-break>見たいのだ。<end>"
EN: "I will not ask you to forgive me. What I did cannot be forgiven. Just let me travel with you. I too want to see the world you have seen.<end>"
KO: "용서해 달라고는 하지 않겠다. 용서받을 일이 아니니까. 다만 함께 여행하게 해 줘. …나도 너희가 봐 온 세상을 보고 싶다.<end>"

## 1350021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいだろ。<line-break>なんも、問題はねえ。<line-break>そうだろ、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Sure. I don't see any problem. Right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "괜찮잖아. 아무 문제도 없어. 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1350022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、すべてよし。<line-break>んじゃ、ま、晴れて出直しってことで…<line-break>俺はゼネテス、冒険者だ。<line-break>これからよろしく頼むぜ。<line-break>冒険者<value:$28>、<line-break>そして、冒険者レムオンよ。<end>"
EN: "Good, then it's settled. A fresh start all around. I'm Xenetes, adventurer. Glad to have you with us, Adventurer <value:$28> and Adventurer Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 전부 좋아. 그럼 새로 시작하는 셈 치고… 나는 제네테스, 모험자다. 앞으로 잘 부탁한다. 모험자 <value:$28>, 그리고 모험자 레무온.<end>"

## 1350023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ああ。<line-break>よろしく頼む、<line-break>冒険者、ゼネテス。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes. Likewise... Adventurer Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "아, 그래. 잘 부탁한다, 모험자 제네테스.<end>"

## 1350024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それと、だ。<end>"
EN: "One more thing.<end>"
KO: "그리고 말이야.<end>"

## 1350026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確か、<value:$28>たちは、<line-break>賢者の森の猫屋敷を中継に<line-break>してんだったな。<end>"
EN: "If I remember right, <value:$28>, you and your companions use the Cat Manor in Sage's Forest as a way station.<end>"
KO: "분명 <value:$28> 일행은 현자의 숲 고양이 저택을 중계지로 쓰고 있었지.<end>"

## 1350027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたは先に行っててくれ、<line-break>レムオン。<end>"
EN: "You go on ahead, Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "넌 먼저 가 있어, 레무온.<end>"

## 1350028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういうことだ？<end>"
EN: "What do you mean?<end>"
KO: "무슨 뜻이지?<end>"

## 1350029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういう…って、あんただって俺の<line-break>性分は知ってんだろ？　我ながら、<line-break>まっすぐ向かう保証はないもんでな。<end>"
EN: "What do I mean? You know what I'm like. Even I can't promise I'll go straight there.<end>"
KO: "무슨 뜻이냐니, 너도 내 성격은 알잖아? 나도 내가 곧장 갈 거라고 장담은 못 하거든.<end>"

## 1350030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだったな…。<line-break>その身体では、すぐには<line-break>冒険とはいかんだろうな…。<end>"
EN: "Of course... In your condition, you cannot return to adventuring immediately.<end>"
KO: "그랬지…. 그 몸으로는 당장 모험하긴 어렵겠군….<end>"

## 1350031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、やだねえ！<line-break>そう辛気くせえ顔されたって、<line-break>ちっともよくはならねえよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, spare me! Putting on such a gloomy face won't make me any better!<end>"
KO: "아아, 싫다 정말! 그렇게 죽상 쓰고 있어 봐야 하나도 나아지지 않아!<end>"

## 1350032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必要なのは、栄養と休養、<line-break>危うく踏みとどまったシャバの<line-break>うまい酒と…<line-break>起きたコトの、後腐れない決着だ。<end>"
EN: "What I need is food and rest, some good drink here in the world of the living I nearly left behind... and a clean end to everything that's happened.<end>"
KO: "필요한 건 영양과 휴식, 간신히 목숨 붙이고 돌아온 세상의 맛있는 술과… 벌어진 일의 뒤끝 없는 결판이지.<end>"

## 1350033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何にせよ、<value:$28>…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お前が俺の力を必要とする時までにゃ、<line-break>ちゃんと万全にしとくから、<line-break>安心しなって。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, <value:$28>, I'll be back in fighting shape by the time you need me. Don't worry.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 <value:$28>…. 네가 내 힘을 필요로 할 때까지는 제대로 완벽하게 준비해 둘 테니 안심해.<end>"

## 1350034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "See you around, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이만, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1350035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あっけないもんだな。<end>"
EN: "It ends so easily.<end>"
KO: "…허무하네.<end>"

## 1350036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、人が死んだな。<line-break>俺は何をやってたんだか…。<end>"
EN: "Someone else is dead. What the hell was I doing?<end>"
KO: "또 사람이 죽었군. 난 대체 뭘 하고 있었던 거지….<end>"

## 1350037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺なら、なんとかなったって<line-break>思うほど、傲慢じゃないさ。<line-break>ただ責任も感じないほど図太くもない。<line-break>シャリはティアナが欲しかったんだ。<line-break>そして、俺はみすみす<line-break>奴にティアナを渡してしまった…。<end>"
EN: "I'm not arrogant enough to think I could have fixed everything. But I'm not so callous that I feel no responsibility. Shalli wanted Tiana, and I let him take her right from under my nose.<end>"
KO: "내가 있었다면 어떻게든 됐을 거라 생각할 만큼 오만하진 않아. 그렇다고 책임을 느끼지 않을 만큼 뻔뻔하지도 않고. 샤리는 티아나를 원했어. 그리고 난 뻔히 보고도 녀석에게 티아나를 넘겨주고 말았지….<end>"

## 1350038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリがいったい何をたくらんで<line-break>こんなことをしたのかは<line-break>わからない。<line-break>だが、こんなにあっさりと<line-break>ロストールを捨てたからには<line-break>もっと、でかい何かが目的なはずだ。<end>"
EN: "I don't know what Shalli was plotting when he did this. But if he abandoned Rostorl so readily, he must be after something much bigger.<end>"
KO: "샤리가 대체 무슨 꿍꿍이로 이런 짓을 했는지는 모르겠어. 하지만 이렇게 쉽게 로스톨을 버린 걸 보면 더 큰 무언가가 목적일 거야.<end>"

## 1350039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>一緒に旅に連れていってくれ。<line-break>頼む…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, let me travel with you. Please.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 나도 함께 여행에 데려가 줘. 부탁이야….<end>"

## 1350040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確か、<value:$28>たちは、<line-break>賢者の森の猫屋敷を中継に<line-break>してんだったな。<end>"
EN: "If I remember right, <value:$28>, you and your companions use the Cat Manor in Sage's Forest as a way station.<end>"
KO: "분명 <value:$28> 일행은 현자의 숲 고양이 저택을 중계지로 쓰고 있었지.<end>"

## 1350041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が俺の力を必要とする時までにゃ、<line-break>ちゃんと万全にしとくから、<line-break>安心しなって。<line-break>じゃあな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'll be back in fighting shape by the time you need me, so don't worry. See you around, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "네가 내 힘을 필요로 할 때까지는 제대로 회복해 둘 테니 걱정 마. 그럼 또 보자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1350042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル軍なら大丈夫だ。<line-break>カルラはロストールに攻めてこられない。<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal Army won't be a problem. Karula can't attack Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "딘갈군이라면 괜찮다. 칼라는 로스톨을 공격할 수 없어.<end>"

## 1350043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国宰相殿の目線で考えてみな。<line-break>カルラにロストールを占領させるなんて<line-break>虎に翼を与えるようなもんだ。<line-break>ロストールが無力化しているのなら<line-break>カルラに軍を進めさせるより<line-break>有利に講和したほうがよっぽどいい。<end>"
EN: "Look at it from the Imperial Chancellor's point of view. Letting Karula occupy Rostorl would be like giving wings to a tiger. If Rostorl has already been neutralized, it makes far more sense to negotiate peace from a position of strength than to let Karula advance.<end>"
KO: "제국 재상의 눈으로 생각해 봐. 카를라에게 로스톨을 점령하게 한다는 건 호랑이에게 날개를 달아 주는 꼴이야. 로스톨이 무력화됐다면 카를라를 진군시키기보다 유리한 조건으로 강화하는 편이 훨씬 낫지.<end>"

## 1350044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1350045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあな。カルラほどの戦略眼を持つ将が<line-break>そのへん無視してまで、俺たちを完全に<line-break>潰そうとした理由まではわからないね。<end>"
EN: "Who knows? I can't say why a commander with Karula's strategic eye would ignore all that just to wipe us out completely.<end>"
KO: "글쎄. 카를라 정도의 전략안을 가진 장수가 그런 것까지 무시해 가며 우리를 완전히 없애려 한 이유까지는 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1350046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、ロストールもメチャクチャだ。<line-break>問題は山積み。<line-break>これから立て直しが大変だな。<end>"
EN: "What a mess. Rostorl is in complete disarray too, and the problems are piling up. Rebuilding it won't be easy.<end>"
KO: "이런, 로스톨도 엉망이군. 문제는 산더미야. 앞으로 재건하기 힘들겠어.<end>"

## 1350047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だがな。もし、お前さんが<line-break>俺の力を必要とするならば<line-break>いつでも呼び出してくれ。<end>"
EN: "Still, if you ever need my help, call for me.<end>"
KO: "하지만 말이야. 만약 네가 내 힘이 필요하다면 언제든 불러 줘.<end>"

## 1350048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺には、どうしても<line-break>お前さんが関わっていることのほうが<line-break>大事だって思えるんでね。<end>"
EN: "Somehow, anything involving you seems more important to me.<end>"
KO: "내겐 아무래도 네가 관련된 일이 더 중요하게 느껴져서 말이야.<end>"

## 1350049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確か、<value:$28>たちは、<line-break>賢者の森の猫屋敷を中継に<line-break>してんだったな。<end>"
EN: "If I remember right, <value:$28>, you and your companions use the Cat Manor in Sage's Forest as a way station.<end>"
KO: "분명 <value:$28> 일행은 현자의 숲 고양이 저택을 중계지로 쓰고 있었지.<end>"

## 1350050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺のことはよく知ってんだろ？<line-break>我ながら、まっすぐ向かう保証は<line-break>ないもんでな。<end>"
EN: "You know me well enough. Even I can't promise I'll go straight there.<end>"
KO: "나를 잘 알잖아? 나도 내가 곧장 갈 거란 보장은 못 하겠거든.<end>"

## 1350051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、やだねえ！<line-break>そう辛気くせえ顔されたって、<line-break>ちっともよくはならねえよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, spare me! Putting on such a gloomy face won't make me any better!<end>"
KO: "아아, 싫다 정말! 그렇게 죽상 쓰고 있어 봐야 하나도 나아지지 않아!<end>"

## 1350052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危うく踏みとどまったシャバの<line-break>うまい酒と…<line-break> <line-break>起きたコトがコトだ、途中で寄らなきゃ<line-break>ならねえところもあるだろうしな。<end>"
EN: "Some good drink here in the world of the living I nearly left behind... And after everything that's happened, there are probably places I'll need to stop along the way.<end>"
KO: "간신히 남아 있는 속세의 맛있는 술도 있고… 일이 이렇게 됐으니 가는 길에 들러야 할 곳도 있겠지.<end>"

## 1350053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、何にせよ、お前が俺の力を<line-break>必要とする時まで、ちゃんと<line-break>万全にしとくから、安心しなって。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, I'll be back in fighting shape by the time you need me. Don't worry.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 아무튼 네가 내 힘을 필요로 할 때까지 완벽하게 준비해 둘 테니 안심해.<end>"

## 1350054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "See you around, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이만, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1350055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アレ、返してくれ。<line-break>ほら、アレだよ、アレ。<end>"
EN: "Give me that thing back. You know, that thing.<end>"
KO: "그거 돌려줘. 있잖아, 그거 말이야, 그거.<end>"

## 1350056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遺書だよ、俺の。<line-break>こうしてちゃんと足があんだから、<line-break>いらねえだろう？<end>"
EN: "My will. I'm still standing here on my own two feet, so you don't need it, do you?<end>"
KO: "유서야, 내 거. 이렇게 멀쩡히 발이 붙어 있으니 필요 없잖아?<end>"

## 1350057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>ゼネテスの遺書を手渡した。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> handed over Xenetes' Last Will.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 제네테스의 유서를 건넸다.<end>"

## 1350058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間、追いつめられるとダメだね。<line-break>こいつは、<line-break>あとで念入りに処分しなきゃな。<end>"
EN: "People really lose their heads when they're cornered. I'll have to make very sure this gets destroyed later.<end>"
KO: "인간은 몰리면 안 되네. 이 녀석은 나중에 철저히 처리해야겠어.<end>"

## 1350059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>助け出していただいて<line-break>本当にありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you so very much for rescuing me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 구해 주셔서 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1350060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これから、ですか？<line-break>はい。実はずっとやりたいと<line-break>考えていたことがあるのです。<end>"
EN: "What will I do now? Well, there is something I have long wanted to do.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 말인가요? 네. 사실 계속 해 보고 싶다고 생각한 일이 있어요.<end>"

## 1350061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様に出会って<line-break>私、ロストールの人々ともっと<line-break>仲良くなりたいと思っておりました。<line-break>二度の兵乱とこの度の内乱で<line-break>ロストールも大きな痛手を受け、<line-break>困っている人も多いことでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Ever since meeting you, <value:$28>, I have wanted to grow closer to the people of Rostorl. Two wars and this latest civil conflict have dealt Rostorl a grievous blow. Many people must be suffering.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님을 만나고 나서 저는 로스톨 사람들과 더 가까워지고 싶다고 생각했습니다. 두 차례의 전란과 이번 내란으로 로스톨도 큰 피해를 입었으니 어려움에 처한 사람도 많겠지요.<end>"

## 1350062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こういう方を助け、広く人とふれあい<line-break>これからのロストールについて<line-break>考えていきたいと思っています。<end>"
EN: "I want to help those people, meet people from all walks of life, and consider what Rostorl's future should be.<end>"
KO: "이런 분들을 돕고 널리 사람들과 어울리면서 앞으로의 로스톨에 대해 생각해 가고 싶습니다.<end>"

## 1350063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リューガ邸の一室を、ロストール復興の<line-break>本拠として提供していただけないか、<line-break>エスト様にお願いしようと思うのです。<line-break>破壊もまぬがれたようですし、<line-break>何より、王宮にいるよりも<line-break>町の方々の声がよく届くはずですもの。<end>"
EN: "I intend to ask Lord Est to provide a room in the Ryuga residence as a base for Rostorl's reconstruction. It appears to have escaped destruction, and above all, the voices of the townspeople will reach me far more readily there than at the palace.<end>"
KO: "류가 저택의 방 하나를 로스톨 부흥의 거점으로 제공해 주실 수 없는지 에스트 님께 부탁드리려 합니다. 파괴도 피한 것 같고, 무엇보다 왕궁보다 마을 사람들의 목소리가 잘 닿을 테니까요.<end>"

## 1350064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様にも<line-break>助けていただきたいことが<line-break>多々出てくると思います。<line-break>お忙しいとは存じますが<line-break>お時間があればお寄りください。<end>"
EN: "I expect there will be much I must ask of you as well, <value:$28>. I know you are busy, but please visit when you have the time.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님께도 도움을 부탁드릴 일이 많이 생길 겁니다. 바쁘시겠지만 시간이 되시면 들러 주세요.<end>"

## 1350065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>助け出していただいて<line-break>本当にありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you so very much for rescuing me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 구해 주셔서 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1350066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これから…ですか？<line-break>具体的なことは考えていないのですが<line-break>王都のみなさんのために何かしようと。<line-break>王都もこの事件で荒れてしまいました。<line-break>王家の人間として、みなさんのために<line-break>率先して事に当たらないと。<end>"
EN: "What will I do now? I have no specific plans, but I want to do something for everyone in the capital. This incident has left the city in ruins. As a member of the royal family, I must take the lead in helping its people.<end>"
KO: "앞으로… 말인가요? 구체적인 건 생각하지 않았지만 왕도의 여러분을 위해 무언가 하려고 합니다. 왕도도 이번 사건으로 황폐해졌어요. 왕가의 사람으로서 여러분을 위해 앞장서야 합니다.<end>"

## 1350067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…王女ですもの。<line-break>そう言わないといけないんですよね…<line-break>きっと。<end>"
EN: "I am a princess, after all. That is what I am supposed to say... surely.<end>"
KO: "…왕녀니까요. 그렇게 말해야 하는 거겠죠… 분명.<end>"

## 1350068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…王女、王女、王女。<line-break>…では、王女でない私は<line-break>何者なのでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Princess, princess, princess... Then who am I, if I am not a princess?<end>"
KO: "…왕녀, 왕녀, 왕녀. …그렇다면 왕녀가 아닌 나는 대체 누구인 거죠?<end>"

## 1350069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "母は…あの人はとてもきれいです。<line-break>お父様には釣り合わない。<line-break>…きっと、誰かがいたはずです。<line-break>きっと、私は王女ですらない…<line-break>ただいつもへらへらと笑顔でいる<line-break>他人に媚びを売るだけの情けない女！<end>"
EN: "My mother... that woman is so beautiful. Far too beautiful for Father. There must have been someone else. Surely I am not even a princess... just a pathetic woman who simpers and smiles, forever currying favor with everyone!<end>"
KO: "어머니는… 그 사람은 정말 아름답습니다. 아버지에게는 어울리지 않아요. …분명 누군가 있었을 겁니다. 분명 저는 왕녀조차 아닐 거예요… 늘 실실 웃으며 남에게 아첨하기만 하는 한심한 여자!<end>"

## 1350070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、放っておいて！<end>"
EN: "So leave me alone!<end>"
KO: "그러니까 내버려 둬!<end>"

## 1350071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1350072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手紙？<line-break>ゼネテス様の？<end>"
EN: "A letter? From Lord Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "편지? 제네테스 님의?<end>"

## 1350073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　これを読んでるってことは<line-break>　　俺は死んだってことか。<end>"
EN: "If you're reading this, I guess that means I'm dead.<end>"
KO: "이걸 읽고 있다는 건 내가 죽었다는 뜻인가.<end>"

## 1350074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　後悔しないようには生きてきたつもりだが、<line-break>　　　　　　　　いざ死ぬとなると、<line-break>　　　結構やり残したことがあるもんだ。<end>"
EN: "I tried to live without regrets, but now that death's staring me in the face, it turns out I've left quite a lot undone.<end>"
KO: "후회하지 않으려고 살아왔지만 막상 죽게 되니 해 보지 못한 일이 꽤 많군.<end>"

## 1350075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　もし、ティアナに会うことがあったら<line-break>　　　　　伝えて欲しいことがある。<end>"
EN: "If you ever see Tiana, there's something I want you to tell her.<end>"
KO: "만약 티아나를 만나게 되면 전해 줬으면 하는 말이 있어.<end>"

## 1350076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　ティアナへ<line-break>　　あんたがどんなに疑いを持とうとも<line-break>　　　　　あんたは叔母貴の娘だ。<end>"
EN: "Tiana, no matter what doubts you may have, you are my aunt's daughter.<end>"
KO: "티아나에게. 네가 아무리 의심해도 너는 숙모의 딸이다.<end>"

## 1350077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　叔母貴は王を愛していた。<line-break>　　　愛されようと必死だった。<line-break>　　　　　他の誰もいないさ。<end>"
EN: "My aunt loved the king. She tried desperately to win his love. There was no one else.<end>"
KO: "숙모는 왕을 사랑했어. 사랑받으려고 필사적이었지. 다른 누구도 아니야.<end>"

## 1350078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　あんたの無理解は叔母貴を苦しめていた。<line-break>　　　叔母貴ががんばったってどうにもならん。<line-break>　　　　　　あんたが嫌っていたのは、<line-break>　　　　　あんた自身だったんだからな。<end>"
EN: "Your failure to understand caused my aunt a lot of pain. There was nothing she could do, no matter how hard she tried. Because the person you hated was yourself.<end>"
KO: "당신의 몰이해가 숙모를 괴롭혔어. 숙모가 아무리 애써도 소용없었지. 당신이 싫어했던 건 바로 당신 자신이었으니까.<end>"

## 1350079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　誰にどう接してよいかわからず、<line-break>　　　　　他人の顔色ばかりうかがい、<line-break>　誰にでも媚びを売る自分自身だったんだ。<end>"
EN: "The part of you that never knew how to act around people, that was always watching for their reactions and trying to please everyone.<end>"
KO: "누구를 어떻게 대해야 할지 몰라서 남의 눈치만 보고, 누구에게나 아첨하던 나 자신이었다.<end>"

## 1350081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　他人の顔色をうかがうってのは<line-break>　　気遣いのできる人間ってことだ。<end>"
EN: "Watching how people react means you're considerate.<end>"
KO: "남의 눈치를 살핀다는 건 배려할 줄 아는 사람이라는 뜻이야.<end>"

## 1350082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　誰にでも媚びを売るってのは、<line-break>　　誰にでも分けへだてなくやさしいってことだ。<end>"
EN: "Trying to please everyone means you're kind to everyone, without favoring one person over another.<end>"
KO: "누구에게나 아첨한다는 건 누구에게나 차별 없이 상냥하다는 뜻이야.<end>"

## 1350083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　そして、それはいろんな人の支えに<line-break>　　　　　　　　なっていた。<end>"
EN: "And that kindness has helped a lot of people.<end>"
KO: "그리고 그건 여러 사람에게 힘이 되어주고 있었다.<end>"

## 1350084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　だから、ティアナ。<line-break>　　あんたはそのままでいいんだ。<line-break>　　いつまでもやさしいティアナで<line-break>　　　　　　いてほしい。<end>"
EN: "So, Tiana, you don't need to change. I want you to stay the kind Tiana you've always been.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 티아나. 넌 그대로면 돼. 언제까지나 다정한 티아나로 있어 줘.<end>"

## 1350085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　そして、できれば<line-break>　　　叔母貴のことをわかってやってほしい。<end>"
EN: "And if you can, I want you to try to understand my aunt.<end>"
KO: " 그리고 가능하면 숙모의 마음도 이해해 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 1350086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス…様…<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes...<end>"
KO: "…제네테스… 님…<end>"

## 1350087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…<line-break>私にもできるでしょうか？<line-break> <line-break>ゼネテス様のご期待に<line-break>応えられるようなことが…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Do you think I can do it? Can I do something worthy of Lord Xenetes's faith in me?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 저도 할 수 있을까요? 제네테스 님의 기대에 부응할 만한 일을….<end>"

## 1350088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か、何か、したいのです。<line-break>この身で、ゼネテス様の遺志に<line-break>応えられる、何かを…。<end>"
EN: "I want to do something, anything, with this life of mine to honor Lord Xenetes's final wishes.<end>"
KO: "뭔가, 뭔가 하고 싶어요. 이 몸으로 제네테스 님의 유지를 이어 갈 무언가를….<end>"

## 1350089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、ロストールですか？<line-break>ロストールは…<line-break>…はっ。<line-break>思い浮かびました。<line-break>私にできること。<line-break>私が、もっともすべきことが…。<end>"
EN: "Rostorl? Rostorl is... Oh! I know now. Something I can do. The thing I ought to do above all else.<end>"
KO: "네, 로스톨이요? 로스톨은… …아. 떠올랐어요. 제가 할 수 있는 일. 제가 가장 해야 할 일이…<end>"

## 1350090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様や、レムオン様、<line-break>ゼネテス様…そして私が生きたあの街に、<line-break>再び…<line-break>いえ、前以上の繁栄を取り戻すのです。<line-break>私たちの、この手で…。<end>"
EN: "That city where Mother, Lord Lemghon, Lord Xenetes, and I all lived... We shall restore its prosperity. No, we shall make it more prosperous than ever, with our own hands.<end>"
KO: "어머니와 레무온 님, 제네테스 님… 그리고 제가 살았던 그 도시에 다시… 아니, 예전 이상의 번영을 되찾을 겁니다. 우리 손으로….<end>"

## 1350091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、まだロストールは<line-break>滅んだわけではありませんわ。<line-break> <line-break>リューガ邸は<line-break>エスト様がお継ぎになるはず。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Rostorl has not fallen yet. Lord Est should inherit the Ryuga residence.<end>"
KO: "네, 아직 로스톨이 멸망한 것은 아닙니다. 류가 저택은 에스트 님이 이어받으실 겁니다.<end>"

## 1350092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの方ならば、きっと<line-break>お力を貸してくださるはずです。<end>"
EN: "I am certain he will lend us his aid.<end>"
KO: "그분이라면 분명 힘을 빌려주실 겁니다.<end>"

## 1350093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、<line-break>次々とやらねばならないことが<line-break>見えてくる…。<line-break>きっと…<line-break>きっとゼネテス様が、<line-break>お示しくださったのですね…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, one task after another is coming to me. Surely... surely Lord Xenetes has shown me the way.<end>"
KO: "아아, 해야 할 일이 차례차례 보이기 시작해…. 분명… 분명 제네테스 님께서 보여 주신 거겠지….<end>"

## 1350094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "母は…あの人はとてもきれいです。<line-break>お父様には釣り合わない<line-break>…きっと、誰かがいたはずです。<line-break>きっと私は王女でもなんでもない！<line-break>ただいつもへらへらと笑顔でいる<line-break>他人に媚びを売るだけの情けない女！<end>"
EN: "My mother... that woman is so beautiful. Far too beautiful for Father. There must have been someone else. Surely I am no princess at all! Just a pathetic woman who simpers and smiles, forever currying favor with everyone!<end>"
KO: "어머니는… 그분은 정말 아름다워요. 아버지와는 어울리지 않아… 분명 누군가 따로 있었을 거예요. 분명 나는 왕녀도 아무것도 아니야! 언제나 실실 웃으면서 남에게 아첨하기만 하는 한심한 여자일 뿐이야!<end>"

## 1350095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、放っておいて！<end>"
EN: "So leave me alone!<end>"
KO: "그러니까 내버려 둬!<end>"

## 1350096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あんたは<line-break>間違いなく、王女だ。<end>"
EN: "You are a princess. No question about it.<end>"
KO: "…넌 틀림없는 공주다.<end>"

## 1350097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ…その声は…<end>"
EN: "Th-that voice...<end>"
KO: "그… 그 목소리는…<end>"

## 1350098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼ、ゼネテス様…。<end>"
EN: "L-Lord Xenetes...<end>"
KO: "제, 제네테스 님….<end>"

## 1350099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴は王を愛していた…<line-break>愛されようと必死だった。<line-break>他の誰もいない、さ。<end>"
EN: "My aunt loved the king. She was desperate for him to love her back. There was no one else.<end>"
KO: "숙모는 왕을 사랑했어…사랑받으려고 필사적이었지. 다른 누구도 없었어.<end>"

## 1350100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの無理解は叔母貴を苦しめてた。<line-break>叔母貴ががんばったってどうにもならん。<line-break> <line-break>あんたが嫌いだったのは、<line-break>あんた自身だったんだから。<end>"
EN: "Your failure to understand her caused my aunt pain, and nothing she did could change that. Because the person you hated was yourself.<end>"
KO: "당신의 몰이해가 아주머니를 괴롭혔어. 아무리 애써도 어쩔 수 없었지. 당신이 싫어한 건 바로 당신 자신이었으니까.<end>"

## 1350101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰にどう接してよいかわからず、<line-break>他人の顔色ばかりうかがい、<line-break>誰にも媚びを売る自分自身だったんだ。<line-break>でも、それが悪いことなのか？<end>"
EN: "You didn't know how to act around anyone, so you watched every face and tried to please everyone. That was the self you hated. But is that really so wrong?<end>"
KO: "누구를 어떻게 대해야 할지 몰라 남의 눈치만 보고 누구에게나 비위를 맞추는 나였어. 하지만 그게 나쁜 건가?<end>"

## 1350103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他人の顔色をうかがうってのは<line-break>気遣いのできる人間ってことだ。<end>"
EN: "Always watching for other people's reactions means you're considerate enough to care how they feel.<end>"
KO: "남의 눈치를 살핀다는 건 배려할 줄 아는 사람이라는 뜻이야.<end>"

## 1350104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰にでも媚びを売るってのは、<line-break>誰にでも分けへだてなく優しいって<line-break>ことだ。<line-break>そして、それはいろんな人の支えに<line-break>なっていた。<end>"
EN: "Trying to please everyone means you're kind to everyone without distinction. And that kindness has supported a great many people.<end>"
KO: "누구에게나 비위를 맞춘다는 건 누구에게나 차별 없이 다정하다는 뜻이야. 그리고 그건 많은 사람에게 힘이 되어 줬지.<end>"

## 1350105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは大切なことじゃないのか？<line-break>俺はそんな優しいあんたでいてほしい。<line-break>そう思ってる。<end>"
EN: "Isn't that important? I want you to stay the kind person you are. I truly do.<end>"
KO: "그건 소중한 일 아닌가? 난 네가 그런 상냥한 사람으로 있어 줬으면 해. 그렇게 생각해.<end>"

## 1350106

EN: "Lord Xenetes... I...<end>"
KO: "…제네테스… 님…. 저는….<end>"

## 1350107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Oh? What's this? You suddenly look rather determined.<end>"
KO: "어? 오오, 무슨 일이야? 갑자기 표정이 늠름해진 것 같은데?<end>"

## 1350108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうっ、<line-break>からかわないでください！<line-break> <line-break>…今、ひらめいたことがあるのです。<line-break>私にできること…<line-break>私が、もっともすべきこと…。<end>"

## 1350109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少々大げさかもしれませんが…<line-break>ロストールの復興です。<line-break> <line-break>私たちの手で、<line-break>以前の繁栄を取り戻せたなら、と。<end>"

## 1350111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲直りもかねて、<line-break>リューガ邸に助力を求めに<line-break>行ったらどうだい？<line-break>エストなら、<line-break>きっと力になってくれると思うぜ。<end>"

## 1350116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350118

## 1350119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なーんて、ふふふ<line-break>甘えたことも言ってはいけませんね。<line-break>やれることをがんばります。<end>"
EN: "Oh, listen to me... Hee hee. I mustn't say such self-indulgent things. I shall do my best with what I can do.<end>"
KO: "…라고, 후후후. 어리광 같은 말도 하면 안 되겠죠. 할 수 있는 일을 열심히 하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1350121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リューガ邸の一室を、ロストール復興の<line-break>本拠として提供していただけないか、<line-break>エスト様に御一考願おうと思うのです。<line-break>破壊もまぬがれたようですし、<line-break>何より、王宮にいるよりも<line-break>街の方々の声がよく届くはずですもの。<end>"
EN: "I intend to ask Lord Est to consider providing a room in the Ryuga residence as a base for Rostorl's reconstruction. It appears to have escaped destruction, and above all, the voices of the townspeople will reach me far more readily there than at the palace.<end>"
KO: "류가 저택의 방 하나를 로스톨 부흥의 거점으로 제공해 주실 수 없는지 에스트 님께 검토를 부탁드리려 합니다. 파괴도 피한 것 같고, 무엇보다 왕궁보다 시민들의 목소리가 잘 닿을 테니까요.<end>"

## 1350122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様にも<line-break>助けていただきたいことが<line-break>多々出てくると思います。<line-break>お忙しいこととは存じますが<line-break>お時間があればお寄り下さい。<end>"
EN: "I expect there will be much I must ask of you as well, <value:$28>. I know you are busy, but please visit when you have the time.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님께도 도움을 부탁드릴 일이 많이 생길 것 같습니다. 바쁘시겠지만 시간 나실 때 들러 주세요.<end>"

## 1350123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ、しぶとい奴め！<line-break>罪を認めろ！<line-break>そうすればラクにしてやる！<end>"
EN: "Damn you, you stubborn bastard! Admit your guilt! Do that, and I'll make it quick!<end>"
KO: "큭, 질긴 녀석! 죄를 인정해라! 그러면 편하게 해주지!<end>"

## 1350124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しぶといだけが…取り柄でね。<line-break>それにラクにするって…殺すんだろ？<line-break>それじゃ…折れるわけにはいかねえ…。<line-break>１人の人間の命は…世界より重いんだ。<line-break>それを、おいそれと捨てるような<line-break>マネはできな…<end>"
EN: "Stubbornness is about all I've got going for me. And by “make it quick,” you mean kill me, right? Then I can't give in. One human life is worth more than the whole world. I can't just throw it away so eas―<end>"
KO: "질긴 것만이… 내 장점이라서 말이야. 게다가 편하게 해준다는 건… 죽이겠다는 거지? 그렇다면… 꺾일 수 없어…. 한 사람의 목숨은… 세계보다 무거워. 그걸 쉽게 내던지는 짓은 할 수 없…<end>"

## 1350125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へらず口を！<line-break>まだ、英雄のつもりか！？<line-break>貴様は今、反逆者なのだよ！<end>"
EN: "Enough of your insolence! Do you still fancy yourself a hero? You are a traitor now!<end>"
KO: "말대꾸를! 아직도 영웅인 줄 아느냐!? 네놈은 지금 반역자다!<end>"

## 1350126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は…俺だ。<line-break>それに…誰だろうが…<line-break>何者だろうが…人の命は…。<line-break>そう、何者だろうが、<line-break>人の命は…大事なんだ…。<line-break>だろ…？　レムオン…。<end>"
EN: "I'm...me. And no matter who a person is...no matter what a person may be, a human life... That's right. No matter what, a human life matters. Doesn't it, Lemghon...?<end>"
KO: "나는… 나다. 그리고… 누구든… 무엇이든… 사람의 목숨은…. 그래, 누구든 사람의 목숨은… 소중해…. 그렇지…? 레무온….<end>"

## 1350127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相当あせってきたようだな？<line-break>千年樹に刻まれた法に従えば<line-break>もう、時間は…ないもんな。<end>"
EN: "You're looking rather desperate. Under the law inscribed upon the Thousand-Year Tree, you haven't got much time left.<end>"
KO: "꽤 초조해진 모양이군? 천년수에 새겨진 법에 따르면 이제 시간은… 얼마 없으니까.<end>"

## 1350128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おのれ、何者だ！？<line-break>ここに何しにきた！<end>"
EN: "Damn you! Who are you? What are you doing here?!<end>"
KO: "네놈, 누구냐!? 여긴 왜 왔지!<end>"

## 1350129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく、来てくれた<line-break>…って言いたいところだが、<line-break>すまねえ。<line-break>ここで、お前さんに<line-break>助けられるわけにはいかねえんだ。<line-break>それじゃ、意味がない。<end>"
EN: "I'd like to say I'm glad you came...but I'm sorry. I can't let you save me here. That would defeat the whole purpose.<end>"
KO: "잘 와 줬다…고 말하고 싶지만 미안하다. 여기서 네 도움을 받을 수는 없어. 그러면 의미가 없거든.<end>"

## 1350130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はレムオンに助けてもらわないと<line-break>俺自身が制度を破ることになる。<line-break>それは、後々の人間のためによくない。<line-break>どこかの英雄みたいに<line-break>多くの人間を不幸にする…<line-break>俺はそういうの…納得できねえんだ。<end>"
EN: "Unless Lemghon saves me, I'll be the one breaking the system. That won't be good for those who come after us. I can't accept making so many people suffer, like some hero would.<end>"
KO: "나는 레무온의 도움을 받지 않으면 내가 제도를 깨뜨리게 돼. 그건 뒤에 살아갈 사람들에게 좋지 않아. 어디 영웅처럼 많은 사람을 불행하게 하는 건… 난 그런 거 납득 못 해.<end>"

## 1350131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、死ね！<line-break>多くの人間のために！<end>"
EN: "Then die! For the sake of the many!<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 죽어라! 수많은 사람을 위해!<end>"

## 1350132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリスを殺した…<line-break>ゼネテスも…殺してしまった…。<line-break>これで…俺は…。<end>"
EN: "I killed Eris...and now I've killed Xenetes too. Now I...<end>"
KO: "에리스를 죽였어… 제네테스도… 죽여 버렸어…. 이걸로… 나는….<end>"

## 1350133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　ウフフフ…アハハハハ<line-break>　　　決定的瞬間だね…。<end>"
EN: "Hee-hee-hee... Ah-ha-ha-ha! This is the decisive moment...<end>"
KO: "우후후후… 아하하하하. 결정적인 순간이네….<end>"

## 1350134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　ね…<line-break>　…ティアナ。<end>"
EN: "Isn't it... Tiana?<end>"
KO: "있잖아… …티아나.<end>"

## 1350135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なっ！<end>"
EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "뭐!?<end>"

## 1350136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様！！！<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes!!!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님!!!<end>"

## 1350137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この…邪悪な吸血鬼め！<end>"
EN: "You...wicked vampire!<end>"
KO: "이… 사악한 흡혈귀 놈!<end>"

## 1350138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何を…言う。<line-break>ティアナ。<end>"
EN: "W-what are you saying, Tiana?<end>"
KO: "무, 무슨 말을… 티아나.<end>"

## 1350139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪悪な吸血鬼って言ったの。<line-break>だってそうでしょう？<line-break>あなた、ダルケニスなのだから…。<line-break>小さいころから<line-break>ずっと一緒にいて<line-break>気づかなかったとでも思って？<end>"
EN: "I called you a wicked vampire. Because that's what you are, isn't it? A Darkenith. We've been together since childhood. Did you really think I hadn't noticed?<end>"
KO: "사악한 흡혈귀라고 했어. 그렇잖아? 당신은 다르케니스니까…. 어릴 때부터 계속 함께 있었는데 눈치채지 못했을 거라고 생각해?<end>"

## 1350140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか？<line-break>本当にあの汚らわしい<line-break>ダルケニスなのか？<end>"
EN: "Surely not! Are you truly one of those filthy Darkenith?<end>"
KO: "설마? 정말 그 더러운 다르케니스란 말인가?<end>"

## 1350141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな！<line-break>そのようなことあるわけが…。<end>"
EN: "Absurd! That cannot possibly be...<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼! 그런 일이 있을 리가….<end>"

## 1350142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、大ありなのさ？<line-break>みんなにも見てもらいなよ。<line-break>ホントの君の姿を。<end>"
EN: "Heh...but it is. Why don't you let everyone see your true form?<end>"
KO: "후후…, 아주 있지? 모두에게도 보여 주라고. 진짜 네 모습을.<end>"

## 1350143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…あ！<line-break>ダルケニス…！<line-break>吸血鬼！<end>"
EN: "Ah...ah! A Darkenith! A vampire!<end>"
KO: "아… 아! 다르케니스…! 흡혈귀!<end>"

## 1350144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち…血を吸われるー！<line-break>ヒィーッ！<end>"
EN: "M-my blood! It'll drink my blood! Eeeek!<end>"
KO: "피… 피를 빨린다! 히익!<end>"

## 1350145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、あなたは闇の種族。<line-break>人間からは目の敵にされ<line-break>狩られるべき種族、ダルケニス。<line-break>あなたに居場所はないわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. You are of the race of darkness: the Darkenith, hated by humans and regarded as a people to be hunted. There is no place for you.<end>"
KO: "그래, 너는 어둠의 종족. 인간에게 눈엣가시로 여겨져 사냥당해야 하는 종족, 다르케니스. 네가 있을 곳은 없어.<end>"

## 1350146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界が受け入れて<line-break>くれないんだったら、<line-break>私たちもこんな世界<line-break>捨ててしまいましょう？<line-break>すべて、何もかも<line-break>ちりに、返して、あげるの…。<end>"
EN: "If this world offers no acceptance, then let's cast it aside together, shall we? Everything, all of it... I'll return it to dust for you...<end>"
KO: "세상이 받아주지 않는다면 우리도 이런 세상… 버려 버릴까요? 전부, 무엇이든 먼지로 돌려주겠어요…<end>"

## 1350147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフフ…<line-break>アハハハハ…<line-break> <line-break>もろい、もろいよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee hee... Ah ha ha ha... So fragile. So very fragile.<end>"
KO: "우후후후… 아하하하하…. 약해, 너무 약해.<end>"

## 1350148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな幻も見抜けずに<line-break>心が壊れるなんて<line-break>ウフフフ…<line-break>アハハハ<line-break>アハハハハハハ<line-break>フハハハハハハ。<end>"
EN: "To have your mind break because you couldn't even see through an illusion like this... Hee hee hee... Ah ha ha! Ah ha ha ha ha ha! Bwah ha ha ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "이런 환상도 꿰뚫어 보지 못하고 마음이 무너지다니. 우후후후… 아하하하, 아하하하하하, 후하하하하하.<end>"

## 1350149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の相手はこいつがしてくれるって。<line-break>ウフフフ、アハハハ。<line-break>いや、大漁、大漁。<line-break>じゃあね、<value:$28>、<line-break>終末を楽しみにね。<line-break>アハハハハハハハハ。<end>"
EN: "This one will keep you busy. Hee hee hee, ah ha ha ha! What a haul, what a haul! Bye-bye, <value:$28>. Look forward to the end of the world! Ah ha ha ha ha ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "네 상대는 이 녀석이 해 줄 거래. 우후후후, 아하하하. 이야, 대어다, 대어. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>, 종말을 기대하라고. 아하하하하하하하.<end>"

## 1350150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…ああ…！<line-break>諸君！<line-break>侵入者を捕らえろ！<end>"
EN: "Ah...ahh! Everyone! Seize the intruder!<end>"
KO: "아… 아아…! 제군! 침입자를 붙잡아라!<end>"

## 1350151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ば、馬鹿な、我が軍の精鋭が、<line-break>こんな簡単に！？<end>"
EN: "I-impossible! The finest soldiers in our army, defeated so easily?!<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼. 우리 군의 정예가 이렇게 쉽게!?<end>"

## 1350152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　すべて徒労…<line-break>　　すべては無意味…<line-break>　　　我は虚無なり…<end>"
EN: "All effort is futile... All is meaningless... I am the Void...<end>"
KO: "모든 건 헛수고… 모든 것은 무의미… 나는 허무이니…<end>"

## 1350153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なに！？<end>"
EN: "W-what?!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐!?<end>"

## 1350154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとよ。<line-break>ホント、助かったぜ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. You really saved me.<end>"
KO: "…고맙다. 정말 살았어.<end>"

## 1350155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公！<line-break>な、なぜ魔人を…。<line-break>これは救国の義挙のはず！<end>"
EN: "Duke Elienai! W-why are you using fiends...? This was supposed to be a righteous bid to save the nation!<end>"
KO: "엘리에나이 공! 왜, 왜 마인을… 이건 구국의 의거일 텐데!<end>"

## 1350156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔人を操るなんて芸当、<line-break>あきらかに人間技じゃない。<line-break>…レムオン以外の誰かの仕業だ。<end>"
EN: "Controlling fiends is plainly beyond human power. Someone other than Lemghon is behind this.<end>"
KO: "마인을 조종한다는 건 분명 인간이 할 수 있는 짓이 아니야. …레무온 말고 다른 누군가의 소행이다.<end>"

## 1350157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　それはどうかしら？<end>"
EN: "Are you sure about that?<end>"
KO: "그건 어떨까?<end>"

## 1350158

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ア、アトレイア様！？<end>"

KO: "아, 아트레이아 님!?<end>"

## 1350159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間にはできなくても…<line-break>ダルケニスになら簡単なこと。<line-break>ね、シャリ様？<end>"

KO: "인간은 못 해도… 다르케니스라면 간단한 일. 그렇죠, 샤리 님?<end>"

JP: "ち…血を吸われるー！<line-break>ヒィーッ！<end>"
