KO: "피… 피를 빨린다! 히익!<end>"

## 1350165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、あなたは闇の種族。<line-break>人間からは目の敵にされ<line-break>狩られるべき種族、ダルケニス。<line-break>あなたに居場所はないわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. You are of the race of darkness: the Darkenith, hated by humans and regarded as a people to be hunted. There is no place for you.<end>"
KO: "그래, 너는 어둠의 종족. 인간에게 눈엣가시로 여겨져 사냥당해야 하는 종족, 다르케니스. 네가 있을 곳은 없어.<end>"

## 1350166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界が受け入れて<line-break>くれないんだったら、<line-break>私たちもこんな世界…<line-break>捨ててしまいましょう？<line-break>すべて、何もかも<line-break>ちりに、返して、あげるの…。<end>"
EN: "If this world offers no acceptance, then let's cast it aside together, shall we? Everything, all of it... I'll return it to dust for you...<end>"
KO: "세계가 받아주지 않는다면 우리도 이런 세계… 버려버릴까요? 전부, 모든 걸 먼지로 돌려줄게…<end>"

## 1350167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、俺は…。<end>"
EN: "I-I...<end>"
KO: "나, 나는….<end>"

## 1350168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、レムオン、<line-break>タルテュバみたく闇に堕ちてよ。<line-break>きっとかわいい怪物になれるよ…。<end>"
EN: "Come now, Lemghon. Fall into darkness like Tartuba. I'm sure you'll make an adorable monster.<end>"
KO: "자, 레무온. 타르튜바처럼 어둠에 떨어져 봐. 분명 귀여운 괴물이 될 수 있을 거야….<end>"

## 1350170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…来い、レムオン。<end>"
EN: "Come, Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "…와라, 레무온.<end>"

## 1350171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…子供のころから、<line-break>ずっと、あこがれてたんだろ？<line-break>なのに、彼女を殺した男を許すの？<line-break>君は、いつでも余裕があるけど、<line-break>それは、ただ、傷つくのを恐れて、<line-break>深く踏み込めないだけじゃない？<end>"
EN: "You've admired her ever since you were a child, haven't you? And yet you'll forgive the man who killed her? You always seem so composed, but isn't that only because you're afraid of being hurt―too afraid to get deeply involved?<end>"
KO: "…어릴 때부터 계속 동경해 왔잖아? 그런데 그녀를 죽인 남자를 용서하는 거야? 넌 언제나 여유 있어 보이지만, 사실은 상처받는 게 두려워 깊이 발을 들이지 못하는 것뿐 아니야?<end>"

## 1350172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かもな。<line-break>それだけかい？<end>"
EN: "Maybe. Is that all?<end>"
KO: "그럴지도. 그게 다야?<end>"

## 1350173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それは、悲しいよ。<end>"
EN: "That's...sad.<end>"
KO: "…그건 슬퍼.<end>"

## 1350174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もはや助けも間にあわん！<line-break>これで終わりだ！<end>"
EN: "No help can reach you in time now! This is the end!<end>"
KO: "이젠 구원도 늦었다! 이걸로 끝이다!<end>"

## 1350175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公…これはいったい…<line-break>なぜ魔人の力を！？<line-break>これは救国の義挙のはず！<end>"
EN: "Duke Elienai...what is the meaning of this? Why do you wield the power of fiends?! This was supposed to be a righteous bid to save the nation!<end>"
KO: "엘리에나이 공… 이게 대체… 왜 마인의 힘을!? 이건 구국의 의거였을 텐데!<end>"

## 1350176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　くすくす…<line-break>　　うふふふ…あーっはははは！<end>"
EN: "Hee-hee... Hee-hee-hee... Ah-ha-ha-ha-ha!<end>"
KO: "후후후… 우후후후… 아하하하하!<end>"

## 1350177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様は！　道化！<line-break>なにがおかしい！<end>"
EN: "You! Jester! What is so amusing?!<end>"
KO: "네놈은! 광대! 뭐가 우습지!<end>"

## 1350178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救国の義挙？<line-break>違う、違う。<line-break> <line-break>君たちみたいな小物に<line-break>そんなことできるわけないじゃないか？<line-break>君たちの役目は…<end>"
EN: "A righteous bid to save the nation? No, no. Small fry like you could never accomplish anything like that. Your role is...<end>"
KO: "구국의 의거? 아니, 아니. 너희 같은 소물들이 그런 일을 할 수 있을 리 없잖아? 너희 역할은….<end>"

## 1350179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礎になることさ。<line-break>世界を終わらせるという<line-break>歴史的な偉業のね！<line-break>ねぇ、レムオン。<end>"
EN: "...to serve as the foundation for a historic achievement: ending the world! Isn't that right, Lemghon?<end>"
KO: "초석이 되는 거야. 세계를 끝낸다는 역사적 위업의 말이지! 그렇지, 레무온.<end>"

## 1350180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<line-break>貴様！<end>"
EN: "Damn you...!<end>"
KO: "큭…. 네놈!<end>"

## 1350181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、アトレイア。<line-break>いいかげんウザイから、<line-break>この短絡バカ、やっちゃってよ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, Atleia. This short-sighted fool is getting annoying. Take care of him.<end>"
KO: "자, 아트레이아. 이제 슬슬 짜증 나니까 이 단순무식한 바보 좀 처리해 줘.<end>"

## 1350182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はアトレイア。<line-break>ずっと、暗闇に閉ざされてきた<line-break>もうひとりの王女です…<line-break>ふふ…ふふふ。<end>"
EN: "I am Atleia, the other princess, long imprisoned in darkness... Heh...heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "나는 아트레이아. 줄곧 어둠 속에 갇혀 있던 또 한 명의 왕녀야… 후후… 후후후.<end>"

## 1350183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様、<line-break>お礼を言っておきます。<end>"
EN: "Lord Lemghon, I should thank you.<end>"
KO: "레무온 님, 감사 인사 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1350184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた方の流した血のおかげで、<line-break>闇の王女にふさわしい力を<line-break>得ることができました。<line-break>世界を無に還すだけの力を…<line-break>ね…。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to the blood you shed, I have gained power worthy of the Princess of Darkness―enough to return the world to nothingness.<end>"
KO: "당신들이 흘린 피 덕분에 어둠의 왕녀에 걸맞은 힘을 얻었습니다. 세계를 무로 돌릴 만큼의 힘을… 말이죠….<end>"

## 1350185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公…いや、レムオン！<line-break>貴様、闇に与するのか！<line-break>いったい、貴様は何者なのだ！<end>"

## 1350186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいんじゃない？　教えてあげなよ。<line-break>虐げられ、恐れられてきた吸血鬼、<line-break>ダルケニスこそ君の正体だってさ。<end>"
EN: "Why not? Go on, tell the truth: you're really a Darkenith, one of the vampires who've been oppressed and feared all this time.<end>"
KO: "괜찮잖아? 알려 줘. 핍박받고 두려움의 대상이 되어온 흡혈귀, 다르케니스야말로 네 정체라고 말이야.<end>"

## 1350189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様…、<line-break>正体が明かされたからには<line-break>世界のどこにも居場所はありません。<line-break>あなたは、迫害され、<line-break>狩られる身なのです。<line-break>人間どもと戦って勝ち取る以外はね。<end>"
EN: "Lord Lemghon, now that your true nature has been exposed, there is nowhere in the world for you. You will be persecuted and hunted―unless you fight those humans and seize a place for yourself.<end>"
KO: "레무온 님…. 정체가 드러난 이상 세계 어디에도 있을 곳은 없습니다. 당신은 박해받고 사냥당하는 몸이에요. 인간들과 싸워서 쟁취하는 방법 말고는 없습니다.<end>"

## 1350212

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml

## 1350214

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

JP: "ウウォォォォォオオオオオ！<end>"

KO: "우오오오오오오오오오!<end>"

## 1350216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やけくその突撃？<line-break>アハハ、見苦しいなぁ。<line-break>それでも貴族なの？<end>"
EN: "A desperate charge? Ah ha ha, how unsightly. And you call yourself a noble?<end>"
KO: "자포자기한 돌격? 아하하, 꼴사납군. 그래도 귀족이야?<end>"

## 1350217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、いいよ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>立派な闇の公爵になれるよう、<line-break>アトレイアとふたりでたっぷりと<line-break>調教してあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, well. Atleia and I will train you thoroughly until you're fit to become a splendid Duke of Darkness.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 좋아. 훌륭한 어둠의 공작이 될 수 있게 아트레이아와 둘이서 실컷 조련해 줄게.<end>"

## 1350218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハハハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Ah ha ha ha ha ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "아하하하하하하하!<end>"

## 1350219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>よき終末を！<end>"
EN: "Goodbye, <value:$28>. Enjoy the end of the world!<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕. <value:$28>, 좋은 종말을!<end>"

## 1350220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ、しぶとい奴め！<line-break>罪を認めろ！<line-break>そうすればラクにしてやる！<end>"
EN: "Damn you, you're tenacious! Confess your crime! Do that, and I'll make it quick!<end>"
KO: "큭, 질긴 녀석! 죄를 인정해라! 그러면 편하게 해주지!<end>"

## 1350221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しぶといだけが…取り柄でね。<line-break>それにラクにするって…殺すんだろ？<line-break>それじゃ…折れるわけにはいかねえ…。<line-break>１人の人間の命は…世界より重いんだ。<line-break>それを、おいそれと捨てるような<line-break>マネはできな…<end>"
EN: "Being tenacious is about all I've got going for me. And by “make it quick,” you mean kill me, right? Then I can't afford to give in. A single human life is worth more than the whole world. I can't just throw one away so eas―<end>"
KO: "질긴 것만이… 내 장점이라서 말이야. 게다가 편하게 해준다는 건… 죽이겠다는 거지? 그렇다면… 꺾일 수 없어…. 한 사람의 목숨은… 세계보다 무거워. 그걸 쉽게 내던지는 짓은 할 수 없…<end>"

## 1350222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へらず口を！<line-break>まだ、英雄のつもりか！？<line-break>貴様は今、反逆者なのだ！<end>"
EN: "Enough of your insolence! Do you still fancy yourself a hero? You are a traitor now!<end>"
KO: "말대꾸를! 아직도 영웅인 줄 아느냐!? 네놈은 지금 반역자다!<end>"

## 1350223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は…俺だ。<line-break>それに…誰だろうが…<line-break>何者だろうが…人の命は…。<line-break>そう、何者だろうが、<line-break>人の命は…大事なんだ。<line-break>だろ…？　レムオン…<end>"
EN: "I'm...me. And no matter who you are...or what you are, a person's life... That's right. No matter what you are, a person's life matters. Doesn't it...Lemghon?<end>"
KO: "난… 나야. 그리고… 누구든… 무엇이든… 사람의 목숨은… 그래, 누구든 사람의 목숨은 소중해. 그렇지…? 레무온…<end>"

## 1350224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相当あせってきたようだな？<line-break>千年樹に刻まれた…法に従えば<line-break>もう、時間は…ないもんな。<end>"
EN: "You're looking pretty desperate. Under the law carved into the Thousand-Year Tree...you haven't got much time left, do you?<end>"
KO: "상당히 초조해진 모양이군? 천년수에 새겨진 법에 따르면 이제 시간은… 없는 거잖아.<end>"

## 1350225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おのれ、何者だ！？<line-break>ここに何しにきた！<end>"
EN: "Damn you! Who are you? What are you doing here?!<end>"
KO: "네놈, 누구냐!? 여긴 왜 왔지!<end>"

## 1350226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…ああ…！<line-break>諸君！<line-break>侵入者を捕らえろ！<end>"
EN: "Ah...ah! All of you! Seize the intruder!<end>"
KO: "아… 아아…! 제군! 침입자를 붙잡아라!<end>"

## 1350227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ば、馬鹿な、我が軍の精鋭が、<line-break>こんな簡単に！？<end>"
EN: "I-impossible! The finest soldiers in my army, defeated so easily?!<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼. 우리 군의 정예가 이렇게 쉽게!?<end>"

## 1350228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとよ。<line-break>ホント、助かったぜ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. You really saved my hide.<end>"
KO: "…고맙다. 정말 살았어.<end>"

## 1350229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ば、ばかな…<end>"
EN: "I-impossible...<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼…<end>"

## 1350230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　フフフ…<line-break>　　ダメね、レムオン。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... You're hopeless, Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 안 되겠네, 레무온.<end>"

## 1350231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ…？<end>"
EN: "Tiana...?<end>"
KO: "티아나…?<end>"

## 1350232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母様を殺せたのは<line-break>ほめてさしあげてもいいけれど<line-break>ゼネテスを殺す器量はなかったのね。<end>"
EN: "I suppose you deserve some praise for managing to kill Mother, but you didn't have what it takes to kill Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "어머님을 죽인 건 칭찬해 줄 수도 있지만 제네테스를 죽일 그릇은 아니었나 보네.<end>"

## 1350233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ…？<line-break>まさか、お前さんが黒幕…？<end>"
EN: "Tiana...? Don't tell me you were behind all this...?<end>"
KO: "티아나…? 설마 네가 흑막…?<end>"

## 1350234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス、現実を受け入れないと。<line-break>まさか、じゃなくて<line-break>なぜ、って聞いた方がリアルよ？<line-break>あなたはレムオンと違って<line-break>現実的な人だと思っていたけれど？<line-break>買いかぶりすぎたかしら？<end>"
EN: "You need to face reality, Xenetes. Instead of saying, “It can't be,” wouldn't it be more realistic to ask why? I thought you were a practical man, unlike Lemghon. Perhaps I gave you too much credit.<end>"
KO: "제네테스, 현실을 받아들여야지. '설마'가 아니라 '왜'라고 묻는 편이 현실적이야. 당신은 레무온과 달리 현실적인 사람이라고 생각했는데? 너무 높게 봤나?<end>"

## 1350235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして…あなた。<line-break>この泥棒猫…<line-break>わたしからすべてを奪うつもりなのね。<line-break>あなたには…<line-break>あなたには…<line-break>何も…何も渡さない！<end>"
EN: "And you... You little thief. You mean to take everything from me, don't you? I won't give you... I won't give you...anything! Nothing!<end>"
KO: "그리고… 당신. 이 도둑고양이… 내게서 모든 걸 빼앗을 생각이군. 당신에게는… 아무것도… 아무것도 주지 않을 거야!<end>"

## 1350236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "痛いでしょう？<line-break>悲しいでしょう？<line-break>胸が張り裂けそうでしょう？<line-break>私が味わった苦しみを受けながら、<line-break>この世の終わりまでみじめに生きるが<line-break>いい。ふふふ、あはは…あははははは。<end>"
EN: "It hurts, doesn't it? You're grieving, aren't you? It feels as if your heart might break, doesn't it? Live on in misery until the end of the world, suffering as I suffered. Hee hee...ha ha... Ahahahahaha!<end>"
KO: "아프지? 슬프지? 가슴이 찢어질 것 같지? 내가 맛본 고통을 겪으며 이 세상 끝날 때까지 비참하게 살아. 후후후, 아하하… 아하하하하.<end>"

## 1350237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく、来てくれた<line-break>…って言いたいところだが、<line-break>すまねえ。<line-break>ここで、お前さんに<line-break>助けられるわけにはいかねえんだ。<line-break>それじゃ、意味がない。<end>"
EN: "I'd like to say I'm glad you came...but I'm sorry. I can't let you save me here. If I did, none of this would mean anything.<end>"
KO: "잘 와 줬다…고 말하고 싶지만 미안하다. 여기서 네 도움을 받을 수는 없어. 그러면 의미가 없거든.<end>"

## 1350238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はレムオンに助けてもらわないと<line-break>俺自身が制度を破ることになる。<line-break>それは、後々の人間のためによくない。<line-break>どこかの英雄みたいに<line-break>多くの人間を不幸にする…<line-break>俺はそういうの…納得できねえんだ。<end>"
EN: "If Lemghon doesn't save me, I'll be the one breaking the system. That won't be good for the people who come after us. I'd be making many people suffer, just like a certain hero did...and I can't accept that.<end>"
KO: "나는 레무온의 도움을 받지 않으면 내가 제도를 깨뜨리게 돼. 그건 뒤에 살아갈 사람들에게 좋지 않아. 어디 영웅처럼 많은 사람을 불행하게 하는 건… 난 그런 거 납득 못 해.<end>"

## 1350239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、死ね！<line-break>多くの人間のために！<end>"
EN: "Then die! For the sake of the many!<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 죽어라! 수많은 사람을 위해!<end>"

## 1350240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリスを殺した…<line-break>ゼネテスも…殺してしまった…。<line-break>これで…俺は…。<end>"
EN: "I killed Eris... And now I've killed Xenetes too. Now I...<end>"
KO: "에리스를 죽였어… 제네테스도… 죽여 버렸어…. 이걸로… 나는….<end>"

## 1350241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　これで…<line-break>　　　　あなたは私のもの…。<end>"
EN: "Now... you're mine...<end>"
KO: "이제… 당신은 내 거야….<end>"

## 1350242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様？<line-break>こ、これは？<end>"
EN: "Lady Tiana? Wh-what is this?<end>"
KO: "티아나 님? 이, 이건?<end>"

## 1350243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ジャマだわ。<line-break>あなたはもう必要がない。<line-break>クズは灰に…<end>"
EN: "You're in my way. I don't need you anymore. Scum to ashes...<end>"
KO: "…방해야. 넌 이제 필요 없어. 쓰레기는 재로….<end>"

## 1350244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぎゃあああああああ！<end>"
EN: "Aaaaaaaaaagh!<end>"
KO: "꺄아아아아아아!<end>"

## 1350245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは、私が王女でも何でもない、<line-break>ただの、あなたの叔母の娘だって<line-break>知ってたのよね、ゼネテス。<end>"
EN: "You knew, didn't you, Xenetes? You knew I wasn't a princess at all. I was merely your aunt's daughter.<end>"
KO: "당신은 내가 왕녀도 뭐도 아닌, 그저 당신 이모의 딸이라는 걸 알고 있었지, 제네테스.<end>"

## 1350246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気は確かか、ティアナ！？<line-break>君はまちがいなく王女だ。<line-break>あの女狐が色恋ごときで！<end>"
EN: "Have you lost your mind, Tiana?! You are the princess, without question. That vixen―over something as trivial as love...!<end>"
KO: "제정신이야, 티아나!? 넌 틀림없는 왕녀야. 그 여우 같은 여자가 고작 연애 따위로!<end>"

## 1350247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐっ！<end>"
EN: "Guh!<end>"
KO: "큭!<end>"

## 1350248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、あのとき、<line-break>あなたはアトレイアを…<line-break>…私を拒絶した。<end>"
EN: "That's why, back then, you rejected Atleia... You rejected me.<end>"
KO: "그래서 그때 당신은 아트레이아를… 나를 거부했군요.<end>"

## 1350250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたにも<line-break>こうなっていただくわ…。<end>"
EN: "I'll make you like this too...<end>"
KO: "당신도 이렇게 되어 줘야겠어….<end>"

## 1350251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが起こしたクーデター、それと<line-break>あなたのために傷つき、死んでいった者<line-break>たちの悲鳴と悲しみのおかげで…<line-break>この力を得ることができました。<line-break>たっぷりと味わいなさい…<line-break>おバカな吸血鬼さん。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to the coup you started, and to the screams and grief of all those wounded and killed for your sake...I was able to obtain this power. Savor it to the fullest...you foolish little vampire.<end>"
KO: "당신이 일으킨 쿠데타, 그리고 당신 때문에 다치고 죽어간 자들의 비명과 슬픔 덕분에… 이 힘을 얻을 수 있었습니다. 실컷 맛보세요… 멍청한 흡혈귀 씨.<end>"

## 1350252

EN: "Tiana...please realize...it's me...<end>"
KO: "…티아나, 나를… 알아봐 줘….<end>"

## 1350263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス、現実を受け入れないと。<line-break>まさか、じゃなくて<line-break>なぜ、って聞いた方がリアルよ？<line-break>あなたはレムオンと違って<line-break>現実的な人だと思っていたけれど？<line-break>買いかぶりすぎたかしら？<end>"
EN: "You need to face reality, Xenetes. Instead of saying, “It can't be,” wouldn't it be more realistic to ask why? I thought you were a practical man, unlike Lemghon. Perhaps I gave you too much credit.<end>"
KO: "제네테스, 현실을 받아들여야지. '설마'가 아니라 '왜'라고 묻는 편이 현실적이야. 당신은 레무온과 달리 현실적인 사람이라고 생각했는데? 너무 높게 봤나?<end>"

## 1350264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無論、俺は現実的な男さ。<line-break>驚いたふりで時間を稼いだだけだ。<line-break>逃げるぞ！<end>"
EN: "Of course I'm a practical man. I was only pretending to be surprised to buy time. Let's get out of here!<end>"
KO: "물론이지, 난 현실적인 남자야. 놀란 척하면서 시간을 번 것뿐이야. 도망치자!<end>"

## 1350265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン、来い！！<end>"
EN: "Lemghon, come on!<end>"
KO: "레무온, 와!!<end>"

## 1350266

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I... I... I'm already...<end>"

## 1350270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恩を売って…おきたいからか…な…。<line-break>さ、バカレムオン、俺を<line-break>安全なところまで運ぶんだ…。<end>"

KO: "은혜를… 팔아 두고 싶어서…겠지…. 자, 바보 레무온, 날 안전한 곳까지 옮겨….<end>"

## 1350271

## 1350272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…醜いわ。ダルケニスのくせに<line-break>人間と手を結ぶ気なのね？<line-break>汚らわしい。<line-break>闇に生きることもできず、<line-break>光に生きることもできない<line-break>なんて、不完全な劣等種なの…。<end>"
EN: "How hideous. A Darkenith, intending to join forces with humans? Disgusting. Unable to live in darkness or in the light... What an imperfect, inferior breed.<end>"
KO: "…추하네. 다르케니스 주제에 인간과 손잡을 생각이야? 더러워. 어둠에서도 살지 못하고 빛에서도 살지 못하다니, 불완전한 열등종이네…<end>"

## 1350273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "눈에 거슬려. 사라져, 어리석은 흡혈귀 씨!<end>"

## 1350274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王宮の中の雰囲気が<line-break>おかしいのです。<end>"

KO: "왕궁 안의 분위기가 이상합니다.<end>"

## 1350291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国に害をなす毒婦エリスと<line-break>ファーロス家を除くため、<line-break>エリエナイ公らが立ち上がられた！ <line-break>王宮は我らが占拠した！<line-break>ティアナ王女、<line-break>エリエナイ公のもとへ来ていただくぞ！<end>"
EN: "Duke Elienai and the others have risen to rid the realm of that venomous woman Eris and House Pharos! The palace is ours! Princess Tiana, you will come with us to Duke Elienai!<end>"
KO: "나라에 해를 끼치는 독부 에리스와 파로스 가문을 제거하기 위해 에리에나이 공 등이 들고일어났다! 왕궁은 우리가 점거했다! 티아나 왕녀, 에리에나이 공에게 와 주셔야겠다!<end>"

## 1350292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノ、ノーブル伯！　<line-break>我らに刃向かわれるおつもりか？<line-break> <line-break>この決起はご尊兄の主導だというのに…<line-break>なんと嘆かわしいことか！<end>"
EN: "C-Count of Noble! Do you mean to oppose us? Your own esteemed elder brother is leading this uprising... How deplorable!<end>"
KO: "노, 노블 백작! 우리에게 맞설 셈이오? 이번 거사는 귀하의 형님이 주도한 것인데… 참으로 한탄스럽소!<end>"

## 1350293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やむをえん！　我らの敵に回るなら、<line-break>伯爵様とはいえ、国害となる人物だ！<line-break>始末しろ！<end>"
EN: "Then we have no choice! Count or not, anyone who turns against us is a threat to the realm! Finish the count off!<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없다! 우리의 적으로 돌아선다면 백작님이라 해도 나라에 해가 되는 인물이다! 처리해라!<end>"

## 1350294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ。ジャマするか、貴様！<line-break>剣のサビにしてくれるわ！<end>"
EN: "Gah! You dare stand in my way? I'll turn you into rust on my blade!<end>"
KO: "큭. 방해하겠다는 거냐, 네놈! 검의 녹으로 만들어 주마!<end>"

## 1350295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らの手から逃れられると思うか！<line-break>ロストールは我らの手中にあるぞ！<end>"
EN: "Do you think you can escape us? Rostorl is in our hands!<end>"
KO: "우리 손에서 도망칠 수 있을 것 같으냐! 로스톨은 우리 손안에 있다!<end>"

## 1350296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>こちらの秘密通路から！<line-break>とにかく王都を脱出しましょう！<line-break>私は、戦い方を知りません。<line-break>どうか…<line-break>どうか、ティアナをお守りください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, this way, through the secret passage! We must get out of the capital! I don't know how to fight. Please... please protect Tiana.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 이 비밀통로로! 일단 왕도를 탈출해요! 저는 싸우는 법을 모릅니다. 부디… 부디 티아나를 지켜 주세요.<end>"

## 1350302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは<line-break>ノーブル伯<value:$28>様ですね。<line-break>よく存じております。<line-break>このクーデターの首謀者<line-break>エリエナイ公はあなたの<line-break>お兄様のはず…、よろしいのですか？<end>"
EN: "You are Count <value:$28> of Noble, are you not? I know who you are. Duke Elienai, the man behind this coup, is your older brother, as I understand it... Are you sure?<end>"
KO: "당신은 노블 백작 <value:$28> 님이시군요. 잘 알고 있습니다. 이번 쿠데타의 주모자 엘리에나이 공은 당신의 형님일 텐데… 괜찮으십니까?<end>"

## 1350304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Count of Noble, I am grateful for your loyalty to the royal house.<end>"
KO: "노블 백작, 왕가에 대한 당신의 충절에 감사드립니다.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml

EN: "You are Dragon-Sigil General <value:$28>, are you not? I know who you are. General, I am grateful for your loyalty to the royal house.<end>"
KO: "당신은 용자장군 <value:$28>님이시군요. 잘 알고 있습니다. 장군, 왕가에 대한 당신의 충절을 고맙게 생각합니다.<end>"

## 1350310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2な、なんでこんなところに<line-break>魔物が…来るわよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3このような魔物が王城の前に…<line-break>これは、ただごとではないようじゃな…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4きゃっ！<line-break>どうして、こんなところに魔物がいるのかな？<line-break>迷子かも？<end><if><value:$01><equal>26なんと禍々しい！<line-break>すさまじい妖気が渦巻いておる！<end><if><value:$01><equal>24こんなところに魔物が！？<line-break>行きます！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5こっちは急いでるってのに！<line-break>うぜーんだよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>25王宮に魔物が…<line-break>これはいったい！？<line-break>何が起ころうとしているの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>6なんだか、すごく嫌な予感がする。<line-break>絶対、急いだほうがいいよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>7な、なんなの。この異様な気配は…？<line-break>何か恐ろしいモノが<line-break>動き出そうとしてる…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>8これは…絶望ですか。<line-break>これほどの念を発するなんて…。<line-break>人間とは壮絶な生き物なのですね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>9こんなところに魔物がいるってのは<line-break>どう考えてもマズイことが起こってるよねー。<line-break>ちょっち、覚悟したほうがよさそうね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>11こ、この闇の波動は…。<line-break>ま、魔人！？<line-break>いいえ…これはもっと深い闇…<end><if><value:$01><equal>12ちっ…見慣れた光景に凶悪な魔物が<line-break>いるってのはあまりいい気分はしないな。<line-break>じゃ、行こうか。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…魔物の進入を許すとは。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10ね、姉ちゃん！<line-break>ど、どうしてこんなところに<line-break>魔物がいるんだよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14むぅ。こんなところに魔物が出よるとわ。<line-break>ゆくぞい！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、こんなところに魔物が出るなんて<line-break>それになんだか…嫌な感じがするよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>16虚無の子よ…。<line-break>何を企んでいる？<end><if><value:$01><equal>17な、なに、なんなのよ。<line-break>この汚らわしい気配は<line-break>なにがそんなに怨めしいのよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>23この禍々しい気配は…。<line-break>エンシャントで感じたものと同じか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ううー。<line-break>なんだか、すごく嫌な予感するよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…この気配。<line-break>闇のモノか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>21何者かが<line-break>私の計画に介入しようとしている…<line-break>邪魔はさせん！<end><if><value:$01><equal>22こんなところに魔物が？<line-break>これはタダのクーデターでは<line-break>ないかもしれないよ。<end>この力は…<line-break>…円卓の騎士か？　それとも？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2W-why are there monsters here...? Here they come!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Monsters like these before the royal castle... This is no ordinary matter...<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Eek! Why are there monsters here? Maybe they're lost?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What a sinister presence! A tremendous demonic aura is swirling around them!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Monsters, here?! Let's go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5We're in a hurry here! What a pain in the ass!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Monsters in the palace... What is this?! What's about to happen?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6I've got a really bad feeling about this. We definitely need to hurry.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7W-what is this strange presence...? Something terrible is beginning to stir...<end><if><value:$01><equal>8Is this... despair? To give off such powerful emotions... Humans truly are extraordinary creatures.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Monsters showing up here means something bad is happening, no matter how you look at it. We should probably brace ourselves.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Th-this wave of darkness... A demon?! No... this is an even deeper darkness...<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Tch... Seeing vicious monsters in such familiar surroundings isn't a pleasant feeling. Well, let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13...To think they allowed monsters to enter.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10S-sis! Why are there monsters here?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Hmm. Monsters appearing in a place like this... Let's go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, monsters are appearing here... And something feels... wrong.<end><if><value:$01><equal>16Child of the Void... What are you plotting?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Wh-what? What is this filthy presence? What does it hate so deeply?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23This sinister presence... Is it the same one I felt in Ancient?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Ugh... I've got a really bad feeling about this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...This presence. A creature of darkness?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21Someone is trying to interfere with my plans... I won't allow it!<end><if><value:$01><equal>22Monsters here? This may be more than a simple coup.<end>This power... A Knight of the Round Table? Or something else?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2왜 이런 곳에 마물이… 온다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3이런 마물이 왕성 앞에…. 보통 일이 아닌 것 같군….<end><if><value:$01><equal>4꺅! 왜 이런 곳에 마물이 있지? 길을 잃었나?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26이 무슨 흉흉함이냐! 엄청난 요기가 소용돌이치고 있다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24이런 곳에 마물이!? 갑니다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5이쪽은 바쁘다고! 성가시게 굴지 마!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25왕궁에 마물이…. 이게 대체!? 무슨 일이 벌어지려는 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6왠지 엄청 불길한 예감이 들어. 무조건 서두르는 게 좋아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7뭐, 뭐야. 이 이상한 기척은…? 뭔가 끔찍한 것이 움직이기 시작하려 해….<end><if><value:$01><equal>8이건… 절망인가요. 이 정도의 염을 내다니…. 인간은 장렬한 생물이군요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9이런 곳에 마물이 있다는 건 아무리 생각해도 좋지 않은 일이 벌어졌다는 거네. 각오하는 게 좋겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11이, 이 어둠의 파동은…. 마, 마인!? 아니… 더 깊은 어둠….<end><if><value:$01><equal>12쳇… 익숙한 풍경에 흉악한 마물이 있는 건 별로 기분 좋지 않군. 자, 갈까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…마물의 침입을 허용하다니.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누, 누나! 왜 이런 곳에 마물이 있는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14으음. 이런 곳에 마물이 나타나다니. 간다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있잖아, 이런 곳에 마물이 나오다니. 게다가 왠지… 불길한 느낌이 들어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>16허무의 아이여…. 무슨 꿍꿍이지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 뭐, 뭐야. 이 더러운 기척은. 뭐가 그렇게 원망스러운 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23이 흉흉한 기척은…. 엔샨트에서 느낀 것과 같은 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19으으…. 왠지 엄청 불길한 예감이 들어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…이 기척. 어둠의 것인가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21누군가 내 계획에 개입하려 하고 있다…. 방해하게 두지 않겠다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>22이런 곳에 마물이? 단순한 쿠데타가 아닐지도 몰라.<end>이 힘은… 원탁의 기사인가? 아니면?<end>"

## 1350311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国政を牛耳る<line-break>毒婦エリス！<line-break>ロストールのため、お前を成敗する！<end>"
EN: "Eris! You vile woman, with the government in your grip! For Rostorl, I'll cut you down!<end>"
KO: "국정을 쥐고 흔드는 독부 에리스! 로스톨을 위해 너를 처단하겠다!<end>"

## 1350312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無礼者！<end>"
EN: "Insolent wretch!<end>"
KO: "무례한 놈!<end>"

## 1350313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰の許しを得たか！<line-break>さがるがよい！<end>"
EN: "Who gave you leave to do this? Stand down!<end>"
KO: "누구 허락을 받았느냐! 물러가라!<end>"

## 1350314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セルモノー陛下の仰せだ。<line-break>エリス・ファーロス…<end>"
EN: "It is His Majesty Selmono's command, Eris Pharos...<end>"
KO: "셀모노 폐하의 명이다. 에리스 파로스….<end>"

## 1350315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国に害をなすエリスと<line-break>ファーロス家を除くように、とな。<end>"
EN: "Eris and House Pharos are a blight upon the realm, and are to be removed.<end>"
KO: "나라에 해를 끼치는 에리스와 파로스 가문을 제거하라는 거군.<end>"

## 1350316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれ！<end>"
EN: "Do it!<end>"
KO: "해치워!<end>"

## 1350317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぎょ、御意っ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ウ、ウオオオオオオオ！！<end>"
EN: "Y-Yes, sir! R-RRAAAAAAGH!!<end>"
KO: "여, 명을 받들겠습니다! 우, 우오오오오오!!<end>"

## 1350318
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティ…ティアナ…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お前は…生きて…。<end>"
EN: "Ti... Tiana... You must... live...<end>"
KO: "티… 티아나…. 너는… 살아서….<end>"

## 1350319
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様、<line-break>お静かに…<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>... Keep quiet...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님, 조용히….<end>"

## 1350320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…悲鳴？<line-break>…血の匂い？<line-break>誰か…死んだ？<end>"
EN: "A scream? The smell of blood? Has someone... died?<end>"
KO: "…비명? …피 냄새? 누가… 죽었어?<end>"

## 1350321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノ、ノーブル伯様！？<line-break>もう手が回って…<end>"
EN: "C-Count Noble?! They've already reached us...<end>"
KO: "노, 노블 백작님!? 벌써 손을 쓰신 건가….<end>"

## 1350322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫です。<line-break><value:$28>様は、<line-break>私たちの味方です。<end>"
EN: "It's all right. <value:$28> is on our side.<end>"
KO: "괜찮습니다. <value:$28> 님은 저희 편입니다.<end>"

## 1350323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わっ、わかりました。<line-break>アトレイア様がそうおっしゃるので<line-break>あれば…<end>"
EN: "I-I understand. If you say so, Lady Atleia...<end>"
KO: "아, 알겠습니다. 아트레이아 님께서 그렇게 말씀하신다면…<end>"

## 1350325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、大変です。レ、レムオン様が<line-break>クーデターを！<end>"
EN: "I-It's terrible! L-Lord Lemghon has launched a coup!<end>"
KO: "큰, 큰일입니다. 레, 레무온 님이 쿠데타를!<end>"

## 1350326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン様が！？<end>"
EN: "Lord Lemghon?!<end>"
KO: "레무온 님이!?<end>"

## 1350328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母様が…<end>"
EN: "My aunt...<end>"
KO: "숙모님이…<end>"

## 1350329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様！<line-break>早く、ここからお逃げください。<line-break>兵士たちがすぐにやってきます！<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia! You must flee at once. The soldiers will be here any moment!<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님! 빨리 여기서 도망치세요. 병사들이 곧 옵니다!<end>"

## 1350330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…秘密通路から、<line-break>脱出しましょう。<end>"
EN: "...Let's escape through the secret passage.<end>"
KO: "…비밀 통로로 탈출하죠.<end>"

## 1350331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私のことはかまいません。<line-break>早くお逃げください。<end>"
EN: "Never mind me. Please, hurry and get out of here.<end>"
KO: "저는 신경 쓰지 마세요. 어서 도망치십시오.<end>"

## 1350332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、いないだと？<line-break>アトレイア様を押さえそこねるとは！<line-break>１隊はここに残れ、残りは他を捜せ！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, she's gone? How could we fail to secure Lady Atleia? One squad stays here. The rest of you, search elsewhere!<end>"
KO: "큭, 없다고? 아트레이아 님을 놓치다니! 한 부대는 여기 남고 나머지는 다른 곳을 찾아라!<end>"

## 1350333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助っ人ありがとさん。<line-break>おかげで勝つことができた。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for the help. Couldn't have won without you.<end>"
KO: "도와줘서 고맙다. 덕분에 이길 수 있었어.<end>"

## 1350334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラを討ち取れなかったのは、<line-break>仕方ないさ。気にするな。<line-break>ま、これも運命だったってことだ。<end>"
EN: "Karula wasn't taken down, but it couldn't be helped. Don't let it bother you. Well, I guess that was fate too.<end>"
KO: "카를라를 쓰러뜨리지 못한 건 어쩔 수 없어. 신경 쓰지 마. 뭐, 이것도 운명이었던 거겠지.<end>"

## 1350335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいでなすった。<line-break>これも運命ってことだな。<end>"
EN: "Well, look who's here. Fate again, I suppose.<end>"
KO: "오셨군. 이것도 운명이라는 거겠지.<end>"

## 1350336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス・ファーロス！<line-break>物資横領および収賄の罪で<line-break>貴様を逮捕する！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes Pharos! You are under arrest for embezzling supplies and accepting bribes!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 파로스! 물자 횡령 및 뇌물수수 혐의로 네놈을 체포한다!<end>"

## 1350338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙れ！　英雄きどりの奸臣が！<line-break>貴様には逆賊エリスと手を組み<line-break>国家を転覆しようとした疑いもあるのだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Silence, you treacherous courtier playing the hero! You are also suspected of conspiring with the rebel Eris to overthrow the state!<end>"
KO: "닥쳐라! 영웅인 척하는 간신배가! 네놈은 역적 에리스와 손잡고 국가를 전복하려 했다는 혐의도 있다.<end>"

## 1350339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前さんこそ、黙るんだな。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ヘタに俺の機嫌を損ねたら<line-break>俺がどうして剣狼って呼ばれてるか<line-break>思い知ることになるぜ？<end>"
EN: "You'd better be the one to keep quiet. Get on my bad side, and you'll find out why they call me the Sword Wolf.<end>"
KO: "…네가 입 다물어. 괜히 내 기분을 거슬렀다간 내가 왜 검랑이라 불리는지 똑똑히 알게 될 거다.<end>"

## 1350340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…<end>"
EN: "Ngh...<end>"
KO: "큭…<end>"

## 1350341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そういうわけで、<line-break>当分、会えなくなりそうだ。<line-break>別れはつらいな。<line-break>んじゃ、ま、長生きしろ。<line-break>長生きするにこしたことはねぇ。<line-break>俺の所に早く来るなよ。<end>"
EN: "So that's that. Looks like we won't be seeing each other for a while. Parting's hard. Well, live a long life. Nothing beats a long life. Don't come to me too soon.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 이유로 당분간 못 만나겠네. 이별은 괴롭군. 그럼, 오래 살아. 오래 사는 게 나쁘진 않으니까.<end>"

## 1350342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、来い！<line-break>反逆者ゼネテス！<end>"
EN: "C-come along, traitor Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "오, 오너라! 반역자 제네테스!<end>"

## 1350343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様なら<line-break>今、はずしてらっしゃる。<line-break>本当に、忙しい人だ。<end>何もかも自分一人で背負い込んで…。<line-break>どうやら、ほとんど、<line-break>眠ってらっしゃらないようだ。<end>"
EN: "Lady Khryuseis is away at the moment. She really is a busy woman.<end>She takes everything upon herself... It seems she barely sleeps at all.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님이라면 지금 자리를 비우셨습니다. 정말 바쁜 분이죠.<end>모든 걸 혼자 짊어지고…. 아무래도 거의 주무시지 못하는 것 같습니다.<end>"

## 1350344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん<line-break>あなたの存在は解放軍に<line-break>かくべからざるものです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you are indispensable to the Liberation Army.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 당신의 존재는 해방군에 없어서는 안 됩니다.<end>"

## 1350345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml

## 1350361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350362

## 1350363

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハァイ、ちょうどいいとこに来たわ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>いよいよ、ロストールを攻略するわよ。<end>"

KO: "하이, 마침 잘 왔어, <value:$28>. 이제 로스톨을 공략할 거야.<end>"

## 1350366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様が敵国深くのゼグナにある<line-break>闇の神器を取りにいくってっからさ。<line-break>放っとくわけにもいかないじゃん？<end>"
EN: "Lord Nemea says he's going deep into enemy territory to Zegna to retrieve the Dark Artifact. We can't very well leave him to it, can we?<end>"
KO: "네메아 님이 적국 깊숙한 제그나에 있는 어둠의 신기를 가지러 간다잖아. 내버려 둘 순 없지?<end>"

## 1350367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、このタイミングになったわけ。<line-break>しっかし、あのお方も<line-break>何考えてんだか、わかんないよね。<line-break>ま、今回の戦、<line-break>たぶん勝てるから<line-break>いいんだけどね。<end>"
EN: "That's why we're making our move now. Still, who knows what he's thinking? Not that it matters. We'll probably win this battle.<end>"
KO: "그래서 이 타이밍이 된 거야. 그런데 그분도 무슨 생각인지 모르겠네. 뭐, 이번 전쟁은 아마 이길 테니 상관없지만.<end>"

## 1350368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、なぜなのです。<line-break>カルラ様。<end>"
EN: "Why is that, Lady Karula?<end>"
KO: "그건 왜입니까, 카를라 님.<end>"

## 1350369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争は戦場だけでやるもんじゃないの。<line-break>まあ、その辺は<line-break>あとのお楽しみってとこかな。<end>"
EN: "Wars aren't fought only on the battlefield. But I'll let you find out the rest later.<end>"
KO: "전쟁은 전장에서만 하는 게 아니야. 뭐, 그 부분은 나중의 즐거움으로 남겨 둘까.<end>"

## 1350370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、アンギルダン将軍に勝った<line-break>ゼネテスが相手ですよ。<end>"
EN: "But we're facing Xenetes, the man who defeated General Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "하지만 앙길단 장군을 이긴 제네테스가 상대예요.<end>"

## 1350371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくらゼネテスでも、<line-break>兵隊がいなくちゃ勝てないでしょ。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしがつかんだ情報だと、<line-break>向こうは仲違いしてて、<line-break>ゼネテスは私兵しか使えないみたいよ。<end>"
EN: "Even Xenetes can't win without soldiers. According to my sources, they've had a falling-out, so it looks like he can only use his private troops.<end>"
KO: "아무리 제네테스라도 병사가 없으면 이길 수 없겠지. 내가 잡은 정보로는 저쪽이 사이가 틀어져서 제네테스는 사병밖에 못 쓴대.<end>"

## 1350372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな…！<end>"
EN: "That can't be...!<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가…!<end>"

## 1350373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の戦、勝ちは決まり。<line-break>ま、奇跡でゼネテスは１回なら<line-break>勝利を収められるかもしれないけど…<end>"
EN: "This battle is as good as won. With a miracle, Xenetes might manage one victory, but...<end>"
KO: "이번 전쟁은 우리가 이겼어. 뭐, 기적이 일어나 제네테스가 한 번쯤 이길 수도 있겠지만….<end>"

## 1350374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "２回、あたしたちをくい止めらんない。<line-break>つまり、あと２回の戦いで<line-break>ロストールは滅ぶわけ。<end>"
EN: "He can't stop us twice. In other words, Rostorl will fall after two more battles.<end>"
KO: "두 번, 우리를 막지 못하면 돼. 그러니까 앞으로 두 번의 전투로 로스톨은 멸망하는 거야.<end>"

## 1350375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、どうせなら１回で勝ちたいよねー。<line-break>くすくす…<end>"
EN: "Still, it'd be nice to win on the first try. Hee hee...<end>"
KO: "그래도 이왕이면 한 번에 이기고 싶잖아. 후후….<end>"

## 1350376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Hm? What is it, Irene?<end>"

## 1350378
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の戦い、私に指揮を<line-break>とらせてください。<end>"

## 1350379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、しゃーないわね。<line-break>そういうことだから、<line-break><value:$28>、助けてあげてね。<end>"

## 1350385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本国からの連絡ではないのね？<line-break>じゃあ、情報の出所はどこなの？<end>"
EN: "This didn't come from the homeland? Then where did the information come from?<end>"
KO: "본국에서 온 연락은 아니지? 그럼 정보 출처는 어디야?<end>"

## 1350387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それが…、はっきりしないのです。<line-break>突然広まったものですから。<end>"
EN: "W-we aren't certain. It just started spreading all of a sudden.<end>"
KO: "그, 그것이… 확실하지 않습니다. 갑자기 퍼진 소문이라서요.<end>"

## 1350388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし思うんだけどさ…。　　<line-break>それってたぶん、ニセ情報よ。<line-break>目の前の敵はおとり。<line-break>こうやってあたしたちが<line-break>混乱してるところに<line-break>奇襲がくるってな感じかしら？<end>"
EN: "You know what I think? It's probably false information. The enemy in front of us is a decoy. While we're in disarray like this, they'll hit us with a surprise attack. That's my guess.<end>"
KO: "내 생각에는 말이야…. 그거 아마 가짜 정보야. 눈앞의 적은 미끼고, 이렇게 우리가 혼란스러워하는 틈에 기습이 오는 식 아닐까?<end>"

## 1350389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "か、カ、カルラ様！<line-break>大変です！<end>"
EN: "L-L-Lady Karula! We have an emergency!<end>"
KO: "카, 카, 카를라 님! 큰일입니다!<end>"

## 1350390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そら来た。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>はいはい、落ち着いて。<line-break>　<line-break>ロストール軍が攻めてきたって<line-break>言うんでしょ。<line-break>それも、後ろから。<end>"
EN: "There it is. All right, all right, calm down. You're going to tell me the Rostorl army has attacked, aren't you? From behind.<end>"
KO: "올 게 왔네. 자자, 진정해. 로스톨군이 공격해 왔다고 말하려는 거지. 그것도 뒤에서.<end>"

## 1350391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな！<line-break>私がもたついたばかりに…！<end>"
EN: "N-no! All because I was too slow...!<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 수가! 제가 꾸물거리는 바람에…!<end>"

## 1350392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気にしないの。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>これだけの人間の命を預かってんだし、<line-break>状況の把握につとめようとした<line-break>指揮に間違いはなかったよ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry about it. You have all these lives in your hands. There was nothing wrong with your decision to get a clear picture of the situation.<end>"
KO: "신경 쓰지 마. 이렇게 많은 사람의 목숨을 맡고 있으니 상황 파악에 힘쓰려 한 지휘는 잘못이 아니었어.<end>"

## 1350393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、しかし！<end>"
EN: "B-but!<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만!<end>"

## 1350394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスのほうが一枚上手だったのよ。<line-break>あたしだって、こんなに早く来るとは、<line-break>思ってなかったわ。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes was one step ahead of us. Even I didn't expect him to get here this quickly.<end>"
KO: "제네테스가 한 수 위였어. 나도 이렇게 빨리 올 줄은 몰랐거든.<end>"

## 1350395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、気持ちを切り替えなさい！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>兵の命を救いたかったら、<line-break>早めに引き上げるしかないわ。<line-break>しんがりにはあたしが出るわ。<end>"
EN: "Now, put it behind you! If you want to save the soldiers' lives, we have to withdraw while we still can. I'll take the rear guard.<end>"
KO: "자, 마음을 다잡아! 병사들의 목숨을 구하고 싶으면 일찍 철수할 수밖에 없어. 후미는 내가 맡을게.<end>"

## 1350397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今は、兵の命が大事よ。<end>"
EN: "Right now, the soldiers' lives come first.<end>"
KO: "지금은 병사들의 목숨이 중요해.<end>"

## 1350398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ！　そうではなく！<line-break>私の敗北で、カルラ様を危険な目に<line-break>あわせるわけには…<end>"
EN: "No, that isn't what I mean! I can't let my defeat put you in danger, Lady Karula...<end>"
KO: "아닙니다! 그런 뜻이 아니라! 제가 패배해서 카를라 님을 위험에 빠뜨릴 수는…<end>"

## 1350399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "여기서 맞아 싸울 거야. 지면 죽는 거니까 각오 단단히 해!<end>"

## 1350407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんとこういう形で　　<line-break>戦うことになるとはな。<line-break>　<line-break>人生ってのは、おもしれぇ。<line-break>なあ、<value:$28>、<line-break>手加減はしない。行くぜ！<end>"
EN: "Never thought I'd end up fighting you like this. Life's a funny thing. Listen, <value:$28>. I won't hold back. Here I come!<end>"
KO: "이런 식으로 너와 싸우게 될 줄이야. 인생이란 참 재미있군. 그렇지, <value:$28>. 봐주진 않겠다. 간다!<end>"

## 1350408
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここでお前を討ちとれば、<line-break>戦力差がどうあれ、<line-break>こっちの勝ちだ。<line-break>いくぜ！<end>"
EN: "If I bring you down here, we win, no matter how badly we're outnumbered. Here I come!<end>"
KO: "여기서 네놈을 쓰러뜨리면 전력 차가 어떻든 우리가 이긴다. 간다!<end>"

## 1350409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<line-break>強くなりやがって…。<line-break>　<line-break>へへ…、複雑な気分だな。<line-break>悪い気はしねぇよ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Damn... You've gotten strong... Heh. I don't quite know how to feel about that, but it isn't a bad feeling, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "큭…. 강해졌구나…. 헤헤… 복잡한 기분이네. 나쁜 기분은 아니야, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1350410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<line-break>カルラひとりならやれたものを！<end>"
EN: "Damn... If it had only been Karula, I could have won!<end>"
KO: "큭…. 카를라 혼자였다면 해치웠을 텐데!<end>"

## 1350412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さーて、味方もちゃんと<line-break>逃げ延びたみたいね。<line-break>あたしたちも退き時かな？<end>"
EN: "Well, it looks like our forces made it to safety. Perhaps it's time we withdrew too?<end>"
KO: "자, 아군도 제대로빠져나간 모양이네. 우리도 물러날 때인가?<end>"

## 1350413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっち気が早いけど…ほい、報酬。<line-break>ま、色々忙しくなりそうだからねー。<end>"
EN: "It's a little early, but here, your reward. Things are probably about to get rather busy.<end>"
KO: "조금 이르긴 하지만… 자, 보수. 뭐, 여러모로 바빠질 것 같으니까.<end>"

## 1350414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところでさー<line-break>さっき、ゼネテスは２回勝てないって<line-break>あたし、言ったよね。<line-break>それって、ゼネテスは１回勝ったら<line-break>もう、この世にいないからなんだ。<line-break>わかるかな、この意味？<end>"
EN: "By the way, remember when I said Xenetes couldn't win twice? That's because after his first victory, he won't be in this world anymore. Do you understand what that means?<end>"
KO: "그런데 말이야, 아까 내가 제네테스는 두 번 이길 수 없다고 했지? 그건 제네테스가 한 번 이기면 더는 이 세상에 없기 때문이야. 이 말뜻, 알겠어?<end>"

## 1350415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールで、エリス王妃と貴族が<line-break>対立してるのは知ってるよね。<line-break>ゼネテスはエリス王妃の甥。<end>"
EN: "You know Queen Eris and the nobles are at odds in Rostorl, don't you? Xenetes is Queen Eris's nephew.<end>"
KO: "로스톨에서 에리스 왕비와 귀족들이 대립하고 있는 건 알지? 제네테스는 에리스 왕비의 조카야.<end>"

## 1350416
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んで、貴族を率いてんのがレムオン。<line-break>この２人が王位継承者ティアナ王女を<line-break>めぐる恋敵だったりするらしいのよ。<line-break>要は仲が悪いってこと。<line-break>だから今回、わざと奇襲じゃなくて<line-break>ゆっくりと大軍勢を進撃させたわけ。<end>"
EN: "And Lemghon is the one leading the nobles. Apparently, those two are also rivals for the affections of Princess Tiana, heir to the throne. In short, they don't get along. That's why I deliberately marched a huge army in slowly instead of launching a surprise attack.<end>"
KO: "그리고 귀족들을 이끄는 게 레무온. 이 둘은 왕위 계승자인 티아나 왕녀를 두고 연적이기도 하다나 봐. 한마디로 사이가 나쁘다는 거지. 그래서 이번엔 일부러 기습하지 않고 대군을 천천히 진격시킨 거야.<end>"

## 1350417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "内部でもめる時間を与えるためにね。<line-break>予定通りレムオンは出兵拒否。<line-break>前の戦いでビビってた貴族も付和雷同。<line-break>で、王妃側が困って屈服すると思いきや、<line-break>ライバルのゼネテスが出ばって<line-break>あたしの軍を破っちゃった。<end>"
EN: "I wanted to give them time to quarrel among themselves. Lemghon refused to send troops, just as planned, and the nobles frightened by the last battle followed his lead. I thought the Queen's faction would be cornered and submit, but then Xenetes, Lemghon's rival, stepped forward and defeated my army.<end>"
KO: "내부에서 싸울 시간을 주려고 말이야. 예상대로 레무온은 출병을 거부했고, 지난 전투에서 겁먹은 귀족들도 덩달아 따랐지. 왕비 측이 곤란해져 굴복하나 했더니 경쟁자인 제네테스가 나서서 내 군대를 깨 버렸어.<end>"

## 1350418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオンは焦るよねー。<line-break>自分は悪役、向こうは救国の英雄だもん。<line-break>で、ふと足下を見ると、気がつくわけ。<line-break>ゼネテスは王妃の軍を残らず連れてった。<line-break>ロストールに王妃を守る兵隊はいない。<line-break>あたしという脅威も消えた。<end>"
EN: "Naturally, Lemghon is getting nervous. He's the villain now, while Xenetes is the hero who saved the kingdom. Then Lemghon looks at what is right under his nose and realizes something: Xenetes took every last one of the Queen's troops with him. There are no soldiers left in Rostorl to protect her, and the threat I posed is gone.<end>"
KO: "레무온은 초조하겠지. 자기는 악역이고 저쪽은 구국의 영웅이니까. 그러다 문득 발밑을 보면 깨닫는 거야. 제네테스가 왕비의 군대를 몽땅 데려갔고, 로스톨에는 왕비를 지킬 병사가 없어. 나라는 위협도 사라졌고.<end>"

## 1350419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残る邪魔者は？<line-break>そのとき、ゼネテスはどうなると思う？<end>"
EN: "So who's the last obstacle in his way? What do you think happens to Xenetes then?<end>"
KO: "남은 방해꾼은? 그때 제네테스는 어떻게 될 것 같아?<end>"

## 1350420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、お役ご免。<line-break>理由つけて殺されちゃう。<line-break>もったいないよねー、いい男なのにさ？<line-break>あたしはこれからリベルダムに戻って<line-break>ゼネテスが捕まった頃合いに<line-break>王都ロストールを攻めるわ。<end>"
EN: "That's right. He'll have outlived his usefulness. They'll invent some reason to kill him. What a waste, when he's such a fine man. I'll return to Liberdam now, then attack the Rostorl capital once Xenetes has been arrested.<end>"
KO: "그래, 용도 끝. 핑계 붙여서 죽임당하겠지. 아깝네, 좋은 남자인데? 나는 이제 리벨덤으로 돌아가서 제네테스가 붙잡힐 무렵 왕도 로스톨을 공격할 거야.<end>"

## 1350421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスのいない戦いなんて<line-break>あんたの手を借りるまでもない。<line-break> <line-break>だから、あんたには<line-break>自由時間をあ・げ・る。<end>"
EN: "Without Xenetes, I won't even need your help to win. So I'm giving you some free time.<end>"
KO: "제네테스가 없는 싸움이라면 네 손을 빌릴 것도 없어. 그러니 너한텐 자유 시간을 줄게.<end>"

## 1350422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "有効に使ってね。<end>"
EN: "Make good use of it.<end>"
KO: "유용하게 써.<end>"

## 1350423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、気にしないで。<line-break>今度こそ勝てばいいんだから。<line-break>行くわよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, don't worry about it. We just have to win next time. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "아, 신경 쓰지 마. 이번에 이기면 되니까. 가자.<end>"

## 1350424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　そして青竜将軍カルラのもとへは<line-break>　　　帝国宰相ベルゼーヴァより<line-break>　　　 一通の書状が届いていた。<end>"
EN: "Then a letter arrived for Azure Dragon General Karula from Imperial Chancellor Beelzeva.<end>"
KO: "그리고 청룡 장군 카를라에게는 제국 재상 벨제바로부터 한 통의 서신이 도착해 있었다.<end>"

## 1350425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王都ロストールでは貴族どもが<line-break>内輪もめを起こし、敵軍は無力化。<line-break>奇跡の将軍ゼネテスは処刑されました。<line-break>まさにカルラ将軍の策略、神のごとし！<line-break>我ら青竜軍、全軍意気軒昂なり！<line-break>いつでもロストールを占領できます！<end>"
EN: "The nobles in the Rostorl capital have turned on one another, leaving the enemy army powerless. Xenetes, the miracle general, has been executed. General Karula's strategy was nothing short of godlike! The entire Azure Dragon Army is in the highest of spirits! We stand ready to occupy Rostorl at any time!<end>"
KO: "왕도 로스톨에서는 귀족들이 내분을 일으켜 적군은 무력화! 기적의 장군 제네테스는 처형됐습니다! 실로 카를라 장군의 책략은 신과 같도다! 우리 청룡군 전군의 사기 충천! 언제든 로스톨을 점령할 수 있습니다!<end>"

## 1350426

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "宰相閣下様から退けって命令が来たの。<line-break>あたしが大軍かかえて<line-break>ロストールに居座るのが恐いんじゃん？<end>"
EN: "Our esteemed Chancellor has ordered us to withdraw. I suppose he's afraid I'll settle into Rostorl with this huge army of mine.<end>"
KO: "재상 각하한테서 물러나라는 명령이 왔어. 내가 대군을 거느리고 로스톨에 눌러앉는 게 무서운 거 아니야?<end>"

## 1350429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぬう！　ネメア陛下不在と見るや<line-break>即座に政治を独占、国利を捨て<line-break>保身に走るか、あのトンガリ頭め！<line-break>このままでは青竜軍の名折れ！<line-break>即座に帝都に軍を返し、玄武軍を<line-break>蹴散らして、あの髪を！<end>"
EN: "Grr! The moment His Majesty Nemea is away, that spike-headed wretch seizes control of the government, casts aside the good of the nation, and moves to protect himself! This will disgrace the Azure Dragon Army! We must turn our forces on the imperial capital at once, crush the Black Tortoise Army, and that hair of his...!<end>"
KO: "으으! 네메아 폐하가 부재하다고 보자마자 즉시 정치를 독점하고, 국익을 버리고 자기 보신에 나서는가, 저 뾰족머리 놈! 이대로라면 청룡군의 수치다! 당장 제도로 군을 돌려 현무군을 걷어차고, 그 머리카락을!<end>"

## 1350430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武軍は拠点防衛戦のエキスパートよ。<line-break>いくら青竜軍でも無傷で蹴散らせないわ。<line-break>アキュリュースには白虎軍がいるのよ。<end>"
EN: "The Black Tortoise Army specializes in defending strongholds. Even the Azure Dragon Army couldn't crush them without taking losses. And the White Tiger Army is in Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "현무군은 거점 방어전의 전문가야. 아무리 청룡군이라도 무상처로 쓸어버릴 수는 없어. 아큐류스에는 백호군도 있잖아.<end>"

## 1350431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…<end>"
EN: "Ah...<end>"
KO: "아…<end>"

## 1350432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークに漁夫の利を<line-break>得させることはないわ。<line-break>機会を待つの。<line-break>さっきの話は聞かなかったことにするわ。<line-break>ここじゃ、なんかあったとき遠いし<line-break>この国の隅々まで兵を割く余裕もない。<end>"
EN: "I won't let Zsilak profit while we fight each other. We'll wait for our chance. I'll pretend I didn't hear what you just said. We're too far away here if anything happens, and we can't spare enough soldiers to cover every corner of this country.<end>"
KO: "지라크에게 어부지리를 주진 않을 거야. 기회를 기다려. 아까 이야기는 못 들은 걸로 하지. 여긴 무슨 일이 생기면 멀기도 하고, 나라 구석구석까지 병력을 나눌 여유도 없어.<end>"

## 1350433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "青竜軍、全軍撤退する！<line-break>リベルダムに守備軍を残し<line-break>ロセンに戻る！　準備にかかれ！<end>"
EN: "The entire Azure Dragon Army will withdraw! Leave a garrison in Liberdam and return to Rocen! Make ready!<end>"
KO: "청룡군, 전군 철수한다! 리벨덤에 수비군을 남기고 로센으로 돌아간다! 준비해라!<end>"

## 1350435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァのやつ、命令書と一緒に<line-break>花束を贈ってきやがったのだ。<line-break>そのカードになんてあったと思う？<end>"
EN: "That bastard Beelzeva sent me a bouquet along with the order. What do you think the card said?<end>"
KO: "벨제바 녀석이 명령서와 함께 꽃다발을 보내왔어. 그 카드에 뭐라고 적혀 있었는지 알아?<end>"

## 1350436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…네 미소의 원수, 왕비 에리스는 죽었다.<end>"

## 1350437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君にも笑顔が戻ったことと思う。<line-break>心から祝福しよう。<line-break>おめでとう…だってさ。<end>"
EN: "I trust your smile has returned. Allow me to offer my heartfelt congratulations. Congratulations...” That's what it said.<end>"

## 1350448

## 1350449
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350450
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350451

## 1350454
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350455
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml

## 1350456
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350457

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "정말! 오빠 심술쟁이!<end>"

## 1350458
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても兄さん、<line-break>あの巨人は…？<line-break>私が手も足も出ないなんて…<end>"

KO: "그런데 오빠, 그 거인은…? 내가 꼼짝도 못 하다니…<end>"

## 1350459
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かなえられなかった願い。<line-break>行き場のない人の思い。<line-break>それらが形を持ったもの…闇の巨人だ。<line-break>非常に強力な魔物だ。<line-break>これまでにも世界を何度か<line-break>危機に陥れたことがある。<end>"
EN: "Unfulfilled wishes. The thoughts of people with nowhere to go. Given form, they become a dark giant―a tremendously powerful monster. It has plunged the world into crisis several times before.<end>"
KO: "이루지 못한 소원. 갈 곳 없는 사람의 마음. 그것들이 형태를 얻은 것… 바로 어둠의 거인이다. 매우 강력한 마물이지. 지금까지도 세계를 몇 번이나 위기에 빠뜨린 적이 있다.<end>"

## 1350460
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0あのボウヤ、<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主かもしれん。<end>あのお嬢ちゃん、<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主かもしれん。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0That boy may possess the Infinite Soul.<end>That girl may possess the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0저 꼬마, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자일지도 몰라.<end>저 아가씨, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자일지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1350461
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルの持ち主というと…<line-break>施文院の伝承に<line-break>世界の終わりに現れるという…<end>"
EN: "The possessor of the Infinite Soul... The Inscription Order tradition says such a person will appear at the end of the world...<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 지닌 자라면… 시문원의 전승에 세계의 종말에 나타난다는…<end>"

## 1350462
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かは終わっても世界は終わらんよ。<line-break>すべてが虚無に帰らない限りな。<line-break> <line-break>そして、破壊神ですら<line-break>世界を虚無にできなかった。<end>"
EN: "Something may come to an end, but not the world―not unless everything returns to the Void. Even the God of Destruction could not reduce the world to the Void.<end>"
KO: "무언가가 끝나도 세계는 끝나지 않아. 모든 것이 허무로 돌아가지 않는 한. 그리고 파괴신조차 세계를 허무로 만들지 못했지.<end>"

## 1350463
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そいつの無限のソウルにひかれて<line-break>かなえられなかった願いが巨人となり<line-break>現れちまったんだろうな。<line-break>あるいは大神官様のいたずらか？<line-break>世界を滅ぼすための<line-break>予行演習かもしれないな。<end>"
EN: "Well, an unfulfilled wish was probably drawn to that Infinite Soul, took the form of a giant, and appeared. Or perhaps it was some mischief by the exalted High Priest. Maybe it was a practice run for destroying the world.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 녀석의 무한의 소울에 이끌려 이루지 못한 소원이 거인이 되어 나타난 거겠지. 아니면 대신관님의 장난인가? 세계를 멸망시키기 위한 예행연습일지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1350464
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんな奴に様付けしないで！<line-break>お兄ちゃ…兄さんがこんな身体に<line-break>なったのも、あいつのせいなんだから！<end>"
EN: "Don't call that wretch “exalted”! Big bro―Brother ended up in this condition because of that wretch!<end>"
KO: "그런 녀석한테 님 붙이지 마! 오, 오빠가 이런 몸이 된 것도 저 녀석 때문이잖아!<end>"

## 1350465
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どんな身体になっても生きていられる。<line-break>自分の意志に従って生きていける。<line-break>妹の笑顔が見られる。それで十分だ。<end>"
EN: "Whatever form my body takes, I can go on living. I can live according to my own will. I can see my sister smile. That's enough.<end>"
KO: "어떤 몸이 되어도 살아갈 수 있다. 자신의 의지에 따라 살아갈 수 있다. 여동생의 웃는 얼굴을 볼 수 있다. 그걸로 충분해.<end>"

## 1350466
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァイ、ヴィア、こいつを送ってやれ。<line-break>こいつは、今でこそこんなザマだが、<line-break>世界の未来を変えうる存在になる。<end>"
EN: "Vai, Via, see this one off. A sorry sight now, perhaps, but someday capable of changing the future of the world.<end>"

## 1350472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んなわけねーわな。<line-break>感覚が残ってるとこには<line-break>鋭く痛みがある。呪いは健在だ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, right. Wherever I still have feeling, the pain's sharp as ever. The curse is alive and well.<end>"
KO: "그럴 리가 없지. 감각이 남은 곳엔 날카로운 통증이 있어. 저주는 건재해.<end>"

## 1350476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまり呪いの解き方もわからないのに<line-break>ただ、でくの坊みたいにオロオロと<line-break>徹夜でここにいたってのか、こいつは？<end>"

## 1350477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつの無限のソウルのおかげで<line-break>俺は死ぬことだけは免れた<line-break>……ってことはないか。<end>"
EN: "Could that Infinite Soul have at least kept me from dying...?<end>"
KO: "이 녀석의 무한의 소울 덕분에 내가 죽는 것만은 피했다… 그런 건가.<end>"

## 1350478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そんなご利益はないにしろ<line-break>こいつの近くにいると、俺みたいな奴も<line-break>善人になりそうだ。ずらかるとしよう。<end>"
EN: "Well, miracle or no miracle, staying this close might turn even someone like me decent. Time to clear out.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 효험은 없더라도 이 녀석 곁에 있으면 나 같은 놈도 착한 사람이 될 것 같군. 사라져 주지.<end>"

## 1350479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇっ<line-break>しょうがねえな。<line-break>ったく！<end>"

## 1350480
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "夜中に徘徊する不審人物発見。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>……って、<line-break>俺も他人のコトは言えないか。<line-break>俺に用かい？<end>"
EN: "Suspicious character spotted prowling around in the middle of the night. Though I suppose I'm in no position to talk. Need something from me?<end>"
KO: "한밤중에 배회하는 수상한 인물 발견. ……이라고 해도 나도 남 말할 처지는 아니군. 내게 볼일 있어?<end>"

## 1350481
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、あばよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, see you around.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 잘 있어.<end>"

## 1350482
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…別に他にも<line-break>用事はないってわけかい？<end>"
EN: "So... was there something else you wanted?<end>"
KO: "…달리 볼일이 있는 건 아니지?<end>"

## 1350483
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや。あんたに会えそうな<line-break>気がして、待ってたら<line-break>…ホントに来たよ。<line-break>俺は賭け事には<line-break>弱いハズなんだがな。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. I had a feeling you might show up, so I waited... and here you are. Strange. Gambling's never been my strong suit.<end>"
KO: "이런. 자네를 만날 것 같은 느낌이 들어 기다렸더니… 정말 왔군. 나는 도박에는 약한 편인데 말이지.<end>"

## 1350484
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かなり運を使っちまったようだ。<line-break>当分、ツキはなさそうだ。<end>"
EN: "Looks like that used up a fair bit of my luck. I doubt I'll have another lucky break for a while.<end>"
KO: "운을 꽤 써버린 모양이군. 당분간은 운이 없겠어.<end>"

## 1350485
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…もしかして、の話だけど<line-break>シャリが俺に言ったこと気にして<line-break>それで、あんた、朝までここにいたの？<end>"
EN: "Just a thought, but... did you stay here until morning because you were worried about what Shalli said to me?<end>"
KO: "…혹시 말인데, 샤리가 나한테 한 말 신경 쓰여서 그래서 네가 아침까지 여기 있었던 거야?<end>"

## 1350486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出たよ。<line-break>お得意のダンマリだ。<line-break>ホント、あんたって食えない女だよ。<end>"
EN: "There it is. That silence you're so good at. You really are an inscrutable woman.<end>"
KO: "또 시작이네. 특기인 묵묵부답이야. 정말, 당신은 속을 모르겠는 여자라니까.<end>"

## 1350487
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行かねえよ。行くわけないだろ？<line-break>来いって言うからには<line-break>敵さんには用意があんだからよ。<end>"
EN: "I'm not going. Of course I'm not. If the enemy's inviting me over, you can bet there's something waiting for me.<end>"
KO: "안 가. 갈 리가 없잖아? 오라고 한 이상 적도 준비를 해 놨을 테니까.<end>"

## 1350488
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただな…<line-break>うずくんだよ<line-break>心が。<end>"
EN: "It's just... my heart aches.<end>"
KO: "그런데 말이야… 욱신거려. 마음이.<end>"

## 1350489
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "双子とのことを聞いてみる<end>"
EN: "Ask about the twins<end>"
KO: "쌍둥이 일을 물어본다<end>"

## 1350490
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっとしておく<end>"
EN: "Leave things be<end>"
KO: "가만히 내버려 둔다<end>"

## 1350491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おせっかいだな、あんた。<end>"
EN: "You really can't help meddling, can you?<end>"
KO: "참견이 심하군, 당신.<end>"

## 1350492
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞かないのな。<end>"
EN: "You're not going to ask?<end>"
KO: "안 묻는구나.<end>"

## 1350493
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妹たちとのことだよ。<line-break>見てたんだろ？<end>"
EN: "About what happened with my younger sisters. You saw it, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "여동생들 이야기야. 보고 있었지?<end>"

## 1350494
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなこと言えた義理じゃないが、<line-break>あいつらのこと…頼む。<end>"
EN: "I have no right to ask this, but... please look after them.<end>"
KO: "내가 이런 말 할 처지는 아니지만, 그 녀석들… 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1350495
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、俺のことは<line-break>あいつらには伝えないでくれ。<line-break>絶対にだ。<end>"
EN: "Just don't tell them about me. No matter what.<end>"
KO: "다만 내 이야기는 그 녀석들에게 하지 말아 줘. 절대로.<end>"

## 1350496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてもだ。<line-break>もし、しゃべったら、もう会わない。<line-break>いいな？<end>"
EN: "I mean it. If you tell them, you'll never see me again. Understood?<end>"
KO: "무조건이다. 만약 말하면 다시는 안 만나. 알겠지?<end>"

## 1350497
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう朝か。<line-break>おい、エルファスが俺にかけた<line-break>呪いのことは知ってるんだよな？<end>"
EN: "Morning already? Hey, you know about the curse Elfas put on me, don't you?<end>"
KO: "벌써 아침인가. 야, 엘파스가 내게 건저주에 대해서는 알고 있지?<end>"

## 1350498
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "平気なように見えてかなり痛い。<line-break>痛いなんてもんじゃない。<line-break>だが、平然としてる、わかるか？<end>"
EN: "I may look fine, but it hurts. A lot. No, \"hurts\" doesn't begin to cover it. Yet here I am, acting as if nothing's wrong. Understand?<end>"
KO: "멀쩡해 보여도 꽤 아파. 아프다는 정도가 아니야. 그래도 태연하게 있는 거야, 알겠어?<end>"

## 1350499
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "痛がっても、どうしようもない。<line-break>ただ、エルファスを喜ばせるだけだ。<line-break>だから、俺は平然として見せてる。<end>"
EN: "Showing the pain wouldn't help. It would only give Elfas satisfaction. That's why I pretend I'm fine.<end>"
KO: "아파해 봐야 소용없다. 엘파스를 기쁘게 할 뿐이야. 그래서 난 아무렇지도 않은 척하고 있어.<end>"

## 1350500
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントのとこ、<line-break>もう手も足も動かない。<line-break> <line-break>魔法でごまかして<line-break>動くように見せてるだけだ。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, I can't move my arms or legs anymore. I'm using magic to make it look as though I can.<end>"
KO: "솔직히 말하면 이제 손발도 움직이지 않아. 마법으로 속여서 움직이는 것처럼 보이게 할 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1350501
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう長くない。<line-break>だが、後悔はしてない。<line-break> <line-break>俺は自分だけのために、<line-break>自分の意志で生きることが<line-break>できたからな。<end>"
EN: "I don't have long. But I have no regrets. I managed to live for myself alone, by my own will.<end>"
KO: "이제 오래 못 가. 하지만 후회는 없어. 난 나 자신만을 위해 내 의지로 살아갈 수 있었으니까.<end>"

## 1350502
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また朝だな、おい<line-break>あんたと朝を迎えるの<line-break>何度目だっけ？<line-break>ま、んなこた、どうでもいっか。<end>"
EN: "Morning again. Hey, how many mornings have we seen in together now? Ah, who cares.<end>"
KO: "또 아침이네. 너랑 아침을 맞는 게 몇 번째더라? 뭐, 그런 건 아무래도 좋나.<end>"

## 1350503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガタガタの身体かかえてるから言える。<line-break>もう俺は立ち直れないから言える。<line-break>再生していく物に少し嫉妬しながらな。<end>"
EN: "I can say this because my body's falling apart. Because I know I'll never recover. Though I can't help feeling a little jealous of the things that can.<end>"
KO: "망가진 몸을 안고 있으니까 할 수 있는 말이야. 난 이제 다시 일어설 수 없으니까 할 수 있어. 다시 태어나는 것들을 조금 질투하면서 말이지.<end>"

## 1350504
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガタガタの街が立ち直っていくのは<line-break>いいなあ…<line-break>朝が来るのもいいもんだなあ。<end>"
EN: "It's good to see a city in ruins putting itself back together. Seeing the morning come isn't so bad either.<end>"
KO: "엉망이던 거리가 다시 일어서는 건 좋구나…. 아침이 오는 것도 나쁘지 않네.<end>"

## 1350505
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意地張ってなんでもない顔して<line-break>生きて、生ききってやるつもりだったが、<line-break>やっぱ時が流れるのが恐かったんだ。<end>"
EN: "I'd meant to be stubborn, pretend nothing was wrong, and live right to the bitter end. But I suppose I was afraid of time passing after all.<end>"
KO: "고집 부리며 아무렇지 않은 얼굴로 살아가고 끝까지 살아낼 생각이었지만, 역시 시간이 흐르는 게 무서웠어.<end>"

## 1350506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、今は、てらいも意地もなく<line-break>こうやって朝日が昇るのを見てられる。<line-break>不思議な気分だな。<end>"
EN: "But now I can watch the sun rise like this, without any pretense or stubborn pride. It's a strange feeling.<end>"
KO: "하지만 지금은 허세도 고집도 없이 이렇게 아침 해가 뜨는 걸 보고 있어. 이상한 기분이군.<end>"

## 1350507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな、これが本当に<line-break>自分のために生きてるってことだな。<line-break>意地でも、あてつけでもなく…<end>"
EN: "Yes... this is what it really means to live for myself. Not out of stubbornness, or to spite anyone.<end>"
KO: "그래, 이게 정말 자기를 위해 사는 거겠지. 오기도, 남에게 보여 주려는 것도 아니고….<end>"

## 1350508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとよ…<line-break><value:$28>…<end>"
EN: "Thanks... <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "고맙다… <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1350509
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、あんたとは会わない。<end>"
EN: "I won't see you again.<end>"
KO: "이제 당신과는 만나지 않아.<end>"

## 1350510
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが<line-break>自分のために生きるってことなんだよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "This is what it means to live for myself, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이게 자신을 위해 산다는 거야, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1350511
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "虚無の剣か？<line-break>闇の神器の１つで強力な魔道器だ。<line-break>鞘から抜けばすさまじい魔力が得られる。<line-break>だが、その代わりに<line-break>使用者は無にされてしまう。<end>"
EN: "The Sword of the Void? It's one of the Dark Artifacts, a powerful magical device. Draw it from its sheath and it grants you tremendous magical power. But in return, the wielder is reduced to nothing.<end>"
KO: "허무의 검인가? 어둠의 신기 중 하나인 강력한 마도기다. 칼집에서 뽑으면 엄청난 마력을 얻지만 대신 사용자는 무로 돌아간다.<end>"

## 1350512
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死ぬんじゃない。<line-break>一切の力を失い、<line-break>そして世界中から忘れ去られるんだ。<line-break>最初からいなかったことになる。<line-break>いたことすら消えてしまうのさ。<end>"
EN: "You don't die. You lose every last bit of your power, and the whole world forgets you. It's as if you never existed. Even the fact that you ever existed is erased.<end>"
KO: "죽는 게 아니야. 모든 힘을 잃고 세상 모두에게 잊히는 거다. 처음부터 존재하지 않았던 게 되는 거야. 존재했다는 사실조차 사라져.<end>"

## 1350513
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔、ゼネテスとつるんでたことなんて<line-break>忘れちまってたな。<line-break>これも虚無の剣のお力か。<end>"
EN: "I'd forgotten that Xenetes and I used to run together. Another blessing from the Sword of the Void, I suppose.<end>"
KO: "옛날 제네테스와 어울렸던 일 따윈 잊고 있었군. 이것도 허무의 검의 힘인가.<end>"

## 1350514
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いが<line-break>もうこの話は終わりにしてくれ。<line-break>ひとつだけ言えることは…<line-break>ゼネテスならあいつをとめられたんだ。<line-break>俺じゃあ、いくら言っても<line-break>あいつをとめられなかったけど、な。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but I don't want to talk about this anymore. There's only one thing I can tell you: Xenetes could have stopped that person. No matter what I said, I couldn't.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 이 이야기는 이제 끝내 줘. 하나만 말하자면… 제네테스라면 그 녀석을 멈출 수 있었어. 나는 아무리 말해도 그 녀석을 멈추지 못했지만.<end>"

## 1350515
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺がシャリの誘いに乗らないっつって<line-break>のこのこ霊峰トールまで行ったこと…<line-break>もしかして、今さら怒ってんの？<end>"
EN: "Are you still angry that I said I wouldn't take Shalli up on his invitation, then went sauntering off to Sacred Mount Thor anyway?<end>"
KO: "내가 샤리의 제안을 거절하겠다고 해놓고 멍청하게 영봉 토르까지 간 일… 혹시 이제 와서 화난 거야?<end>"

## 1350516
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出たよ。またダンマリだ。<line-break>俺がウソをつくなんて<line-break>日常茶飯事だろ？<end>"
EN: "There it is. The silent treatment again. I lie all the time, don't I?<end>"
KO: "또 시작이네. 또 입을 닫았어. 내가 거짓말하는 건 일상이잖아?<end>"

## 1350518
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…悪かったよ。<line-break>心配してくれてるのに<line-break>ウソついて…<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. You were worried about me, and I lied to you...<end>"
KO: "…미안했어. 걱정해 주고 있는데 거짓말해서….<end>"

## 1350519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ちっ<end>"
EN: "Tch.<end>"
KO: "…칫<end>"

## 1350520
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見張りに来たの？<line-break>俺が消えないかって。<end>"
EN: "Come to keep watch over me? Make sure I don't disappear?<end>"
KO: "감시하러 왔어? 내가 사라질까 봐.<end>"

## 1350521
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3消えたら、もうわかんねえよ。<line-break>虚無の剣の力で<line-break>あんたも俺のこと、完全に忘れる。<end>消えねえよ。<line-break>それが俺の<line-break>あいつやゼネテスへの意地だからな。<end>消えることへの<line-break>恐怖も未練もないはずだったのに…<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3If I disappear, you won't even know it. The Sword of the Void will make you forget me completely too.<end>I'm not going to disappear. That's my way of defying that person and Xenetes.<end>I thought I had no fear of disappearing, nothing left to cling to...<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3사라지면 이제 알 수 없어. 허무의 검의 힘으로 당신도 나를 완전히 잊게 돼.<end>사라지지 않아. 그게 내가 그 녀석과 제네테스에게 보여 줄 오기니까.<end>사라지는 것에 대한 두려움도 미련도 없을 줄 알았는데…<end>"

## 1350522
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんでもねえよ。<end>"
EN: "It's nothing.<end>"
KO: "…아무것도 아니야.<end>"

## 1350523
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3こんなカワイイ横顔してたなんて<line-break>ほんの少し前までは<line-break>気づかなかったんだよな…<end>…ありがとよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>今日も俺みたいな奴のために<line-break>俺がひとりで苦しまないようにって。<end>ちっ、<line-break>なんだってこんなときに<line-break>こんな奴と出会っちまったんだ。<line-break>もう少し遅ければ<line-break>俺はいなかったし。<line-break>もう少し早ければ…<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Until just a little while ago, I never realized you had such a pretty profile...<end>Thanks, <value:$28>. Here you are again today, making sure someone like me doesn't have to suffer alone.<end>Damn it. Why did I have to meet someone like you at a time like this? A little later, and I'd have been gone. A little sooner, and...<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3이렇게 귀여운 옆얼굴이었다니 조금 전까지만 해도 몰랐군….<end>…고맙다, <value:$28>. 오늘도 나 같은 놈을 위해, 내가 혼자 괴로워하지 않도록 해 주는구나.<end>쳇, 왜 하필 이런 때 이런 녀석을 만나 버린 거야. 조금만 늦었으면 난 없었을 테고. 조금만 빨랐으면….<end>"

## 1350524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ、起きてたのかよ。<line-break>なんでもねえよ！<line-break>黙ってたから、わからなかったぜ。<end>"
EN: "Tch. You were awake? It was nothing! You weren't saying anything, so how was I supposed to know?<end>"
KO: "칫, 깨어 있었냐. 아무것도 아니야! 조용히 있길래 몰랐잖아.<end>"

## 1350525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3ヒマなの？<end>俺の顔とか好みなわけ？<end>趣味は徹夜とか？<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Nothing better to do?<end>What, is my face your type?<end>Is staying up all night a hobby of yours?<end>"

## 1350528
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さん！<end>"
EN: "Brother!<end>"
